# 交通工具

**vehicle**
英 /ˈviːəkl/ 美 /ˈviːəkl/
n. 车辆；交通工具
[义] A machine with an engine or motor used for carrying people or goods on land, water, or in the air. 靠引擎或马达在陆、水、空运送人或物的机器（motor vehicle 机动车，electric vehicle 电动车，commercial vehicle 商用车辆）。可数。vehicle 为各类具体交通工具的上位词，比 car 更正式、更宽。
[例] ① Every vehicle on the road must display a valid license plate. 路上每辆车都必须挂有效牌照。 ② The company is switching its fleet to electric vehicles. 公司正把车队换成电动车。
[源] 源自拉丁语 *vehiculum*（运输工具），*vehere*（运送）→ 承载移动之物。
[族] convey 运送, conveyor 传送带
[搭] motor vehicle 机动车, electric vehicle 电动车, commercial vehicle 商用车辆, military vehicle 军用车辆, register a vehicle 给车辆登记
[联] car 汽车, bus 公共汽车, train 火车, transport 运输, traffic 交通, driver 司机, engine 引擎, fleet 车队
[记] 可数：a vehicle。vehicle 亦可喻指传播媒介（a vehicle for change 变革的载体），属比喻义

**transport**
英 /ˈtrænspɔːt/ 美 /ˈtrænspɔːrt/
n. 运输；交通系统；运输工具
[义] A system or means of carrying people or goods from one place to another; the activity of moving them. 把人或货从一地运到另一地的系统、方式或活动（public transport 公共交通，means of transport 交通方式，air transport 空运）。不可数/可数。动词 transport /trænˈspɔːt/ 表「运输、运送」，属通用动作义。
[例] ① Most commuters rely on public transport to get to work. 大多数通勤者靠公共交通上班。 ② The storm disrupted road and rail transport across the region. 风暴扰乱了该地区公路与铁路运输。
[源] 源自拉丁语 *transportare*（运过），*trans-*（越过）+ *portare*（携带）→ 把人或物带过去。
[族] transportation 交通运输, portable 便携的, export 出口, import 进口
[搭] public transport 公共交通, means of transport 交通方式, air transport 空运, transport network 交通网络, free transport 免费交通
[联] transportation 交通运输, traffic 交通, vehicle 车辆, commute 通勤, passenger 乘客, freight 货运, transit 公交, logistics 物流
[记] 英式 transport 常指交通系统；美式 transportation 更常说「交通运输」这一整体。动词义不在本章主收

**transportation**
英 /ˌtrænspɔːˈteɪʃn/ 美 /ˌtrænspərˈteɪʃn/
n. 交通运输；运输系统
[义] The movement of people or goods from one place to another, and the means used to do it. 人员或货物的运送活动及所用方式（transportation system 交通系统，department of transportation 交通部，transportation hub 交通枢纽）。不可数/可数。美式日常比 transport 更常用；英式多指「运输行为」本身。
[例] ① The city invested billions in public transportation. 该市在公共交通上投入数十亿。 ② Transportation costs pushed up the price of imported goods. 运输成本推高了进口商品价格。
[搭] public transportation 公共交通, transportation system 交通系统, transportation hub 交通枢纽, transportation costs 运输成本, Department of Transportation 交通部
[联] transport 运输, traffic 交通, commute 通勤, infrastructure 基础设施, logistics 物流, vehicle 车辆, transit 公交
[记] 不可数/可数。美式 DOT = Department of Transportation。与 transport 近义，常可互换

**traffic**
英 /ˈtræfɪk/
n. 交通；车流；运输量
[义] The movement of vehicles, ships, or aircraft along routes; the vehicles on a road at a given time. 车辆、船舶或飞机在路线上的移动；某时某路上的车流（heavy traffic 交通拥堵, traffic jam 堵车, air traffic 空中交通）。不可数。traffic 亦可指非法交易（drug traffic），语境不同。
[例] ① We were stuck in traffic for over an hour. 我们在堵车中困了一个多小时。 ② Air traffic increased sharply after the holiday. 假期后空中交通量激增。
[源] 源自意大利语 *traffico*（贸易、往来），经法语进入英语，先指商业运输，后泛指交通流。
[搭] heavy traffic 交通拥堵, traffic jam 堵车, air traffic 空中交通, traffic light 红绿灯, rush-hour traffic 高峰车流
[联] road 道路, highway 高速公路, commute 通勤, congestion 拥堵, vehicle 车辆, accident 事故, pedestrian 行人
[记] 不可数。traffic jam 固定搭配。traffic vs. transport：前者强调「流动/车流」，后者强调「系统/方式」

**public transport**
英 /ˌpʌblɪk ˈtrænspɔːt/ 美 /ˌpʌblɪk ˈtrænspɔːrt/
n. 公共交通
[义] Buses, trains, subways, and other services available for anyone to use, usually run by the government or on contract. 供公众使用的巴士、火车、地铁等，通常由政府或承包方运营（reliable public transport 可靠的公共交通, use public transport 乘公共交通）。不可数。
[例] ① Living near a subway line makes public transport easy. 住在地铁线附近，乘公共交通很方便。 ② The mayor promised to improve public transport across the city. 市长承诺改善全市公共交通。
[搭] reliable public transport 可靠的公共交通, use public transport 乘公共交通, public transport network 公交网络, free public transport 免费公交
[联] bus 公共汽车, train 火车, subway 地铁, commute 通勤, ticket 车票, passenger 乘客, fare 票价
[记] 不可数。英式 public transport，美式亦常说 public transportation。与 private car 相对

**car**
英/美 /kɑː(r)/
n. 汽车；轿车
[义] A road vehicle with an engine and four wheels, designed to carry a small number of people. 有引擎、四轮、供少数人乘坐的公路车辆（drive a car 开车, park the car 停车, electric car 电动汽车）。可数。car 为日常最常用词；automobile 更正式。
[例] ① She left her car in the garage overnight. 她把车停在车库过夜。 ② More families now own two cars. 更多家庭现在拥有两辆车。
[搭] drive a car 开车, park the car 停车, electric car 电动汽车, car accident 车祸, get in/out of the car 上/下车
[联] vehicle 车辆, bus 公共汽车, driver 司机, traffic 交通, parking 停车, highway 高速公路, automobile 汽车
[记] 可数：a car。美式 by car，英式亦常用 by car。car vs. automobile：后者书面/正式

**bus**
英/美 /bʌs/
n. 公共汽车；巴士
[义] A large road vehicle that carries passengers along a fixed route for a fare. 沿固定线路、收费载客的大型公路车辆（catch the bus 赶公交, school bus 校车, bus stop 公交站）。可数。
[例] ① I missed the last bus and had to take a taxi. 我错过了末班公交，只好打车。 ② The bus was packed during the morning rush. 早高峰时公交车挤满人。
[搭] catch the bus 赶公交, school bus 校车, bus stop 公交站, bus route 公交线路, double-decker bus 双层巴士
[联] train 火车, subway 地铁, public transport 公共交通, passenger 乘客, fare 票价, commute 通勤, coach 长途大巴
[记] 可数：a bus。复数 buses 或 busses。by bus 表交通方式。coach 在英国常指城际长途大巴

**train**
英/美 /treɪn/
n. 火车；列车
[义] A connected line of railway carriages pulled by an engine, carrying passengers or freight. 由机车牵引、运送乘客或货物的一列铁路车厢（catch a train 赶火车, express train 快车, train station 火车站）。可数。
[例] ① The train to Edinburgh leaves from platform three. 去爱丁堡的火车从三号站台出发。 ② We took the overnight train to save on hotels. 我们乘夜车以省住宿费。
[搭] catch a train 赶火车, express train 快车, train station 火车站, bullet train 高铁, miss the train 误车
[联] railway 铁路, subway 地铁, platform 站台, passenger 乘客, commute 通勤, ticket 车票, freight 货运
[记] 可数：a train。train vs. subway：后者指城市地下铁路系统

**subway**
英 /ˈsʌbweɪ/ 美 /ˈsʌbweɪ/
n. 地铁；地下通道
[义] An underground railway system in a city (AmE); in BrE, a path under a road for pedestrians. 〔美式〕城市地下铁路；〔英式〕供行人穿越马路的地下通道（take the subway 乘地铁, subway station 地铁站）。可数。英式表「地铁」常用 underground 或 metro。
[例] ① The subway was crowded at rush hour. 高峰时段地铁很挤。 ② She takes the subway to work every day. 她每天乘地铁上班。
[搭] take the subway 乘地铁, subway station 地铁站, subway line 地铁线路, subway car 地铁车厢
[联] metro 地铁, train 火车, underground 地铁, platform 站台, commute 通勤, public transport 公共交通
[记] 可数。AmE subway = BrE underground/metro；BrE subway = 人行地下通道，勿混

**metro**
英 /ˈmetrəʊ/ 美 /ˈmetroʊ/
n. 地铁；大城市
[义] An underground railway system in a city, especially in European cities or in the name of a system. 城市地下铁路，尤指欧洲或系统名称中（Paris Metro 巴黎地铁, metro line 地铁线, take the metro 乘地铁）。可数。亦作形容词 metro area 大都会区。
[例] ① We bought tickets at the metro entrance. 我们在地铁入口买了票。 ② The metro runs until midnight on weekends. 周末地铁运营至午夜。
[搭] take the metro 乘地铁, metro line 地铁线, metro station 地铁站, metro card 地铁卡
[联] subway 地铁, underground 地铁, train 火车, platform 站台, commute 通勤, public transport 公共交通
[记] 可数。源自 metropolitan（大都市的）。许多城市地铁以 Metro 命名

**taxi**
英/美 /ˈtæksi/
n. 出租车
v. （飞机）滑行
[义] A car with a driver that you pay to take you somewhere, usually with a meter. 付费搭载乘客、通常按里程计费的汽车（call a taxi 叫出租车, taxi rank 出租车站, taxi driver 出租车司机）。可数。动词指飞机在地面的滑行。
[例] ① We hailed a taxi outside the station. 我们在站外拦了一辆出租车。 ② The taxi dropped us at the hotel entrance. 出租车把我们送到酒店门口。
[搭] call a taxi 叫出租车, taxi rank 出租车站, taxi driver 出租车司机, take a taxi 打车, taxi fare 出租车费
[联] cab 出租车, car 汽车, driver 司机, fare 票价, Uber 优步, commute 通勤, airport 机场
[记] 可数：a taxi。cab 为同义口语词，美式更常见。hail a taxi 拦车

**cab**
英/美 /kæb/
n. 出租车；驾驶室
[义] A taxi; the enclosed space where the driver sits in a truck, train, or crane. 出租车；卡车、火车或起重机的驾驶舱（yellow cab 黄出租车〔纽约〕, cab driver 出租车司机）。可数。
[例] ① He jumped into a cab and headed for the airport. 他跳上一辆出租车赶往机场。 ② The truck driver climbed into the cab and started the engine. 卡车司机爬进驾驶室启动了引擎。
[搭] yellow cab 黄出租车, cab driver 出租车司机, call a cab 叫车, cab fare 车费
[联] taxi 出租车, driver 司机, fare 票价, truck 卡车, commute 通勤
[记] 可数：a cab。美式 cab 几乎专指出租车；亦指火车头/卡车驾驶室

**bicycle**
英 /ˈbaɪsɪkl/ 美 /ˈbaɪsɪkl/
n. 自行车
v. 骑自行车
[义] A two-wheeled vehicle that you ride by pushing pedals with your feet. 靠脚踏板驱动的两轮车（ride a bicycle 骑自行车, bicycle lane 自行车道, electric bicycle 电动自行车）。可数。口语常缩为 bike。
[例] ① She cycles to work on her bicycle every morning. 她每天早上骑自行车上班。 ② The city added more bicycle lanes downtown. 市中心增设了更多自行车道。
[源] 源自 bi-（二）+ cycle（轮）→ 两轮车。19 世纪发明后入英。
[族] bike 自行车, cyclist 骑行者, tricycle 三轮车, motorcycle 摩托车
[搭] ride a bicycle 骑自行车, bicycle lane 自行车道, electric bicycle 电动自行车, bicycle helmet 自行车头盔
[联] bike 自行车, motorcycle 摩托车, cyclist 骑行者, pedal 踏板, commute 通勤, traffic 交通
[记] 可数：a bicycle。口语 bike 更常见。bicycle vs. motorcycle：后者有引擎

**bike**
英/美 /baɪk/
n. 自行车；摩托车
v. 骑车
[义] A bicycle; in some contexts, a motorcycle. 自行车；某些语境亦指摩托车（mountain bike 山地车, ride a bike 骑车, bike rack 自行车架）。可数。
[例] ① He locked his bike to the rail outside the café. 他把自行车锁在咖啡馆外的栏杆上。 ② They went for a bike ride along the river. 他们沿河骑车兜风。
[搭] mountain bike 山地车, ride a bike 骑车, bike rack 自行车架, bike lane 自行车道, electric bike 电动车
[联] bicycle 自行车, motorcycle 摩托车, cyclist 骑行者, pedal 踏板, commute 通勤
[记] 可数：a bike。口语极常用。语境中需区分 bicycle 与 motorcycle

**motorcycle**
英 /ˈməʊtəsaɪkl/ 美 /ˈmoʊtərsaɪkl/
n. 摩托车
[义] A two-wheeled road vehicle with an engine, ridden by one or two people. 有引擎的两轮公路车辆，可一至两人同乘（ride a motorcycle 骑摩托, motorcycle helmet 头盔, motorcycle license 摩托车驾照）。可数。口语常简称 motorbike（英式）或 bike（需语境）。
[例] ① He commutes by motorcycle when the weather is fine. 天气好时他骑摩托通勤。 ② The motorcycle sped past us on the highway. 摩托车在高速上从我们身边飞驰而过。
[搭] ride a motorcycle 骑摩托, motorcycle helmet 头盔, motorcycle license 摩托车驾照, motorcycle accident 摩托车事故
[联] bike 自行车, motorbike 摩托车, vehicle 车辆, helmet 头盔, traffic 交通, highway 高速公路
[记] 可数：a motorcycle。英式 motorbike 同义更口语

**truck**
英/美 /trʌk/
n. 卡车；货车
[义] A large road vehicle used for carrying heavy loads or goods. 运载重物或货物的大型公路车辆（delivery truck 送货车, pickup truck 皮卡, truck driver 卡车司机）。可数。英式 lorry 同义。
[例] ① A truck blocked the lane after the accident. 事故后一辆卡车堵住了车道。 ② The warehouse ships orders by truck overnight. 仓库夜间用卡车发货。
[搭] delivery truck 送货车, pickup truck 皮卡, truck driver 卡车司机, truck load 卡车荷载, food truck 餐车
[联] lorry 卡车, van 厢式货车, vehicle 车辆, freight 货运, highway 高速公路, driver 司机
[记] 可数：a truck。美式 truck，英式 lorry 更常说「重型货车」；pickup truck 美式常用

**lorry**
英 /ˈlɒri/ 美 /ˈlɔːri/
n. 卡车；货车
[义] A large motor vehicle for transporting goods by road. 公路运货的大型机动车（articulated lorry 铰接式卡车, lorry driver 卡车司机）。可数。主要为英式；美式用 truck。
[例] ① A lorry jackknifed on the motorway, closing two lanes. 一辆卡车在高速上折刀式失控，封闭两条车道。 ② Lorries queued at the border checkpoint for hours. 卡车在边境检查站排了数小时队。
[搭] articulated lorry 铰接式卡车, lorry driver 卡车司机, lorry load 卡车荷载
[联] truck 卡车, van 厢式货车, freight 货运, motorway 高速公路, driver 司机, warehouse 仓库
[记] 可数：a lorry。英式专用；美式对应 truck

**van**
英/美 /væn/
n. 厢式货车；面包车
[义] A medium-sized road vehicle with no side windows at the back, used for carrying goods or people. 后部无侧窗的中型公路车辆，运货或载人（delivery van 送货车, van pool 拼车 van, camper van 露营车）。可数。
[例] ① The removal van arrived at eight in the morning. 搬家货车早上八点到了。 ② He drives a van for a local courier company. 他给一家本地快递公司开厢式货车。
[搭] delivery van 送货车, van pool 拼车, camper van 露营车, police van 警用面包车, load a van 装车
[联] truck 卡车, lorry 卡车, vehicle 车辆, driver 司机, delivery 配送, cargo 货物
[记] 可数：a van。比 truck 小，常用于城市配送

**SUV**
英 /ˌes juː ˈviː/ 美 /ˌes juː ˈviː/
n. 运动型多用途车；越野车
[义] A large car with high ground clearance and often four-wheel drive, designed for both road and rough terrain. 底盘较高、常配四驱的大型汽车，兼顾公路与越野（family SUV 家庭 SUV, electric SUV 电动 SUV, compact SUV 紧凑型 SUV）。可数。全称 sport utility vehicle。
[例] ① They traded their sedan for a spacious SUV. 他们把轿车换了一辆宽敞的 SUV。 ② SUV sales have overtaken sedans in many markets. 许多市场上 SUV 销量已超过轿车。
[搭] family SUV 家庭 SUV, electric SUV 电动 SUV, compact SUV 紧凑型 SUV, SUV market SUV 市场
[联] car 汽车, jeep 吉普, vehicle 车辆, four-wheel drive 四驱, off-road 越野, sedan 轿车
[记] 可数：an SUV。缩写读字母 S-U-V。与 crossover 部分重叠，crossover 更偏公路

**ambulance**
英 /ˈæmbjələns/ 美 /ˈæmbjələns/
n. 救护车
[义] A vehicle equipped to take sick or injured people to hospital. 配备设备、运送伤病者前往医院的专用车辆（call an ambulance 叫救护车, ambulance crew 救护人员, ambulance siren 救护鸣笛）。可数。
[例] ① Paramedics arrived in an ambulance within minutes. 救护人员几分钟内乘救护车赶到。 ② The ambulance rushed through traffic with its siren on. 救护车鸣笛穿过车流。
[源] 源自法语 *hôpital ambulant*（移动医院），*ambulare*（行走）→ 可移动的医疗车。
[族] ambulatory 可步行的
[搭] call an ambulance 叫救护车, ambulance crew 救护人员, ambulance siren 救护鸣笛, ambulance service 救护服务
[联] hospital 医院, emergency 紧急, paramedic 护理人员, siren 警笛, accident 事故, stretcher 担架
[记] 可数：an ambulance。固定搭配 call an ambulance

**ship**
英/美 /ʃɪp/
n. 大船；舰
v. 运送；发货
[义] A large boat for carrying people or goods across seas or oceans. 跨海或远洋运送人员或货物的大型船只（cargo ship 货轮, cruise ship 邮轮, ship captain 船长）。可数。动词 ship 指发货、运送，属通用动作。
[例] ① The cargo ship docked at the port after three weeks at sea. 货轮在海上三周后靠港。 ② Thousands of passengers boarded the cruise ship at dawn. 数千乘客黎明时分登上邮轮。
[搭] cargo ship 货轮, cruise ship 邮轮, ship captain 船长, shipwreck 海难, board a ship 登船
[联] boat 小船, ferry 渡轮, port 港口, vessel 船只, sailor 水手, ocean 海洋
[记] 可数：a ship。ship vs. boat：前者更大，常指海船；后者可指小船

**boat**
英/美 /bəʊt/
n. 小船；艇
[义] A small vessel for travelling on water, moved by oars, sails, or a motor. 靠桨、帆或马达在水上行驶的小型船只（row a boat 划船, fishing boat 渔船, motorboat 摩托艇）。可数。
[例] ① They rented a boat and explored the lake. 他们租了条小船游湖。 ② The fishing boat returned to harbour at sunset. 渔船日落时分回港。
[搭] row a boat 划船, fishing boat 渔船, motorboat 摩托艇, lifeboat 救生艇, boat trip 乘船游览
[联] ship 大船, ferry 渡轮, yacht 游艇, sail 航行, harbour 港口, canoe 独木舟
[记] 可数：a boat。by boat 表交通方式。大船用 ship，小船用 boat

**ferry**
英/美 /ˈferi/
n. 渡轮；渡口
v. 摆渡；运送
[义] A boat or service that carries people, vehicles, or goods across a river or short stretch of sea. 载人、车或货过河或短距离海域的船只或服务（car ferry 汽车渡轮, ferry terminal 渡轮码头, take the ferry 乘渡轮）。可数/动词。
[例] ① We took the ferry to the island—it was cheaper than flying. 我们乘渡轮去岛上，比坐飞机便宜。 ② The ferry carries hundreds of commuters across the bay each morning. 渡轮每天早上运送数百通勤者过湾。
[搭] car ferry 汽车渡轮, ferry terminal 渡轮码头, take the ferry 乘渡轮, ferry crossing 渡口, ferry schedule 渡轮时刻表
[联] boat 小船, ship 大船, harbour 港口, terminal 航站楼, passenger 乘客, commute 通勤
[记] 可数/动词。ferry 强调「短程摆渡」；cruise 强调「长途游览」

**airplane**
英 /ˈeəpleɪn/ 美 /ˈerpleɪn/
n. 飞机
[义] A powered flying vehicle with fixed wings and engines. 有固定翼和引擎的动力飞行器（board an airplane 登机, airplane ticket 机票, airplane mode 飞行模式）。可数。英式亦拼 aeroplane；aircraft 更正式、可含直升机等。
[例] ① The airplane landed smoothly despite the crosswind. 尽管有侧风，飞机仍平稳着陆。 ② Passengers must switch phones to airplane mode before takeoff. 乘客须在起飞前将手机调至飞行模式。
[搭] board an airplane 登机, airplane ticket 机票, airplane mode 飞行模式, commercial airplane 民航客机
[联] aircraft 飞机, plane 飞机, airport 机场, pilot 飞行员, flight 航班, jet 喷气式飞机
[记] 可数：an airplane。美式 airplane，英式 aeroplane；口语 plane 最常用

**plane**
英/美 /pleɪn/
n. 飞机；平面
[义] An airplane; a flat surface in geometry. 飞机；几何中的平面（catch a plane 赶飞机, private plane 私人飞机, jet plane 喷气机）。可数。作「飞机」时为 airplane 的口语缩略。
[例] ① She was late and almost missed her plane. 她迟到了，差点误机。 ② The private plane touched down on a grass strip. 私人飞机在草跑道着陆。
[搭] catch a plane 赶飞机, private plane 私人飞机, jet plane 喷气机, board a plane 登机
[联] airplane 飞机, aircraft 飞机, airport 机场, flight 航班, pilot 飞行员, jet 喷气机
[记] 可数：a plane。plane 亦指「刨子、平面」，靠语境区分

**aircraft**
英 /ˈeəkrɑːft/ 美 /ˈerkræft/
n. 飞机；航空器
[义] Any machine that can fly, including airplanes, helicopters, and drones. 一切能飞行的机器，含固定翼飞机、直升机、无人机等（military aircraft 军用飞机, aircraft carrier 航母, commercial aircraft 民航机）。单复数同形。
[例] ① All aircraft were grounded during the storm. 风暴期间所有航空器停飞。 ② The museum displays historic aircraft from both world wars. 博物馆展出两次世界大战的历史飞机。
[搭] military aircraft 军用飞机, aircraft carrier 航母, commercial aircraft 民航机, aircraft engine 航空发动机
[联] airplane 飞机, helicopter 直升机, jet 喷气机, pilot 飞行员, airport 机场, flight 航班
[记] 单复数同形：one aircraft, two aircraft。比 plane 正式，新闻与航空业常用

**helicopter**
英 /ˈhelɪkɒptə(r)/ 美 /ˈhelɪkɑːptər/
n. 直升机
[义] An aircraft with large blades on top that spin to lift it vertically off the ground. 靠顶部旋翼旋转垂直起降的航空器（rescue helicopter 救援直升机, helicopter pad 停机坪, helicopter tour 直升机观光）。可数。口语常简称 chopper 或 heli。
[例] ① A rescue helicopter airlifted the hikers from the ridge. 救援直升机把登山者从山脊吊运出来。 ② Helicopters circled overhead during the protest. 抗议期间直升机在头顶盘旋。
[源] 源自希腊语 *helix*（螺旋）+ *pteron*（翼）→ 螺旋翼。
[族] helicopt 相关技术词
[搭] rescue helicopter 救援直升机, helicopter pad 停机坪, helicopter tour 直升机观光, helicopter pilot 直升机驾驶员
[联] aircraft 航空器, airplane 飞机, pilot 飞行员, rotor 旋翼, airlift 空运, emergency 紧急
[记] 可数：a helicopter。口语 chopper 非正式

**road**
英/美 /rəʊd/
n. 路；道路；公路
[义] A hard surface built for vehicles, bicycles, or pedestrians to travel on. 供车辆、自行车或行人通行的硬质路面（main road 主干道, road trip 公路旅行, road accident 交通事故）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① The road was icy and drivers were told to slow down. 路面结冰，司机被告知减速。 ② They planned a road trip along the coast. 他们计划沿海岸公路旅行。
[搭] main road 主干道, road trip 公路旅行, road accident 交通事故, on the road 在路上, road sign 路标
[联] street 街道, highway 高速公路, traffic 交通, lane 车道, bridge 桥梁, vehicle 车辆
[记] 可数/不可数。road vs. street：road 泛指道路，street 多指城镇内街

**street**
英/美 /striːt/
n. 街道
[义] A public road in a city or town, usually with buildings along one or both sides. 城镇中通常两侧有建筑的公共道路（main street 主街, cross the street 过马路, street parking 路边停车）。可数。
[例] ① Children were playing in the street after school. 放学后孩子们在街上玩。 ② The café is on a quiet side street. 咖啡馆在一条安静的小巷里。
[搭] main street 主街, cross the street 过马路, street parking 路边停车, one-way street 单行道, street corner 街角
[联] road 道路, avenue 大道, lane 小巷, traffic 交通, pedestrian 行人, sidewalk 人行道
[记] 可数：a street。in the street（英式）/ on the street（美式）表「在街上」

**highway**
英 /ˈhaɪweɪ/ 美 /ˈhaɪweɪ/
n. 公路；干线道路
[义] A main public road, especially one connecting cities or regions. 连接城市或地区的主干公路（interstate highway 州际公路, highway patrol 公路巡警, highway exit 高速出口）。可数。美式常指高速或州际公路；英式 motorway 更专指高速。
[例] ① We drove for hours on the highway without stopping. 我们在公路上开了数小时未停。 ② Construction closed two lanes of the highway. 施工封闭了公路两条车道。
[搭] interstate highway 州际公路, highway patrol 公路巡警, highway exit 高速出口, highway traffic 公路车流
[联] freeway 高速公路, motorway 高速公路, road 道路, traffic 交通, lane 车道, toll 过路费
[记] 可数：a highway。highway vs. freeway/motorway：后两者多指全封闭高速

**freeway**
英 /ˈfriːweɪ/ 美 /ˈfriːweɪ/
n. 高速公路
[义] A wide divided road with no traffic lights and limited access, designed for fast travel between cities. 多车道、无红绿灯、出入口受限的快速公路，连接城市间（AmE）。可数。英式对应 motorway；free 指「免费」，相对 toll road 收费路。
[例] ① Rush-hour traffic on the freeway barely moved. 高峰时段高速上几乎动弹不得。 ② Take the freeway north and exit at mile 42. 走高速向北，在 42 英里处下匝道。
[搭] freeway exit 高速出口, freeway traffic 高速车流, merge onto the freeway 并入高速, freeway ramp 高速匝道
[联] motorway 高速公路, highway 公路, traffic 交通, lane 车道, commute 通勤, toll road 收费路
[记] 可数：a freeway。主要为美式。英式 motorway；部分 freeway 亦收费，free 为历史名

**motorway**
英 /ˈməʊtəweɪ/ 美 /ˈmoʊtərweɪ/
n. 高速公路
[义] A wide road with multiple lanes and limited access, designed for fast long-distance travel (BrE). 多车道、出入口受限、供长途快速行驶的宽路（英式）（motorway service station 高速服务区, join the motorway 上高速, motorway junction 高速立交）。可数。
[例] ① The accident caused miles of tailbacks on the motorway. 事故导致高速上排起数英里长龙。 ② We joined the motorway at junction twelve. 我们在十二号立交上高速。
[搭] motorway service station 高速服务区, join the motorway 上高速, motorway junction 高速立交, motorway lane 高速车道
[联] freeway 高速公路, highway 公路, lorry 卡车, traffic 交通, commute 通勤, toll 过路费
[记] 可数：a motorway。英式专用；美式 freeway 或 interstate

**railway**
英 /ˈreɪlweɪ/ 美 /ˈreɪlweɪ/
n. 铁路；铁道系统
[义] A system of tracks and trains for carrying passengers and freight; the organization that runs it. 供列车运行、运送乘客与货物的轨道系统及其运营机构（railway station 火车站, railway line 铁路线, underground railway 地下铁路）。可数/不可数。美式亦说 railroad。
[例] ① The railway connects the capital to the port city. 铁路连接首都与港口城市。 ② Railway workers voted to strike over pay. 铁路工人就薪酬问题投票罢工。
[搭] railway station 火车站, railway line 铁路线, underground railway 地下铁路, railway network 铁路网
[联] train 火车, rail 铁轨, platform 站台, station 车站, commute 通勤, freight 货运
[记] 可数/不可数。英式 railway，美式 railroad 更常见

**station**
英/美 /ˈsteɪʃn/
n. 车站；站
[义] A place where trains or buses regularly stop for passengers to get on or off; a building for a particular service. 火车或巴士定期停靠、乘客上下车的场所（train station 火车站, bus station 公交站, gas station 加油站）。可数。
[例] ① Meet me at the station at six. 六点在车站见我。 ② The bus station was crowded with holiday travellers. 公交站挤满假日旅客。
[源] 源自拉丁语 *statio*（站立之处），*stare*（站立）→ 列车停靠之处。
[族] stationary 静止的, stat 站立（词根）
[搭] train station 火车站, bus station 公交站, subway station 地铁站, police station 警察局, gas station 加油站
[联] platform 站台, train 火车, bus 公共汽车, ticket 车票, commute 通勤, terminal 航站楼
[记] 可数：a station。gas station 美式，英式 petrol station

**platform**
英 /ˈplætfɔːm/ 美 /ˈplætfɔːrm/
n. 站台；平台
[义] The raised area beside railway tracks where passengers wait and board trains; a raised flat surface in general. 铁路轨道旁供乘客候车、登车的站台；泛指平台（platform three 三号站台, wait on the platform 在站台等候）。可数。
[例] ① The train to Bristol is departing from platform two. 开往布里斯托的列车从二号站台出发。 ② Passengers stood on the platform as the express rolled in. 快车进站时乘客站在站台上。
[搭] platform three 三号站台, wait on the platform 在站台等候, railway platform 铁路站台, platform edge 站台边缘
[联] station 车站, train 火车, subway 地铁, ticket 车票, commute 通勤, announcement 广播
[记] 可数：a platform。mind the gap（英式）站台与车间隙警示

**bridge**
英/美 /brɪdʒ/
n. 桥；桥梁
[义] A structure built to carry a road or railway across a river, valley, or other obstacle. 跨越河流、峡谷或其他障碍、承载公路或铁路的结构（road bridge 公路桥, railway bridge 铁路桥, cross the bridge 过桥）。可数。
[例] ① The old stone bridge still carries traffic after two centuries. 这座古老石桥两百年后仍通车。 ② Engineers inspected the bridge for cracks after the earthquake. 地震后工程师检查桥梁裂缝。
[搭] road bridge 公路桥, railway bridge 铁路桥, cross the bridge 过桥, bridge toll 过桥费, suspension bridge 悬索桥
[联] tunnel 隧道, road 道路, river 河流, highway 公路, traffic 交通, construction 建设
[记] 可数：a bridge。bridge vs. tunnel：前者架空，后者地下/水下

**tunnel**
英/美 /ˈtʌnl/
n. 隧道
[义] An underground or underwater passage built through a hill or beneath a road, river, or sea. 穿山或建于路、河、海下的通道（railway tunnel 铁路隧道, tunnel entrance 隧道入口, go through a tunnel 过隧道）。可数。
[例] ① The train disappeared into a long tunnel under the mountains. 列车消失在穿山长隧道里。 ② Traffic queued at the tunnel entrance during rush hour. 高峰时段车辆在隧道口排队。
[搭] railway tunnel 铁路隧道, tunnel entrance 隧道入口, road tunnel 公路隧道, tunnel vision 隧道视野〔喻〕
[联] bridge 桥梁, subway 地铁, railway 铁路, highway 公路, construction 建设, commute 通勤
[记] 可数：a tunnel。tunnel vision 喻「视野狭隘」

**parking**
英 /ˈpɑːkɪŋ/ 美 /ˈpɑːrkɪŋ/
n. 停车；停车位
[义] The act of leaving a vehicle in a place for a period; spaces where this is allowed. 将车辆停放一段时间的行为；允许停放的区域（parking lot 停车场, parking space 停车位, no parking 禁止停车）。不可数。
[例] ① Parking downtown is expensive and hard to find. 市中心停车贵且难找。 ② The hotel offers free parking for guests. 酒店为客人提供免费停车。
[搭] parking lot 停车场, parking space 停车位, no parking 禁止停车, parking garage 停车库, parking meter 停车计时器
[联] car 汽车, garage 车库, vehicle 车辆, traffic 交通, ticket 罚单, commute 通勤
[记] 不可数。parking lot（美式）/ car park（英式）。parking ticket 违章停车罚单

**engine**
英 /ˈendʒɪn/ 美 /ˈendʒɪn/
n. 引擎；发动机
[义] The machine that makes a vehicle move by burning fuel or using electricity. 靠燃烧燃料或电力驱动车辆移动的机器（car engine 汽车引擎, engine failure 引擎故障, search engine 搜索引擎〔另一义〕）。可数。
[例] ① The engine coughed and died at the traffic light. 引擎在红绿灯处咳喘着熄火了。 ② Electric cars have fewer moving parts in the engine. 电动汽车引擎活动部件更少。
[源] 源自拉丁语 *ingenium*（装置、天赋）→ 产生动力的装置。
[族] engineer 工程师, engineering 工程
[搭] car engine 汽车引擎, engine failure 引擎故障, start the engine 启动引擎, engine room 机舱
[联] motor 马达, vehicle 车辆, fuel 燃料, mechanic 机修工, horsepower 马力, brake 刹车
[记] 可数：an engine。search engine 为另一常用义。engine vs. motor：后者可指更小型的电动机

**wheel**
英/美 /wiːl/
n. 轮子；方向盘
[义] A circular object that turns around a central point and allows a vehicle to move; the steering wheel in a car. 绕中心旋转、使车辆移动的圆形部件；汽车方向盘（steering wheel 方向盘, spare wheel 备胎, behind the wheel 在驾驶）。可数。
[例] ① One of the wheels came loose on the highway. 高速上有一个轮子松了。 ② She got behind the wheel and drove us home. 她坐进驾驶位把我们开回家。
[搭] steering wheel 方向盘, spare wheel 备胎, behind the wheel 在驾驶, wheel alignment 四轮定位
[联] tire 轮胎, car 汽车, brake 刹车, engine 引擎, axle 车轴, vehicle 车辆
[记] 可数：a wheel。behind the wheel = 开车。 reinvent the wheel 喻「重复造轮子」

**seat belt**
英 /ˈsiːt belt/ 美 /ˈsiːt belt/
n. 安全带
[义] A strap in a vehicle that you fasten across your body for safety in an accident. 车祸时系于身体、起保护作用的车辆安全带（fasten your seat belt 系安全带, seat belt law 安全带法规）。可数。
[例] ① Please fasten your seat belt before we depart. 开车前请系好安全带。 ② Police stopped drivers who were not wearing seat belts. 警察拦下未系安全带的司机。
[搭] fasten your seat belt 系安全带, seat belt law 安全带法规, rear seat belt 后排安全带, unbuckle a seat belt 解开安全带
[联] car 汽车, safety 安全, accident 事故, airbag 安全气囊, driver 司机, passenger 乘客
[记] 可数：a seat belt。亦作 seatbelt 一词拼写。 buckle up 口语「系好安全带」

**driver**
英/美 /ˈdraɪvə(r)/
n. 司机；驾驶员
[义] A person who drives a vehicle. 驾驶车辆的人（taxi driver 出租车司机, bus driver 公交司机, drunk driver 醉驾司机）。可数。
[例] ① The bus driver announced the next stop. 公交司机报下一站。 ② Young drivers pay higher insurance premiums. 年轻司机保险费更高。
[搭] taxi driver 出租车司机, bus driver 公交司机, drunk driver 醉驾司机, driver license 驾照, back-seat driver 指手画脚的乘客
[联] passenger 乘客, car 汽车, vehicle 车辆, traffic 交通, commute 通勤, license 执照
[记] 可数：a driver。driver's license（美式）/ driving licence（英式）。动词 drive 属通用动作义

**passenger**
英/美 /ˈpæsɪndʒə(r)/
n. 乘客；旅客
[义] A person travelling in a vehicle, train, plane, or ship but not driving or crewing it. 乘坐车辆、火车、飞机或轮船但非驾驶或乘务的人员（passenger seat 乘客座, passenger train 客运列车, passenger traffic 客运量）。可数。
[例] ① The flight had over two hundred passengers on board. 航班上有两百多名乘客。 ② Passengers are asked to remain seated during turbulence. 遇气流时请乘客就座。
[源] 源自法语 *passager*（过路人），*passage*（通过）→ 途经的旅客。
[族] passage 通道, pass 通过
[搭] passenger seat 乘客座, passenger train 客运列车, passenger traffic 客运量, passenger ferry 客渡, fellow passenger 同车/同机乘客
[联] driver 司机, commute 通勤, ticket 车票, flight 航班, luggage 行李, crew 乘务组
[记] 可数：a passenger。passenger vs. commuter：后者强调日常固定往返

**commuter**
英/美 /kəˈmjuːtə(r)/
n. 通勤者
[义] A person who travels regularly between home and work, especially by train or bus. 定期往返家与工作地点的人，尤指乘火车或公交者（daily commuter 日常通勤者, commuter train 通勤列车, commuter belt 通勤带）。可数。
[例] ① Commuters packed the morning train into the city. 进城早班列车挤满通勤者。 ② Rising fares angered suburban commuters. 票价上涨激怒了郊区通勤者。
[源] 源自 commute（缩短〔刑罚〕→ 用月票「缩短」路程），commute 作「通勤」为美式 19 世纪用法。
[族] commute 通勤, commuting 通勤（名词）
[搭] daily commuter 日常通勤者, commuter train 通勤列车, commuter belt 通勤带, commuter traffic 通勤车流
[联] train 火车, bus 公共汽车, subway 地铁, rush hour 高峰, public transport 公共交通, suburb 郊区
[记] 可数：a commuter。commuter train 专指高峰加密班次

**coach**
英/美 /kəʊtʃ/
n. 长途大巴；教练
[义] A comfortable bus for long journeys between cities; in sports, a trainer. 城际间舒适的长途巴士（coach station 长途汽车站, coach trip 大巴旅行, coach driver 大巴司机）。可数。英式 coach 常指长途客运大巴；美式 long-distance bus 更常说。
[例] ① We booked seats on the overnight coach to Paris. 我们订了去巴黎夜行大巴的座位。 ② The coach pulled into the terminal twenty minutes late. 大巴晚二十分钟进站。
[搭] coach station 长途汽车站, coach trip 大巴旅行, coach driver 大巴司机, coach service 大巴服务, express coach 快线大巴
[联] bus 公共汽车, passenger 乘客, terminal 航站楼, commute 通勤, highway 公路, fare 票价
[记] 可数：a coach。coach 亦指「教练」，语境区分。BrE coach vs. AmE bus（长途）

**tram**
英/美 /træm/
n. 有轨电车
[义] A passenger vehicle that runs on rails along public streets, usually powered by electricity. 沿公共街道轨道运行、通常电力驱动的客运车辆（tram line 电车线路, take a tram 乘电车, tram stop 电车站）。可数。美式亦说 streetcar 或 trolley。
[例] ① The tram rattled through the old town centre. 电车哐当穿过老城中心。 ② Tourists often take the tram up the hill to the castle. 游客常乘电车上山去城堡。
[搭] tram line 电车线路, take a tram 乘电车, tram stop 电车站, cable tram 缆索电车
[联] train 火车, subway 地铁, bus 公共汽车, rail 铁轨, platform 站台, public transport 公共交通
[记] 可数：a tram。英式 tram，美式 streetcar/trolley 更常见

**scooter**
英/美 /ˈskuːtə(r)/
n. 小型摩托车；滑板车
[义] A light two-wheeled vehicle with a small engine, or a platform with wheels that you push with one foot. 小引擎两轮机动车，或单脚蹬地滑行的两轮板车（electric scooter 电动滑板车, motor scooter 小摩托, rent a scooter 租滑板车）。可数。
[例] ① She zipped through traffic on an electric scooter. 她骑电动滑板车在车流中穿梭。 ② The city banned scooters on sidewalks after several accidents. 几起事故后市里禁止在人行道骑滑板车。
[搭] electric scooter 电动滑板车, motor scooter 小摩托, rent a scooter 租滑板车, scooter rental 滑板车租赁
[联] motorcycle 摩托车, bike 自行车, moped 轻便摩托, commute 通勤, traffic 交通, helmet 头盔
[记] 可数：a scooter。scooter 可指两种不同工具，靠语境区分

**fire engine**
英/美 /ˈfaɪər endʒɪn/
n. 消防车
[义] A truck carrying firefighters and equipment to put out fires. 载消防员与灭火设备赶赴火场的专用卡车（fire engine siren 消防警笛, fire engine crew 消防队员）。可数。美式亦说 fire truck。
[例] ① A fire engine raced past with its siren wailing. 消防车鸣笛疾驰而过。 ② Children waved at the fire engine during the parade. 游行中孩子们朝消防车挥手。
[搭] fire engine siren 消防警笛, fire engine crew 消防队员, call a fire engine 叫消防车
[联] ambulance 救护车, truck 卡车, siren 警笛, emergency 紧急, engine 引擎, vehicle 车辆
[记] 可数：a fire engine。美式 fire truck 同义

**tractor**
英/美 /ˈtræktə(r)/
n. 拖拉机；牵引车
[义] A powerful motor vehicle with large rear wheels, used on farms to pull machinery or on airports to move aircraft. 大后轮、用于农场拖挂农具或机场拖飞机的重型机动车（farm tractor 农用拖拉机, tractor trailer 拖挂卡车, airport tractor 机场牵引车）。可数。
[例] ① The farmer started the tractor before sunrise. 农夫日出前启动了拖拉机。 ② An airport tractor towed the plane to the gate. 机场牵引车把飞机拖到登机口。
[源] 源自拉丁语 *trahere*（拉）+ -tor（执行者）→ 拖拉之物。
[族] traction 牵引, tractor 同族, attract 吸引
[搭] farm tractor 农用拖拉机, tractor trailer 拖挂卡车, airport tractor 机场牵引车, tractor pull 拖拉机拉力赛
[联] truck 卡车, trailer 拖车, farm 农场, vehicle 车辆, engine 引擎, harvest 收获
[记] 可数：a tractor。tractor trailer 美式指「卡车+拖挂」组合

**yacht**
英/美 /jɒt/
n. 游艇；帆船
[义] A large boat with sails or an engine, used for pleasure racing or cruising. 带帆或引擎、用于娱乐竞赛或巡航的大型船只（luxury yacht 豪华游艇, yacht club 游艇俱乐部, sailing yacht 帆船）。可数。
[例] ① They spent the weekend on a yacht in the harbour. 他们周末在港里的游艇上度过。 ② The yacht race drew spectators along the coast. 帆船赛吸引沿岸观众。
[搭] luxury yacht 豪华游艇, yacht club 游艇俱乐部, sailing yacht 帆船, motor yacht 机动游艇
[联] boat 小船, ship 大船, harbour 港口, sail 航行, cruise 巡航, marina 游艇码头
[记] 可数：a yacht。yacht 强调休闲/竞赛，非货运

**jet**
英/美 /dʒet/
n. 喷气式飞机；喷射流
[义] An aircraft powered by jet engines; a stream of liquid or gas forced out. 喷气引擎驱动的飞机；喷出的液流或气流（private jet 私人喷气机, jet lag 时差, jet engine 喷气发动机）。可数。
[例] ① The CEO flew to New York on a private jet. 首席执行官乘私人喷气机赴纽约。 ② Commercial jets lined up on the runway for takeoff. 民航客机在跑道上排队起飞。
[搭] private jet 私人喷气机, jet lag 时差, jet engine 喷气发动机, jet fuel 航空煤油, jet stream 急流
[联] airplane 飞机, aircraft 航空器, runway 跑道, pilot 飞行员, flight 航班, engine 引擎
[记] 可数：a jet。jet lag 固定搭配。jet vs. plane：前者强调喷气动力

**drone**
英/美 /drəʊn/
n. 无人机；嗡嗡声
[义] An unmanned aircraft controlled remotely or by autopilot. 遥控或自动驾驶的无人飞行器（delivery drone 送货无人机, drone footage 无人机航拍, fly a drone 飞无人机）。可数。
[例] ① The company tested delivery drones in suburban neighbourhoods. 公司在郊区测试送货无人机。 ② Drones must stay below the legal altitude limit. 无人机须保持在法定高度以下。
[搭] delivery drone 送货无人机, drone footage 无人机航拍, fly a drone 飞无人机, drone pilot 无人机操控员
[联] aircraft 航空器, helicopter 直升机, pilot 飞行员, camera 摄像头, delivery 配送, aviation 航空
[记] 可数：a drone。drone 亦指「雄蜂、单调嗡嗡声」，语境区分

**aeroplane**
英 /ˈeərəpleɪn/ 美 /ˈerəpleɪn/
n. 飞机
[义] A powered flying vehicle with fixed wings (BrE spelling). 有固定翼的动力飞行器（英式拼写）（board an aeroplane 登机, aeroplane ticket 机票）。可数。美式拼 airplane；口语 plane 最常用。
[例] ① The aeroplane climbed steadily after takeoff. 飞机起飞后稳步爬升。 ② Model aeroplanes hung from the museum ceiling. 博物馆天花板上挂着飞机模型。
[搭] board an aeroplane 登机, aeroplane ticket 机票, model aeroplane 飞机模型
[联] airplane 飞机, plane 飞机, aircraft 航空器, airport 机场, pilot 飞行员, flight 航班
[记] 可数：an aeroplane。BrE aeroplane，AmE airplane

**sedan**
英 /sɪˈdæn/ 美 /sɪˈdæn/
n. 轿车；三厢车
[义] A car with a separate boot and four doors, seating four or five people. 有独立行李箱、四门、坐四至五人的汽车（family sedan 家庭轿车, sedan car 三厢轿车, luxury sedan 豪华轿车）。可数。美式常用；英式多称 saloon (car)。
[例] ① They traded their SUV for a fuel-efficient sedan. 他们把 SUV 换成了省油轿车。 ② Sedan sales have fallen as buyers choose crossovers. 买家转向跨界车，轿车销量下滑。
[搭] family sedan 家庭轿车, luxury sedan 豪华轿车, sedan car 三厢轿车, mid-size sedan 中型轿车
[联] car 汽车, SUV 越野车, vehicle 车辆, trunk 行李箱, commute 通勤, parking 停车
[记] 可数：a sedan。BrE saloon car 同义

**limousine**
英 /ˈlɪməziːn/ 美 /ˌlɪməˈziːn/
n. 豪华轿车； limo
[义] A large, luxurious car, especially one driven by a chauffeur with a partition between driver and passengers. 大型豪华轿车，尤指司机与乘客隔开的专职驾驶车辆（stretch limousine 加长礼宾车, limousine service 礼宾车服务）。可数。口语常缩为 limo。
[例] ① A limousine waited outside the hotel for the delegates. 一辆礼宾车在大门外等候代表。 ② They arrived at the gala in a white stretch limousine. 他们乘白色加长礼宾车抵达晚宴。
[搭] stretch limousine 加长礼宾车, limousine service 礼宾车服务, hire a limousine 租礼宾车
[联] chauffeur 专职司机, taxi 出租车, car 汽车, luxury 豪华, wedding 婚礼, airport 机场
[记] 可数：a limousine。口语 limo /ˈliːmoʊ/ 更常见

**pilot**
英/美 /ˈpaɪlət/
n. 飞行员；领航员
v. 驾驶；试点
[义] A person who flies an aircraft; someone who guides a ship into or out of a harbour. 驾驶飞机的人；引导船舶进出港的领航员（airline pilot 民航飞行员, pilot license 飞行执照, co-pilot 副驾）。可数。
[例] ① The pilot announced that we would land in twenty minutes. 飞行员宣布二十分钟后着陆。 ② She trained for years to become a commercial airline pilot. 她受训多年成为民航飞行员。
[源] 源自希腊语 *pedon*（舵、桨）→ 掌舵者 → 领航/驾驶。
[族] autopilot 自动驾驶
[搭] airline pilot 民航飞行员, pilot license 飞行执照, co-pilot 副驾, pilot error 人为操作失误, harbor pilot 港务领航员
[联] airplane 飞机, aircraft 航空器, flight 航班, cockpit 驾驶舱, captain 机长, airport 机场
[记] 可数：a pilot。pilot 作动词表「试点、引航」

**captain**
英/美 /ˈkæptɪn/
n. 船长；机长；队长
[义] The person in command of a ship or aircraft; a leader of a team. 指挥船舶或飞机的人；队长（ship captain 船长, captain of the aircraft 机长, speak to the captain 联系机长）。可数。
[例] ① The captain ordered the crew to prepare for rough seas. 船长命令船员准备应对大浪。 ② Passengers applauded when the captain landed the plane safely. 机长安全着陆时乘客鼓掌。
[源] 源自拉丁语 *capitaneus*（首领），*caput*（头）→ 头领。
[族] chief 首领, capital 首都
[搭] ship captain 船长, captain of the aircraft 机长, speak to the captain 联系机长, captain's cabin 船长室
[联] pilot 飞行员, ship 大船, crew 乘务组, sailor 水手, flight 航班, harbour 港口
[记] 可数：a captain。机上 captain 与 pilot 常并用；captain 强调指挥权

**chauffeur**
英/美 /ˈʃəʊfə(r)/
n. 专职司机
v. 为……当司机
[义] A person employed to drive a private or hired car. 受雇驾驶私人或租用的汽车的人（personal chauffeur 私人司机, chauffeur-driven car 专车, chauffeur service 专车服务）。可数。
[例] ① A chauffeur opened the door as the executive arrived. 高管抵达时专职司机拉开了车门。 ② She chauffeured her parents to the airport at dawn. 她黎明时分开车送父母去机场。
[源] 源自法语 *chauffer*（加热）→ 早期蒸汽汽车「加热者」→ 司机。
[搭] personal chauffeur 私人司机, chauffeur-driven car 专车, chauffeur service 专车服务
[联] driver 司机, limousine 礼宾车, taxi 出租车, car 汽车, luxury 豪华
[记] 可数：a chauffeur。比 driver 更强调受雇与正式服务

**cyclist**
英/美 /ˈsaɪklɪst/
n. 骑自行车的人
[义] A person who rides a bicycle, especially on roads or in sport. 骑自行车的人，尤指公路或竞技骑行者（commuting cyclist 通勤骑行者, cyclist lane 自行车道, professional cyclist 职业车手）。可数。
[例] ① Cyclists must share the road with cars in many cities. 许多城市里骑行者与汽车共用道路。 ② The race attracted top cyclists from around the world. 赛事吸引全球顶尖车手。
[搭] commuting cyclist 通勤骑行者, cyclist lane 自行车道, professional cyclist 职业车手, cyclist helmet 骑行头盔
[联] bicycle 自行车, bike 自行车, traffic 交通, commute 通勤, pedestrian 行人, helmet 头盔
[记] 可数：a cyclist。cycle 作动词/名词表「循环、周期」为另一义

**motorist**
英/美 /ˈməʊtərɪst/
n. 驾车者；车主
[义] A person who drives a car, especially as a member of the general public on public roads. 驾驶汽车的人，尤指在公共道路上的一般驾驶者（motorist association 车主协会, stranded motorist 受困车主, motorist warning 驾车警示）。可数。
[例] ① Police warned motorists to slow down on icy roads. 警方提醒驾车者在结冰路面上减速。 ② The breakdown service assists stranded motorists 24 hours a day. 道路救援全天候帮助受困车主。
[搭] motorist association 车主协会, stranded motorist 受困车主, motorist warning 驾车警示, fellow motorist 其他驾车者
[联] driver 司机, car 汽车, traffic 交通, highway 公路, road 道路, vehicle 车辆
[记] 可数：a motorist。比 driver 更强调「私家车主/公路驾驶者」身份

**lane**
英/美 /leɪn/
n. 车道；小巷
[义] Part of a road marked for a single line of traffic; a narrow street. 道路上供单列车辆通行的划分区域；窄巷（fast lane 快车道, bus lane 公交专用道, change lanes 变道）。可数。
[例] ① The truck blocked the left lane for miles. 卡车堵住了左侧车道数英里。 ② Cyclists have a dedicated lane along the river. 河边设有自行车专用道。
[搭] fast lane 快车道, bus lane 公交专用道, change lanes 变道, lane closure 车道封闭, cycling lane 自行车道
[联] road 道路, highway 公路, traffic 交通, vehicle 车辆, intersection 交叉路口, commute 通勤
[记] 可数：a lane。life in the fast lane 喻「快节奏生活」

**intersection**
英 /ˌɪntəˈsekʃn/ 美 /ˌɪntərˈsekʃn/
n. 交叉路口
[义] A place where two or more roads meet or cross. 两条或多条道路交汇之处（busy intersection 繁忙路口, intersection accident 路口事故, four-way intersection 十字路口）。可数。英式亦说 crossroads 或 junction。
[例] ① Traffic lights were installed at the dangerous intersection. 危险路口安装了红绿灯。 ② Turn left at the next intersection. 在下一个交叉路口左转。
[搭] busy intersection 繁忙路口, intersection accident 路口事故, four-way intersection 十字路口, intersection camera 路口监控
[联] road 道路, traffic light 红绿灯, crosswalk 人行横道, turn 转弯, accident 事故, junction 交叉口
[记] 可数：an intersection。BrE junction 亦常用

**roundabout**
英 /ˈraʊndəbaʊt/ 美 /ˈraʊndəbaʊt/
n. 环岛；旋转木马
[义] A circular junction where traffic flows around a central island; a revolving ride at a fair. 车辆绕中心岛环行的圆形交叉口；旋转木马。可数。美式 traffic circle 或 rotary（新英格兰）。
[例] ① Take the second exit at the roundabout. 在环岛走第二个出口。 ② Roundabouts reduce serious accidents at busy junctions. 环岛减少了繁忙路口的严重事故。
[搭] take the exit at the roundabout 在环岛出口驶出, mini-roundabout 小型环岛, traffic roundabout 交通环岛
[联] intersection 交叉路口, junction 交叉口, traffic 交通, road 道路, turn 转弯, exit 出口
[记] 可数：a roundabout。英式极常见；美式部分地区用 traffic circle

**traffic light**
英 /ˈtræfɪk laɪt/ 美 /ˈtræfɪk laɪt/
n. 红绿灯；交通信号灯
[义] A set of coloured lights at a road junction that controls traffic by showing red, amber/yellow, and green. 路口用红、黄、绿控制车流的信号灯组（run a red traffic light 闯红灯, traffic light sequence 信号周期）。可数。常用复数 traffic lights。
[例] ① The traffic lights turned red just as we approached. 我们刚到时红绿灯变红了。 ② Cyclists must obey traffic lights like other vehicles. 骑行者须像其他车辆一样遵守红绿灯。
[搭] run a red traffic light 闯红灯, traffic light camera 红绿灯摄像头, at the traffic lights 在红绿灯处
[联] intersection 交叉路口, road 道路, traffic 交通, pedestrian 行人, crosswalk 人行横道, junction 交叉口
[记] 可数，常用复数 traffic lights。amber（英式）/ yellow（美式）指黄灯

**crosswalk**
英 /ˈkrɒswɔːk/ 美 /ˈkrɔːswɔːk/
n. 人行横道
[义] A marked path across a road where pedestrians have priority to cross (AmE). 路面上标出、行人优先穿越的过道（美式）（marked crosswalk 标线人行横道, crosswalk signal 人行横道信号）。可数。英式 zebra crossing 或 pedestrian crossing。
[例] ① Drivers must yield to pedestrians in the crosswalk. 司机须让人行横道上的行人先行。 ② She waited at the crosswalk for the walk signal. 她在人行横道等行人通行信号。
[搭] marked crosswalk 标线人行横道, crosswalk signal 人行横道信号, school crosswalk 学校人行横道
[联] pedestrian 行人, traffic light 红绿灯, street 街道, intersection 交叉路口, sidewalk 人行道, zebra crossing 斑马线
[记] 可数：a crosswalk。AmE crosswalk，BrE zebra crossing / pedestrian crossing

**toll road**
英 /ˈtəʊl rəʊd/ 美 /ˈtoʊl roʊd/
n. 收费公路
[义] A road that drivers must pay to use. 驾驶者须付费通行的公路（toll booth 收费站, toll fee 过路费, electronic toll 电子收费）。可数。
[例] ① We saved time by taking the toll road around the city. 我们走绕城收费公路省了时间。 ② The toll road charges cars by distance travelled. 该收费公路按行驶里程计费。
[搭] toll booth 收费站, toll fee 过路费, electronic toll 电子收费, toll road pass 收费公路通行证
[联] freeway 高速公路, motorway 高速公路, highway 公路, bridge 桥梁, tunnel 隧道, fare 票价
[记] 可数：a toll road。与 freeway（名义免费）相对；亦说 tollway, turnpike（美式）

**port**
英/美 /pɔːt/
n. 港口；港
[义] A town or area with facilities where ships load and unload; a place on a computer for connecting devices (另一义). 有船舶装卸设施的城镇或区域（sea port 海港, port city 港口城市, container port 集装箱港）。可数。
[例] ① Cargo ships queued outside the busy port. 货轮在繁忙港口外排队。 ② The ferry leaves from port at six in the morning. 渡轮早上六点从港出发。
[搭] sea port 海港, port city 港口城市, container port 集装箱港, port authority 港务局, port of entry 入境口岸
[联] harbour 港湾, ship 大船, dock 码头, cargo 货物, ferry 渡轮, vessel 船只
[记] 可数：a port。airport 航空港；port 海港。port 亦指计算机端口

**harbour**
英 /ˈhɑːbə(r)/ 美 /ˈhɑːrbər/
n. 港湾；海港
v. 庇护；怀有
[义] An area of water near the coast where ships can anchor safely; a place that gives protection. 近岸可供船舶安全停泊的水域（fishing harbour 渔港, harbour wall 防波堤, in harbour 在港内）。可数。美式拼 harbor。
[例] ① Yachts bobbed gently in the sheltered harbour. 游艇在避风港里轻轻摇晃。 ② The town grew around a natural harbour. 城镇围绕天然港湾发展起来。
[搭] fishing harbour 渔港, harbour wall 防波堤, in harbour 在港内, harbour master 港务长
[联] port 港口, dock 码头, boat 小船, ship 大船, anchor 锚, marina 游艇码头
[记] 可数：a harbour。harbour 强调停泊水域；port 强调装卸设施与行政港

**dock**
英/美 /dɒk/
n. 码头；船坞
v. 停靠；扣减
[义] A structure where ships are loaded, unloaded, or repaired; the area beside it. 船舶装卸或维修的构筑物及其旁区域（loading dock 装卸码头, dry dock 干船坞, dock worker 码头工人）。可数。
[例] ① The cruise ship docked at pier seven. 邮轮停靠七号码头。 ② Dock workers unloaded containers through the night. 码头工人彻夜卸集装箱。
[搭] loading dock 装卸码头, dry dock 干船坞, dock worker 码头工人, dock at 停靠于
[联] port 港口, harbour 港湾, ship 大船, cargo 货物, warehouse 仓库, ferry 渡轮
[记] 可数：a dock。dock 作动词亦指「扣工资、对接 spacecraft」

**runway**
英/美 /ˈrʌnweɪ/
n. 跑道
[义] A long strip of ground at an airport where aircraft take off and land. 机场供飞机起降的长条地面（runway length 跑道长度, clear the runway 清空跑道, fashion runway 时装 T 台〔另一义〕）。可数。
[例] ① The plane accelerated down the runway and lifted off. 飞机在跑道上加速起飞。 ② Fog closed the runway for two hours. 大雾导致跑道关闭两小时。
[搭] runway length 跑道长度, clear the runway 清空跑道, runway lights 跑道灯, secondary runway 副跑道
[联] airport 机场, airplane 飞机, pilot 飞行员, takeoff 起飞, landing 着陆, aircraft 航空器
[记] 可数：a runway。fashion runway 为另一常用义

**terminal**
英/美 /ˈtɜːmɪnl/
n. 航站楼；终点站
[义] A building at an airport where passengers arrive and depart; the end station of a transport line. 机场旅客进出的大楼；交通线路终点站（airport terminal 航站楼, bus terminal 公交总站, terminal building 候机/候车楼）。可数。
[例] ① We met at the international terminal before check-in. 我们在值机前在国际航站楼见面。 ② The bus terminal was crowded with holiday travellers. 公交总站挤满假日旅客。
[源] 源自拉丁语 *terminalis*（末端的），*terminus*（终点）→ 线路终点之建筑。
[族] terminate 终止, terminus 终点站
[搭] airport terminal 航站楼, bus terminal 公交总站, terminal building 候机楼, terminal gate 登机口
[联] airport 机场, station 车站, platform 站台, passenger 乘客, flight 航班, gate 登机口
[记] 可数：a terminal。terminal 亦指「终端、末期」，语境区分

**garage**
英 /ˈɡærɑːʒ/ 美 /ɡəˈrɑːʒ/
n. 车库；汽车修理厂
[义] A building for keeping a car in; a place where cars are repaired and fuel is sold. 停放汽车的建筑物；修车兼售油的场所（park in the garage 停进车库, garage door 车库门, garage sale 车库甩卖〔美式〕）。可数。
[例] ① He pulled the car into the garage before the storm. 暴风雨前他把车开进车库。 ② The garage quoted two hundred pounds for the repair. 修理厂报价两百镑维修费。
[搭] park in the garage 停进车库, garage door 车库门, garage sale 车库甩卖, attached garage 连体车库
[联] parking 停车, car 汽车, vehicle 车辆, mechanic 机修工, petrol station 加油站, driveway 车道

英 /ˈpetrəl steɪʃn/ 美 /ˈpetrəl steɪʃn/
n. 加油站
[义] A place where petrol is sold for cars (BrE). 出售汽油供汽车加注的场所（英式）（fill up at a petrol station 在加油站加满, petrol station forecourt 加油站前庭）。可数。美式 gas station 或 service station。
[例] ① We stopped at a petrol station to buy snacks and fuel. 我们在加油站停了一下买零食和油。 ② The nearest petrol station is five miles down the road. 最近的加油站在前方五英里。
[搭] fill up at a petrol station 在加油站加满, petrol station forecourt 加油站前庭, 24-hour petrol station 24 小时加油站
[联] fuel 燃料, petrol 汽油, gasoline 汽油, gas station 加油站, car 汽车, motorway 高速公路
[记] 可数：a petrol station。BrE petrol station，AmE gas station

**gas station**
英 /ˈɡæs steɪʃn/ 美 /ˈɡæs steɪʃn/
n. 加油站
[义] A place where gasoline is sold for vehicles (AmE). 出售汽油供车辆加注的场所（美式）（stop at a gas station 在加油站停, gas station attendant 加油员）。可数。英式 petrol station。
[例] ① Pull over at the next gas station—we're almost out of fuel. 在下个加油站停一下，油快没了。 ② Many gas stations now offer electric charging points. 许多加油站现提供充电点。
[搭] stop at a gas station 在加油站停, gas station attendant 加油员, gas station convenience store 加油站便利店
[联] petrol station 加油站, fuel 燃料, gasoline 汽油, car 汽车, highway 公路, electric charging 充电
[记] 可数：a gas station。AmE gas = gasoline，非「天然气」

**brake**
英/美 /breɪk/
n. 刹车；制动器
v. 刹车
[义] A device for slowing or stopping a vehicle; to operate it. 使车辆减速或停住的装置；踩刹（apply the brakes 踩刹车, brake pedal 刹车踏板, anti-lock brakes 防抱死刹车）。可数/动词。
[例] ① She hit the brakes when a dog ran into the road. 狗冲上马路时她猛踩刹车。 ② The truck's brakes failed on the steep hill. 卡车在陡坡上刹车失灵。
[搭] apply the brakes 踩刹车, brake pedal 刹车踏板, anti-lock brakes 防抱死刹车, handbrake 手刹, brake failure 刹车失灵
[联] wheel 轮子, engine 引擎, tire 轮胎, accident 事故, traffic 交通, vehicle 车辆
[记] brake / braked / braking。break（打破）同音异义，勿混

**tire**
英/美 /ˈtaɪə(r)/
n. 轮胎
v. 使疲劳
[义] The thick rubber ring around a wheel (AmE spelling tyre in BrE). 轮圈外的厚橡胶圈（美式拼 tire，英式 tyre）（flat tire 爆胎, spare tire 备胎, tire pressure 胎压）。可数。动词 tire 表「疲劳」，不同义。
[例] ① We pulled over after getting a flat tire on the highway. 高速上爆胎后我们靠边停车。 ② Check your tire pressure before a long trip. 长途前检查胎压。

[联] wheel 轮子, brake 刹车, car 汽车, mechanic 机修工, highway 公路, spare 备用的
[记] 可数：a tire。BrE tyre，AmE tire。flat tire / puncture 英式

**tyre**
英/美 /ˈtaɪə(r)/
n. 轮胎
[义] The thick rubber covering on a wheel (BrE spelling). 轮圈外的厚橡胶层（英式拼写）（flat tyre 爆胎, spare tyre 备胎, tyre tread 胎纹）。可数。美式 tire。
[例] ① The mechanic rotated all four tyres during the service. 保养时机修工四轮调位。 ② Worn tyres increase stopping distance in the rain. 胎纹磨损会增加雨天刹车距离。

[联] tire 轮胎, wheel 轮子, brake 刹车, car 汽车, puncture 扎胎, mechanic 机修工

英/美 /trʌŋk/
n. 行李箱；树干
[义] The enclosed space at the back of a car for luggage (AmE); a large strong box (另一义). 汽车后部放行李的封闭空间（美式）；大箱（另一义）。可数。英式 boot。
[例] ① We packed camping gear in the trunk before leaving. 出发前我们把露营装备装进后备箱。 ② He popped the trunk to load the groceries. 他打开后备箱装杂货。

[记] 可数：the trunk。AmE trunk，BrE boot。trunk 亦指「树干、象鼻」

**boot**
英/美 /buːt/
n. 后备箱；靴子
[义] The storage space at the back of a car (BrE); a strong shoe (另一义). 汽车后部储物空间（英式）；靴子。可数。
[例] ① She kept a spare tyre in the boot. 她在后备箱放了备胎。 ② They loaded suitcases into the boot and set off. 他们把行李箱装进后备箱出发了。
[搭] car boot 后备箱, boot space 后备箱空间, in the boot 在后备箱里
[联] trunk 后备箱, car 汽车, luggage 行李, tyre 轮胎, sedan 轿车, vehicle 车辆
[记] 可数：the boot。BrE boot，AmE trunk。boot 作「靴子」为高频另一义

**windshield**
英 /ˈwɪndʃiːld/ 美 /ˈwɪndʃiːld/
n. 挡风玻璃
[义] The large glass window at the front of a vehicle (AmE). 车辆前部的大块玻璃（美式）（windshield wiper 雨刮, crack in the windshield 挡风玻璃裂纹, windshield washer 玻璃清洗液）。可数。英式 windscreen。
[例] ① A stone chipped the windshield on the motorway. 高速上飞石把挡风玻璃崩裂了。 ② Frost covered the windshield on the cold morning. 寒晨霜覆盖了挡风玻璃。

[联] windscreen 挡风玻璃, car 汽车, wiper 雨刮, window 窗户, driver 司机, visibility 能见度

英 /ˈwɪndskriːn/ 美 /ˈwɪndskriːn/

[例] ① The windscreen misted up in the rain. 雨中挡风玻璃起雾了。 ② Police stopped the driver for a cracked windscreen. 警察因挡风玻璃破裂拦下司机。

[例] ① Witnesses wrote down the license plate before the car sped away. 目击者记下肇事车车牌后它疾驰而去。 ② Each state issues its own license plate design. 各州发行自己的车牌样式。

[联] car 汽车, vehicle 车辆, registration 登记, driver 司机, police 警察, traffic 交通

英/美 /fjuːl/
n. 燃料
v. 给……加燃料；加剧
[义] Material such as petrol, diesel, or electricity used to produce power in an engine. 汽油、柴油或电力等用于引擎产生动力的物质（run out of fuel 燃油耗尽, fuel efficiency 燃油效率, fossil fuel 化石燃料）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① The warning light came on—we were low on fuel. 警示灯亮了，油不多了。 ② Airlines face rising fuel costs each quarter. 航空公司每季度面临燃油成本上涨。

[联] petrol 汽油, gasoline 汽油, diesel 柴油, engine 引擎, gas station 加油站, electric 电动的
[记] 不可数/可数。fuel 作动词表「加剧（冲突、谣言）」

**petrol**
英 /ˈpetrəl/ 美 /pəˈtrəl/
n. 汽油
[义] Liquid fuel for car engines (BrE). 供汽车引擎使用的液体燃料（英式）（petrol pump 油泵, run out of petrol 汽油耗尽, unleaded petrol 无铅汽油）。不可数。美式 gasoline 或 gas。
[例] ① Petrol prices rose again this month. 本月汽油又涨价了。 ② The car runs on unleaded petrol only. 这车只加无铅汽油。

**gasoline**
英 /ˈɡæsəliːn/ 美 /ˈɡæsəliːn/
n. 汽油
[义] Liquid fuel for internal-combustion engines (AmE). 内燃机用的液体燃料（美式）（gasoline engine 汽油发动机, gasoline price 油价, leaded gasoline 含铅汽油）。不可数。英式 petrol；口语 gas。
[例] ① Gasoline prices vary widely from state to state. 各州油价差异很大。 ② The hybrid switches between gasoline and electric power. 混动车在汽油与电力间切换。
[搭] gasoline engine 汽油发动机, gasoline price 油价, leaded gasoline 含铅汽油, gallon of gasoline 一加仑汽油

**diesel**
英/美 /ˈdiːzl/
n. 柴油；柴油车
[义] Heavy fuel used in many trucks, buses, and some cars; a vehicle that uses it. 许多卡车、巴士及部分轿车使用的重质燃料；柴油车（diesel engine 柴油发动机, diesel fuel 柴油, diesel exhaust 柴油尾气）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① The lorry runs on diesel rather than petrol. 这辆货车烧柴油而非汽油。 ② New rules limit diesel emissions in city centres. 新规限制市中心柴油尾气排放。
[搭] diesel engine 柴油发动机, diesel fuel 柴油, diesel exhaust 柴油尾气, diesel car 柴油轿车

**fare**
英/美 /feə(r)/

[义] The money a passenger pays to travel by bus, train, taxi, or other transport. 乘客乘公交、火车、出租车等所付费用（bus fare 公交票价, taxi fare 出租车费, fare increase 涨价）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① The taxi fare from the airport was forty dollars. 从机场打车费四十美元。 ② Commuters protested against another fare hike. 通勤者抗议再次涨价。
[搭] bus fare 公交票价, taxi fare 出租车费, fare increase 涨价, pay the fare 付车费, reduced fare 优惠票价
[联] ticket 车票, passenger 乘客, taxi 出租车, bus 公共汽车, commute 通勤, public transport 公共交通

[例] ① Workers loaded cargo onto the plane through the night. 工人彻夜把货物装上飞机。 ② The cargo included medical supplies and food. 货物含医疗物资和食品。

[义] Goods transported in bulk by truck, train, ship, or aircraft; the system or cost of transporting them. 卡车、火车、船或飞机 bulk 运输的货物；货运系统或费用（freight train 货运列车, freight company 货运公司, air freight 空运）。不可数/动词。

n. 拖车；预告片
[义] A vehicle without its own engine, pulled by a truck or car; a short film advertising a movie (另一义). 无自身引擎、由卡车或汽车拖行的车辆；电影预告片。可数。
[例] ① The truck pulled a long trailer loaded with timber. 卡车拖着载满木材的长挂车。 ② They hitched the trailer to the car for the camping trip. 他们为露营把拖挂连到轿车后。
[搭] truck trailer 卡车拖挂, trailer hitch 拖挂连接器, trailer park 拖车营地, load a trailer 装拖挂
[联] truck 卡车, tractor 拖拉机, cargo 货物, lorry 卡车, caravan 拖挂房车, vehicle 车辆
[记] 可数：a trailer。movie trailer 为另一高频义

**fleet**
英/美 /fliːt/
n. 车队；船队
[义] A group of vehicles, ships, or aircraft owned or operated by the same organization. 同一机构拥有或运营的一组车辆、船舶或飞机（car fleet 汽车车队, fleet manager 车队经理, delivery fleet 配送车队）。可数。
[例] ① The company is replacing its entire fleet with electric vans. 公司正用电动厢货替换整个车队。 ② The airline expanded its fleet with ten new jets. 航司以十架新喷气机扩充机队。
[搭] car fleet 汽车车队, fleet manager 车队经理, delivery fleet 配送车队, fleet vehicle 车队车辆, electric fleet 电动车队
[联] vehicle 车辆, truck 卡车, ship 大船, aircraft 航空器, company 公司, delivery 配送
[记] 可数：a fleet。fleet 亦指「海军舰队」

**minivan**
英 /ˈmɪnivæn/ 美 /ˈmɪnivæn/
n. 小型面包车；七座车
[义] A small van designed to carry several passengers, typically with sliding side doors. 供多人乘坐、通常带侧滑门的小型厢式车（family minivan 家庭七座, minivan seat 中排座位, rent a minivan 租七座车）。可数。
[例] ① They bought a minivan when their second child was born. 第二个孩子出生后他们买了辆七座车。 ② The minivan fit three car seats across the back row. 七座车后排放得下三个儿童座椅。
[搭] family minivan 家庭七座, minivan seat 中排座位, rent a minivan 租七座车, minivan market 七座车市场
[联] van 厢式货车, SUV 越野车, car 汽车, family 家庭, passenger 乘客, vehicle 车辆
[记] 可数：a minivan。minivan vs. van：前者偏家用载人

**hatchback**
英 /ˈhætʃbæk/ 美 /ˈhætʃbæk/
n. 掀背车；两厢车
[义] A car with a sloping back door that opens upward, sharing one space for passengers and luggage. 后门上掀、乘客与行李同一空间的汽车（compact hatchback 紧凑型两厢, five-door hatchback 五门两厢）。可数。
[例] ① Her first car was a small red hatchback. 她的第一辆车是红色小两厢。 ② Hatchbacks are easier to park in tight city streets. 两厢车在狭窄街道更好停。
[搭] compact hatchback 紧凑型两厢, five-door hatchback 五门两厢, hatchback door 后掀门
[联] sedan 轿车, car 汽车, trunk 后备箱, boot 后备箱, parking 停车, vehicle 车辆
[记] 可数：a hatchback。与 sedan（三厢）相对

**jeep**
英/美 /dʒiːp/
n. 吉普车；越野车
[义] A small sturdy motor vehicle with four-wheel drive, designed for rough roads; the brand name is often used generically. 四轮驱动、适应坏路的小型坚固机动车；品牌名常泛指越野车（military jeep 军用吉普, jeep trail 越野小径, rent a jeep 租吉普）。可数。
[例] ① They rented a jeep to explore the mountain tracks. 他们租了辆吉普探山地小径。 ② The jeep climbed the muddy slope without slipping. 吉普爬上泥泞坡道未打滑。
[搭] military jeep 军用吉普, jeep trail 越野小径, rent a jeep 租吉普, four-wheel-drive jeep 四驱吉普
[联] SUV 越野车, four-wheel drive 四驱, off-road 越野, vehicle 车辆, truck 卡车, adventure 探险
[记] 可数：a jeep。Jeep 为注册商标，口语可泛指越野车

**pickup**
英/美 /ˈpɪkʌp/
n. 皮卡；轻型货车
[义] A small truck with an open back for carrying goods (pickup truck). 后部开放、用于运货的小型卡车（pickup truck 皮卡, pickup bed 货斗, drive a pickup 开皮卡）。可数。亦指「搭便车、好转」等，语境区分。
[例] ① He loaded lumber into the back of his pickup. 他把木材装进皮卡货斗。 ② Pickups are popular in rural areas for farm work. 皮卡在农村地区因农活而流行。
[搭] pickup truck 皮卡, pickup bed 货斗, drive a pickup 开皮卡, pickup driver 皮卡司机
[联] truck 卡车, lorry 卡车, van 厢式货车, cargo 货物, vehicle 车辆, farm 农场
[记] 可数：a pickup。全称 pickup truck；pick up 为动词短语「捡起、接人」

**caravan**
英 /ˈkærəvæn/ 美 /ˈkærəvæn/
n. 拖挂房车； caravan
[义] A vehicle without an engine that is pulled by a car and equipped for living in (BrE); a group travelling together (另一义). 由汽车拖行、可居住的无引擎车辆（英式）；结伴旅行队。可数。美式 travel trailer 或 RV。
[例] ① They toured the coast with a small caravan behind the car. 他们车后拖小房车沿海旅行。 ② The caravan site filled up quickly during the holiday. 假日里房车营地很快订满。
[搭] caravan site 房车营地, tow a caravan 拖房车, touring caravan 旅行拖挂, caravan park 房车公园
[联] trailer 拖车, RV 房车, camping 露营, tow 拖, vehicle 车辆, motorhome 自行式房车
[记] 可数：a caravan。BrE caravan；AmE travel trailer / RV。caravan 亦指「商队」

**RV**
英 /ˌɑː ˈviː/ 美 /ˌɑːr ˈviː/
n. 房车； recreational vehicle
[义] A motor vehicle equipped for living in, with beds, kitchen, and bathroom (AmE). 配有床、厨房和卫生间的可居住机动车（美式）（RV park 房车营地, RV trip 房车旅行, rent an RV 租房车）。可数。全称 recreational vehicle。
[例] ① They crossed the country in an RV over six weeks. 他们开房车六周横穿全国。 ② RV parks along the highway offer hookups for water and power. 沿途房车营地提供水电接口。
[搭] RV park 房车营地, RV trip 房车旅行, rent an RV 租房车, RV rental 房车租赁
[联] caravan 拖挂房车, motorhome 自行式房车, trailer 拖车, camping 露营, highway 公路, vehicle 车辆
[记] 可数：an RV。读字母 R-V。BrE 更常说 motorhome 或 caravan

**saloon**
英 /səˈluːn/ 美 /səˈluːn/
n. 三厢轿车；酒吧
[义] A car with a separate enclosed boot and a roof that does not slope down at the back (BrE). 有独立封闭行李箱、后部车顶不下斜的轿车（英式）（family saloon 家庭三厢, saloon car 三厢轿车）。可数。亦指「酒吧、大厅」，语境区分。
[例] ① He drives a reliable four-door saloon to work. 他开可靠四门三厢上班。 ② Saloon cars dominate the company fleet. 三厢车占公司车队主体。
[搭] family saloon 家庭三厢, saloon car 三厢轿车, four-door saloon 四门三厢
[联] sedan 轿车, car 汽车, boot 后备箱, hatchback 掀背车, vehicle 车辆
[记] 可数：a saloon。BrE saloon car = AmE sedan。saloon 亦指酒吧（西部片）

**double-decker**
英 /ˌdʌbl ˈdekə(r)/
n. 双层巴士；双层结构
[义] A bus with two floors for passengers. 供乘客使用的双层公共汽车（double-decker bus 双层巴士, open-top double-decker 敞篷双层, tour on a double-decker 乘双层观光）。可数。
[例] ① Tourists photographed Big Ben from an open-top double-decker. 游客从敞篷双层巴士上拍大本钟。 ② Double-deckers can carry more passengers on busy routes. 繁忙线路双层车可载更多客。
[搭] double-decker bus 双层巴士, open-top double-decker 敞篷双层, double-decker tour 双层观光
[联] bus 公共汽车, London 伦敦, passenger 乘客, public transport 公共交通, route 线路, tour 观光

**minibus**
英/美 /ˈmɪnibʌs/
n. 小巴；中型客车
[义] A small bus carrying about eight to twenty-five passengers. 载客约八至二十五人的小型巴士（school minibus 校车小巴, minibus service 小巴服务, hire a minibus 租小巴）。可数。
[例] ① A minibus picked up guests from the hotel. 小巴从酒店接客人。 ② The club chartered a minibus for the away match. 俱乐部为小巴包车赴客场。
[搭] school minibus 校车小巴, minibus service 小巴服务, hire a minibus 租小巴, minibus driver 小巴司机
[联] bus 公共汽车, van 厢式货车, passenger 乘客, shuttle 班车, coach 长途大巴, vehicle 车辆

**moped**
英/美 /ˈməʊped/
n. 轻便摩托车；助力车
[义] A small motorcycle with a light engine, often with pedals. 小引擎摩托车，常带脚踏（ride a moped 骑轻便摩托, moped license 轻便摩托驾照, electric moped 电动助力车）。可数。
[例] ① She commutes on a moped through narrow alleys. 她骑轻便摩托穿窄巷通勤。 ② Mopeds are popular among students in the city centre. 市中心学生爱用轻便摩托。
[搭] ride a moped 骑轻便摩托, moped license 轻便摩托驾照, electric moped 电动助力车
[联] motorcycle 摩托车, scooter 滑板车, bike 自行车, helmet 头盔, commute 通勤, traffic 交通
[记] 可数：a moped。mo + pedal → 原指可脚踏的摩托

**streetcar**
英 /ˈstriːtkɑː(r)/ 美 /ˈstriːtkɑːr/
n. 有轨电车
[义] A passenger vehicle running on rails along city streets (AmE). 沿城市街道轨道运行的客运车辆（美式）（streetcar line 电车线, streetcar stop 电车站, ride the streetcar 乘电车）。可数。英式 tram；新奥尔良等地保留此称。
[例] ① We rode the streetcar down to the waterfront. 我们乘有轨电车到滨水区。 ② The old streetcar clanged through the historic district. 老电车叮当穿过历史街区。
[搭] streetcar line 电车线, streetcar stop 电车站, ride the streetcar 乘电车, historic streetcar 历史电车
[联] tram 有轨电车, trolley 电车, train 火车, public transport 公共交通, platform 站台

**tow truck**
英/美 /ˈtəʊ trʌk/
n. 拖车；清障车
[义] A truck used to pull broken-down or illegally parked vehicles away. 拖走故障或违章停放车辆的专用卡车（call a tow truck 叫拖车, tow truck driver 拖车司机, tow truck service 道路救援）。可数。英式 breakdown truck / recovery vehicle。
[例] ① A tow truck arrived within thirty minutes of the call. 打电话后三十分钟内拖车赶到。 ② Police ordered a tow truck to remove the abandoned car. 警方叫清障车拖走废弃车辆。

[联] truck 卡车, breakdown 故障, mechanic 机修工, garage 修理厂, vehicle 车辆, accident 事故
[记] 可数：a tow truck。BrE breakdown truck / recovery lorry

n. 班车；穿梭巴士
[义] A bus, train, or other vehicle that travels regularly between two places. 在两地点间定期往返的巴士、火车或其他车辆（airport shuttle 机场班车, shuttle bus 穿梭巴士, free shuttle 免费班车）。可数。
[例] ① The hotel runs a free shuttle to the airport every hour. 酒店每小时开免费机场班车。 ② We took the shuttle from the parking lot to the terminal. 我们乘班车从停车场到航站楼。

[联] bus 公共汽车, airport 机场, terminal 航站楼, passenger 乘客, commute 通勤, train 火车

**cruise ship**
英/美 /ˈkruːz ʃɪp/
n. 邮轮；游轮
[义] A large passenger ship used for pleasure voyages, with entertainment and meals on board. 供休闲航行、船上有娱乐与餐饮的大型客轮（board a cruise ship 登邮轮, cruise ship port 邮轮港口, cruise ship cabin 客舱）。可数。
[例] ① Thousands of passengers disembarked from the cruise ship at dawn. 数千乘客黎明时分下邮轮。 ② The cruise ship sailed through the Caribbean for a week. 邮轮在加勒比海航行一周。
[搭] board a cruise ship 登邮轮, cruise ship port 邮轮港口, cruise ship cabin 客舱, luxury cruise ship 豪华邮轮
[联] ship 大船, ferry 渡轮, yacht 游艇, passenger 乘客, harbour 港湾, port 港口
[记] 可数：a cruise ship。cruise 作动词表「巡航、缓慢移动」

**canoe**
英/美 /kəˈnuː/
n. 独木舟；划艇
[义] A light narrow boat pointed at both ends, moved by paddling. 两端尖、靠桨划行的轻窄小船（paddle a canoe 划独木舟, canoe trip 泛舟之旅, canoe rental 租 canoe）。可数。
[例] ① They paddled the canoe quietly along the riverbank. 他们沿河岸静悄悄地划独木舟。 ② Canoe rentals are available at the lake in summer. 夏季湖边可租独木舟。
[搭] paddle a canoe 划独木舟, canoe trip 泛舟之旅, canoe rental 租独木舟, canoe paddle 船桨

[记] 可数：a canoe。canoe vs. kayak：前者开放式、单刃桨；后者常封闭式、双刃桨

英 /ˌsʌbməˈriːn/ 美 /ˈsʌbməriːn/

[义] A warship or research vessel that can travel underwater. 可在水下航行的军舰或科研船（nuclear submarine 核潜艇, submarine crew 艇员, submarine base 潜艇基地）。可数。口语 sub。
[例] ① The submarine surfaced near the coast at dawn. 潜艇黎明在近岸上浮。 ② Tourist submarines offer short dives for visitors. 观光潜艇为游客提供短潜。
[搭] nuclear submarine 核潜艇, submarine crew 艇员, submarine base 潜艇基地, research submarine 科研潜艇
[联] ship 大船, vessel 船只, navy 海军, underwater 水下的, torpedo 鱼雷, captain 船长
[记] 可数：a submarine。sub-（下）+ marine（海的）

**lifeboat**
英/美 /ˈlaɪfbəʊt/
n. 救生艇
[义] A small boat carried on a ship or kept at a coast for saving people in danger at sea. 船上携带或海岸备置、用于海上救人的小艇（launch a lifeboat 放下救生艇, lifeboat crew 救生艇队员, lifeboat station 救生站）。可数。
[例] ① Crew members lowered the lifeboats as the ship listed. 船倾斜时船员放下救生艇。 ② The lifeboat rescued fishermen caught in the storm. 救生艇救起风暴中的渔民。
[搭] launch a lifeboat 放下救生艇, lifeboat crew 救生艇队员, lifeboat station 救生站, inflatable lifeboat 充气救生筏
[联] boat 小船, ship 大船, rescue 救援, emergency 紧急, harbour 港湾, crew 船员
[记] 可数：a lifeboat。life + boat → 救命之舟

**marina**
英/美 /məˈriːnə/
n. 游艇码头； marina
[义] A specially designed harbour for small boats and yachts, with moorings and services. 为小艇与游艇设计、提供泊位与服务的港湾（yacht marina 游艇码头, marina berth 泊位, stay at a marina 停靠 marina）。可数。
[例] ① We moored the boat at the marina for the winter. 我们把船停在游艇码头过冬。 ② The marina offers fuel, repairs, and showers for sailors. 码头提供加油、维修和淋浴。
[搭] yacht marina 游艇码头, marina berth 泊位, stay at a marina 停靠 marina, marina services 码头服务
[联] harbour 港湾, yacht 游艇, boat 小船, dock 码头, moor 停泊, sail 航行
[记] 可数：a marina。源自 marine（海的）

**anchor**
英/美 /ˈæŋkə(r)/
n. 锚
v. 抛锚；固定
[义] A heavy metal object dropped from a ship to hold it in place; to secure a vessel. 自船抛下、固定船位的重金属物；使船固定（drop anchor 抛锚, weigh anchor 起锚, anchor chain 锚链）。可数/动词。
[例] ① The captain ordered the crew to drop anchor in the bay. 船长命船员在海湾抛锚。 ② Fishing boats anchored close to the shore at dusk. 渔船黄昏在近岸抛锚。
[搭] drop anchor 抛锚, weigh anchor 起锚, anchor chain 锚链, at anchor 抛锚中, news anchor 新闻主播〔另一义〕
[联] ship 大船, boat 小船, harbour 港湾, moor 停泊, vessel 船只, sailor 水手
[记] anchor / anchored / anchoring。news anchor 为另一常用义

**cockpit**
英/美 /ˈkɒkpɪt/
n. 驾驶舱；座舱
[义] The area in an aircraft, car, or racing boat where the pilot or driver sits and controls the vehicle. 飞机、赛车或赛艇中飞行员或驾驶员就座并操控的区域（aircraft cockpit 飞机驾驶舱, cockpit voice recorder 驾驶舱话音记录器, enter the cockpit 进入驾驶舱）。可数。
[例] ① Passengers are not allowed in the cockpit during flight. 飞行中乘客不得进入驾驶舱。 ② The pilot checked the instruments in the cockpit before takeoff. 起飞前飞行员检查驾驶舱仪表。
