[记] 可数：a cockpit。原指斗鸡场，后喻「操控中心」

**monorail**
英/美 /ˈmɒnəʊreɪl/
n. 单轨铁路
[义] A railway system where trains run on a single rail, often elevated. 列车在单根轨道上运行的铁路系统，常为高架（city monorail 城市单轨, monorail station 单轨站, take the monorail 乘单轨）。可数。
[例] ① The monorail links the airport to the city centre. 单轨连接机场与市中心。 ② Tourists ride the monorail through the theme park. 游客乘单轨穿越主题公园。
[搭] city monorail 城市单轨, monorail station 单轨站, take the monorail 乘单轨, elevated monorail 高架单轨
[联] train 火车, subway 地铁, rail 铁轨, station 车站, public transport 公共交通, platform 站台
[记] 可数：a monorail。mono-（单）+ rail（轨）

**cable car**
英 /ˈkeɪbl kɑː(r)/ 美 /ˈkeɪbl kɑːr/
n. 缆车
[义] A vehicle hanging from a cable, used to carry people up and down mountains or across rivers. 悬于缆绳、用于山地或跨河运送人员的车辆（mountain cable car 山地缆车, cable car ride 乘缆车, scenic cable car 观光缆车）。可数。
[例] ① We took a cable car to the summit for the view. 我们乘缆车到山顶看风景。 ② The cable car sways slightly in the wind. 缆车在风中微微晃动。
[搭] mountain cable car 山地缆车, cable car ride 乘缆车, scenic cable car 观光缆车, cable car station 缆车站
[联] tram 有轨电车, train 火车, mountain 山, tourist 游客, ski 滑雪, gondola 贡多拉
[记] 可数：a cable car。旧金山 cable car 为地标

**bumper**
英/美 /ˈbʌmpə(r)/
n. 保险杠
[义] A bar fixed along the front or back of a car to reduce damage in a minor collision. 装在汽车前后、减轻轻微碰撞损伤的横条（front bumper 前保险杠, bumper sticker 车尾贴纸, bumper-to-bumper traffic 走走停停的车流）。可数。
[例] ① The parking mishap left a dent in the rear bumper. 停车小事故在后保险杠留了凹痕。 ② Bumper stickers declared support for every cause imaginable. 车尾贴满各种诉求贴纸。
[搭] front bumper 前保险杠, rear bumper 后保险杠, bumper sticker 车尾贴纸, bumper-to-bumper traffic 走走停停的车流
[联] car 汽车, accident 事故, vehicle 车辆, collision 碰撞, traffic 交通, repair 维修
[记] 可数：a bumper。bumper crop 喻「大丰收」，不同义

**headlight**
英/美 /ˈhedlaɪt/
n. 前大灯
[义] A powerful light at the front of a vehicle. 车辆前部的强光灯（turn on headlights 开大灯, headlight beam 光束, dim the headlights 调暗大灯）。可数。常用复数 headlights。英式亦说 headlamp。
[例] ① Remember to turn on your headlights after dark. 天黑后记得开大灯。 ② Oncoming headlights blinded him for a moment. 对向车灯一时晃得他睁不开眼。
[搭] turn on headlights 开大灯, headlight beam 光束, dim the headlights 调暗大灯, broken headlight 损坏的前灯
[联] car 汽车, night 夜晚, visibility 能见度, traffic 交通, windshield 挡风玻璃, driver 司机
[记] 可数，常用复数 headlights。BrE headlamp 同义

**horn**
英/美 /hɔːn/
n. 喇叭；号角
[义] The device on a vehicle that makes a loud warning sound; a brass musical instrument (另一义). 车辆上发出响亮警示声的装置；铜管乐器。可数。
[例] ① The driver honked the horn at the pedestrian who stepped out. 行人突然走出时司机按喇叭。 ② Horns blared in the traffic jam downtown. 市区堵车中喇叭齐鸣。
[搭] honk the horn 按喇叭, car horn 汽车喇叭, horn blare 喇叭鸣响, blow the horn 按喇叭
[联] traffic 交通, car 汽车, warning 警告, driver 司机, accident 事故, noise 噪音
[记] 可数：a horn。honk 为按喇叭动词（AmE）

**axle**
英/美 /ˈæksl/
n. 车轴；轴
[义] A rod connecting a pair of wheels on a vehicle. 连接一对车轮的杆（rear axle 后轴, broken axle 断轴, axle load 轴荷）。可数。
[例] ① The mechanic found a crack in the rear axle. 机修工发现后轴有裂纹。 ② Heavy trucks spread weight across multiple axles. 重型卡车将重量分摊到多根车轴。
[搭] rear axle 后轴, broken axle 断轴, axle load 轴荷, front axle 前轴
[联] wheel 轮子, truck 卡车, vehicle 车辆, brake 刹车, tire 轮胎, mechanic 机修工
[记] 可数：an axle。 axle load 为货运与桥梁限重术语

**airbag**
英/美 /ˈeəbæɡ/
n. 安全气囊
[义] A safety bag in a vehicle that inflates instantly in a crash to protect occupants. 车祸时瞬间充气、保护乘员的安全气囊（airbag deployment 气囊弹出, driver airbag 驾驶座气囊, side airbag 侧气囊）。可数。
[例] ① The airbag deployed when the car hit the barrier. 撞栏时安全气囊弹出。 ② New models include side airbags for rear passengers. 新车型为后排配侧气囊。
[搭] airbag deployment 气囊弹出, driver airbag 驾驶座气囊, side airbag 侧气囊, airbag recall 气囊召回
[联] seat belt 安全带, crash 碰撞, safety 安全, car 汽车, passenger 乘客, accident 事故
[记] 可数：an airbag。air + bag。须与 seat belt 同用，不可替代

**steering wheel**
英 /ˈstɪərɪŋ wiːl/ 美 /ˈstɪrɪŋ wiːl/
n. 方向盘
[义] The wheel that the driver turns to control the direction of a car or other vehicle. 驾驶员转动以控制汽车等行进方向的圆盘（grip the steering wheel 握方向盘, steering wheel lock 方向盘锁, hands on the steering wheel 双手握盘）。可数。
[例] ① Keep both hands on the steering wheel while driving. 开车时双手握方向盘。 ② The steering wheel vibrated at high speed. 高速时方向盘振动。
[搭] grip the steering wheel 握方向盘, steering wheel lock 方向盘锁, hands on the steering wheel 双手握盘
[联] wheel 轮子, driver 司机, car 汽车, brake 刹车, turn 转弯, vehicle 车辆
[记] 可数：a steering wheel。steer 为动词「掌舵、转向」

**wiper**
英/美 /ˈwaɪpə(r)/
n. 雨刮器
[义] A device with a rubber blade that moves across a windshield to clear rain or snow. 橡胶片在挡风玻璃上摆动、清除雨雪的装置（windshield wiper 雨刮, wiper blade 雨刮片, turn on the wipers 开雨刮）。可数。常用复数 wipers。
[例] ① The wipers struggled to keep up with the heavy rain. 大雨中雨刮几乎跟不上。 ② Replace worn wiper blades before winter. 入冬前更换磨损雨刮片。
[搭] windshield wiper 雨刮, wiper blade 雨刮片, turn on the wipers 开雨刮, rear wiper 后雨刮
[联] windshield 挡风玻璃, windscreen 挡风玻璃, rain 雨, visibility 能见度, car 汽车, driver 司机
[记] 可数，常用复数 wipers。wipe 为动词「擦」

**dashboard**
英/美 /ˈdæʃbɔːd/
n. 仪表盘；仪表板
[义] The panel in front of the driver with instruments, gauges, and controls. 驾驶员前方带仪表、 gauges 与操控的 panel（dashboard light 仪表灯, dashboard camera 行车记录仪, on the dashboard 在仪表板上）。可数。
[例] ① A warning light flashed on the dashboard. 仪表盘上警示灯闪烁。 ② She kept spare change in a cup on the dashboard. 她把零钱放在仪表板杯架里。
[搭] dashboard light 仪表灯, dashboard camera 行车记录仪, on the dashboard 在仪表板上, digital dashboard 数字仪表
[联] steering wheel 方向盘, driver 司机, car 汽车, speedometer 车速表, engine 引擎, cockpit 驾驶舱
[记] 可数：a dashboard。dash（猛冲）+ board；原指马车前挡泥板

**rearview mirror**
英 /ˈrɪəvjuː mɪrə(r)/ 美 /ˈrɪrvjuː mɪrər/
n. 后视镜
[义] A mirror inside a car that lets the driver see behind the vehicle. 车内供驾驶员看车后情况的镜子（check the rearview mirror 看后视镜, rearview mirror adjustment 后视镜调节）。可数。
[例] ① Always check your rearview mirror before changing lanes. 变道前务必看后视镜。 ② The taxi's rearview mirror hung by a thread. 出租车后视镜几乎要掉下来。
[搭] check the rearview mirror 看后视镜, rearview mirror adjustment 后视镜调节, rearview mirror frame 后视镜框
[联] mirror 镜子, driver 司机, car 汽车, lane 车道, blind spot 盲区, side mirror 侧镜
[记] 可数：a rearview mirror。亦写 rear-view mirror

**hybrid**
英/美 /ˈhaɪbrɪd/
n. 混合动力车；混合物
adj. 混合的
[义] A vehicle that uses both a petrol engine and an electric motor to save fuel. 同时使用汽油机与电动机以省油的车辆（hybrid car 混动车, plug-in hybrid 插电混动, hybrid engine 混动系统）。可数/形容词。
[例] ① Hybrid cars are popular in cities with stop-start traffic. 混动车在走走停停的城市很受欢迎。 ② She bought a plug-in hybrid to shorten her commute costs. 她买了插电混动以降通勤成本。
[源] 源自拉丁语 *hybrida*（杂种）→ 两种动力混合。
[搭] hybrid car 混动车, plug-in hybrid 插电混动, hybrid engine 混动系统, hybrid vehicle 混动车
[联] electric vehicle 电动车, car 汽车, fuel 燃料, engine 引擎, battery 电池, emission 排放
[记] 可数/形容词。hybrid 亦指「杂交、混合体」，语境区分

**charging station**
英 /ˈtʃɑːdʒɪŋ steɪʃn/ 美 /ˈtʃɑːrdʒɪŋ steɪʃn/
n. 充电站
[义] A place where electric vehicles can connect to recharge their batteries. 电动汽车连接补能的站点（EV charging station 电动车充电站, fast charging station 快充站, public charging station 公共充电站）。可数。
[例] ① We waited at a charging station while the battery topped up. 我们在充电站等电池补满。 ② Supermarkets are installing charging stations in their car parks. 超市在停车场安装充电站。
[搭] EV charging station 电动车充电站, fast charging station 快充站, public charging station 公共充电站, charging station network 充电网络
[联] electric vehicle 电动车, battery 电池, car 汽车, petrol station 加油站, parking 停车, fuel 燃料
[记] 可数：a charging station。亦说 charging point / charger

**junction**
英/美 /ˈdʒʌŋkʃn/
n. 交叉路口；交汇处
[义] A place where roads or railway lines meet or cross. 道路或铁路交汇之处（road junction 道路交叉口, railway junction 铁路枢纽, at the junction 在路口）。可数。英式 road junction；美式 intersection 更常说。
[例] ① Turn right at the next junction. 在下个路口右转。 ② The railway junction handles freight from three lines. 铁路枢纽处理三条线的货运。
[搭] road junction 道路交叉口, railway junction 铁路枢纽, at the junction 在路口, busy junction 繁忙路口
[联] intersection 交叉路口, roundabout 环岛, road 道路, traffic 交通, turn 转弯, railway 铁路
[记] 可数：a junction。junction vs. intersection：前者英式更常用

**overpass**
英/美 /ˈəʊvəpɑːs/
n. 立交桥；天桥
[义] A bridge carrying one road over another (AmE). 使一条路跨越另一条路的桥梁（美式）（highway overpass 高速立交桥, pedestrian overpass 人行天桥, flyover 英式）。可数。
[例] ① The overpass reduced congestion at the busy intersection. 立交桥缓解了繁忙路口拥堵。 ② Graffiti covered the concrete walls of the overpass. 涂鸦布满立交桥混凝土墙。
[搭] highway overpass 高速立交桥, pedestrian overpass 人行天桥, overpass bridge 立交桥
[联] underpass 地下通道, bridge 桥梁, highway 公路, intersection 交叉路口, traffic 交通, road 道路
[记] 可数：an overpass。BrE flyover 同义

**underpass**
英/美 /ˈʌndəpɑːs/
n. 地下通道；下穿道
[义] A road or path that passes under another road or railway. 从另一道路或铁路下方穿过的路或通道（pedestrian underpass 人行地道, railway underpass 铁路下穿, dark underpass 昏暗地道）。可数。
[例] ① Pedestrians use the underpass to cross the motorway safely. 行人走地下通道安全穿越高速。 ② The underpass flooded after heavy rain. 大雨后地下通道积水。
[搭] pedestrian underpass 人行地道, railway underpass 铁路下穿, dark underpass 昏暗地道
[联] overpass 立交桥, tunnel 隧道, crosswalk 人行横道, road 道路, pedestrian 行人, bridge 桥梁
[记] 可数：an underpass。under + pass → 从下方通过

**ramp**
英/美 /ræmp/
n. 匝道；坡道
[义] A sloping road connecting a highway to another road; a slope for wheelchair access. 连接高速公路与其他道路的斜路；轮椅坡道（highway ramp 高速匝道, on-ramp 入口匝道, off-ramp 出口匝道）。可数。
[例] ① Merge carefully when joining the freeway from the ramp. 从匝道并入高速时小心。 ② The disabled parking space has a ramp to the sidewalk. 残障车位有坡道通人行道。
[搭] highway ramp 高速匝道, on-ramp 入口匝道, off-ramp 出口匝道, wheelchair ramp 轮椅坡道
[联] freeway 高速公路, motorway 高速公路, highway 公路, merge 并入, exit 出口, intersection 交叉路口
[记] 可数：a ramp。on-ramp / off-ramp 美式高速常用

**car park**
英 /ˈkɑː pɑːk/ 美 /ˈkɑːr pɑːrk/
n. 停车场
[义] An area or building where people can leave their cars (BrE). 供人停车的区域或建筑（英式）（multi-storey car park 多层停车场, car park ticket 停车票, free car park 免费停车场）。可数。美式 parking lot / parking garage。
[例] ① The car park was full by nine in the morning. 停车场早上九点就满了。 ② Remember where you left the car in the multi-storey car park. 记住多层停车场里车停哪层。
[搭] multi-storey car park 多层停车场, car park ticket 停车票, free car park 免费停车场, car park space 停车位
[联] parking 停车, parking lot 停车场, garage 车库, car 汽车, vehicle 车辆, ticket 罚单
[记] 可数：a car park。BrE car park，AmE parking lot

**number plate**
英 /ˈnʌmbə pleɪt/ 美 /ˈnʌmbər pleɪt/
n. 车牌
[义] A plate on a vehicle showing its registration number (BrE). 车辆上显示登记号码的牌（英式）（front number plate 前牌, number plate recognition 车牌识别, fake number plate 假牌）。可数。美式 license plate。
[例] ① The witness noted the number plate before the car drove off. 目击者记下肇事车车牌后它驶离。 ② Automatic number plate recognition cameras line the motorway. 高速沿线设自动车牌识别摄像头。
[搭] front number plate 前牌, number plate recognition 车牌识别, fake number plate 假牌, read a number plate 记车牌
[联] license plate 车牌, car 汽车, vehicle 车辆, registration 登记, police 警察, traffic 交通
[记] 可数：a number plate。BrE number plate，AmE license plate

**rush hour**
英 /ˈrʌʃ aʊə(r)/ 美 /ˈrʌʃ aʊər/
n. 高峰时段
[义] The times at the beginning and end of the working day when traffic is busiest. 工作日开始与结束时交通最繁忙的时段（morning rush hour 早高峰, rush-hour traffic 高峰车流, avoid rush hour 避开高峰）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① Commutes take twice as long during rush hour. 高峰时段通勤时间翻倍。 ② She leaves early to beat the morning rush hour. 她早出发以避开早高峰。
[搭] morning rush hour 早高峰, evening rush hour 晚高峰, rush-hour traffic 高峰车流, avoid rush hour 避开高峰
[联] commute 通勤, traffic 交通, congestion 拥堵, subway 地铁, bus 公共汽车, commuter 通勤者
[记] 可数/不可数。rush hour vs. peak hour：前者更口语

**carpool**
英/美 /ˈkɑːpuːl/
n. 拼车；共乘
v. 拼车上下班
[义] An arrangement where people share a car journey, often to work; to take part in it (AmE). 多人共用一辆车出行，常指上下班（美式）（carpool lane 共乘车道, join a carpool 加入拼车, carpool with colleagues 与同事拼车）。可数/动词。英式 lift-share。
[例] ① They carpool to the office three days a week. 他们每周三天拼车上班。 ② The carpool lane is reserved for vehicles with two or more people. 共乘车道专供两人及以上车辆。
[搭] carpool lane 共乘车道, join a carpool 加入拼车, carpool with colleagues 与同事拼车, carpool schedule 拼车日程
[联] commute 通勤, driver 司机, passenger 乘客, traffic 交通, lane 车道, public transport 公共交通
[记] 可数/动词。亦拼 car pool。carpool lane = HOV lane（high-occupancy vehicle）

**speed limit**
英 /ˈspiːd lɪmɪt/ 美 /ˈspiːd lɪmɪt/
n. 限速
[义] The maximum speed legally allowed on a particular road. 某条道路上法律允许的最高速度（30 mph speed limit 三十英里限速, exceed the speed limit 超速, speed limit sign 限速标志）。可数。
[例] ① The speed limit drops to twenty near the school. 学校附近限速降至二十。 ② Cameras enforce the speed limit on this stretch of motorway. 这段高速用摄像头执行限速。
[搭] exceed the speed limit 超速, speed limit sign 限速标志, lower the speed limit 降低限速, 30 mph speed limit 三十英里限速
[联] highway 公路, motorway 高速公路, traffic 交通, driver 司机, police 警察, road 道路
[记] 可数：a speed limit。speed 本身为通用词；speed limit 为交通设施/规则用语，收本章

**container ship**
英/美 /kənˈteɪnə ʃɪp/
n. 集装箱船
[义] A large cargo ship designed to carry standard metal containers. 运载标准金属集装箱的大型货轮（container ship port 集装箱港口, container ship traffic 集装箱航运, mega container ship 超大型集装箱船）。可数。
[例] ① Container ships queue for days at major ports during peak season. 旺季主要港口集装箱船排队数天。 ② A single container ship can carry thousands of boxes. 一艘集装箱船可运数千箱。
[搭] container ship port 集装箱港口, container ship traffic 集装箱航运, mega container ship 超大型集装箱船
[联] cargo 货物, freight 货运, port 港口, dock 码头, vessel 船只, shipping 航运
[记] 可数：a container ship。全球贸易骨干船型

**hovercraft**
英/美 /ˈhɒvəkrɑːft/
n. 气垫船
[义] A vehicle that travels over land or water on a cushion of air. 靠气垫在陆地或水面行驶的交通工具（passenger hovercraft 客运气垫船, hovercraft crossing 气垫渡, hovercraft service 气垫服务）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① The hovercraft crossed the channel in under forty minutes. 气垫船四十分钟内过海峡。 ② Hovercraft can reach beaches without a proper harbour. 气垫船无需正规码头即可登滩。
[搭] passenger hovercraft 客运气垫船, hovercraft crossing 气垫渡, hovercraft service 气垫服务
[联] ferry 渡轮, boat 小船, ship 大船, vessel 船只, coast 海岸, passenger 乘客
[记] 可数/不可数。hover（悬浮）+ craft（飞行器/船）

**gondola**
英/美 /ˈɡɒndələ/
n. 贡多拉；缆车吊厢
[义] A long narrow boat used on the canals of Venice; a cabin hanging from a cable on a ski lift or scenic ride. 威尼斯运河上的狭长小船；滑雪或观光缆绳下的吊厢（Venice gondola 威尼斯贡多拉, ski gondola 滑雪缆车, gondola ride 乘贡多拉/缆车）。可数。
[例] ① Tourists queued for a gondola ride through the canals. 游客排队乘贡多拉游运河。 ② The ski gondola carried us to the summit in minutes. 滑雪缆车几分钟把我们送到山顶。
[搭] Venice gondola 威尼斯贡多拉, ski gondola 滑雪缆车, gondola ride 乘贡多拉/缆车, cable gondola 吊厢缆车
[联] boat 小船, canal 运河, cable car 缆车, Venice 威尼斯, ski 滑雪, tourist 游客
[记] 可数：a gondola。义随场景：水城小船 vs. 山地吊厢

**motorhome**
英/美 /ˈməʊtəhəʊm/
n. 自行式房车
[义] A large motor vehicle built like a house on wheels, with beds, kitchen, and bathroom. 带床、厨房和卫生间的「轮子上的房子」式大型机动车（rent a motorhome 租房车, motorhome trip 房车旅行, motorhome park 房车营地）。可数。
[例] ① They crossed the desert in a motorhome with solar panels on the roof. 他们开顶装太阳能板的房车穿越沙漠。 ② Motorhome rentals spike every summer along the coast. 沿海夏季房车租赁激增。
[搭] rent a motorhome 租房车, motorhome trip 房车旅行, motorhome park 房车营地, motorhome hookup 房车水电接口
[联] RV 房车, caravan 拖挂房车, camping 露营, highway 公路, vehicle 车辆, trailer 拖车
[记] 可数：a motorhome。BrE 亦常用 motorhome；与 caravan（拖挂）不同，自带引擎

**kayak**
英/美 /ˈkaɪæk/
n. 皮划艇； kayak
[义] A small narrow boat for one or two people, closed on top except for a cockpit, moved with a double-bladed paddle. 一至两人、除 cockpit 外顶盖封闭、用双刃桨划行的小窄船（sea kayak 海上皮划艇, kayak paddle 双叶桨, rent a kayak 租皮划艇）。可数。
[例] ① We paddled kayaks around the island at sunrise. 日出时我们划皮划艇绕岛。 ② Kayaks are easier to roll upright after capsizing than canoes. 皮划艇翻后比独木舟更易翻正。
[搭] sea kayak 海上皮划艇, kayak paddle 双叶桨, rent a kayak 租皮划艇, kayak tour 皮划艇之旅
[联] canoe 独木舟, paddle 桨, boat 小船, river 河, lake 湖, life jacket 救生衣
[记] 可数：a kayak。源自因纽特语。qajaq 为原词

**sailboat**
英/美 /ˈseɪlbəʊt/
n. 帆船
[义] A boat that is driven mainly by sails rather than an engine. 主要靠帆而非引擎驱动的船（sailing sailboat 帆船, sailboat race 帆船赛, rent a sailboat 租帆船）。可数。亦说 sailing boat（英式）。
[例] ① White sailboats dotted the bay on the windy afternoon. 有风的下午海湾点缀着白帆。 ② She learned to handle a sailboat on the lake as a teenager. 她少年时在湖上学驾帆船。
[搭] sailboat race 帆船赛, rent a sailboat 租帆船, sailboat club 帆船俱乐部, racing sailboat 竞赛帆船
[联] boat 小船, sail 帆, yacht 游艇, wind 风, harbour 港湾, marina 游艇码头
[记] 可数：a sailboat。BrE sailing boat 同义

**dinghy**
英/美 /ˈdɪŋi/
n. 小艇；橡皮艇
[义] A small open boat, often carried on or towed by a larger ship; an inflatable rubber boat. 常由大船携带或拖带的小敞船；充气橡皮艇（inflatable dinghy 充气艇, rowing dinghy 划艇, ship's dinghy 船载小艇）。可数。
[例] ① The crew lowered a dinghy to reach the shallow shore. 船员放下小艇抵浅岸。 ② An inflatable dinghy carried us from the yacht to the beach. 充气艇把我们从游艇送到海滩。
[搭] inflatable dinghy 充气艇, rowing dinghy 划艇, ship's dinghy 船载小艇, dinghy sailing 小艇帆船
[联] boat 小船, yacht 游艇, row 划船, lifeboat 救生艇, harbour 港湾, paddle 桨
[记] 可数：a dinghy。发音 /ˈdɪŋi/ 或 /ˈdɪŋɡi/

**barge**
英/美 /bɑːdʒ/
n. 驳船；平底货船
[义] A long flat-bottomed boat for carrying freight on rivers and canals, often pushed or towed. 运河与江河上运货的长平底船，常被顶推或拖带（ canal barge 运河驳船, barge traffic 驳船运输, house barge 住家驳船）。可数。
[例] ① Barges moved slowly along the canal loaded with grain. 驳船载满谷物沿运河缓行。 ② Some people convert old barges into floating homes. 有人把旧驳船改成水上住家。
[搭] canal barge 运河驳船, barge traffic 驳船运输, house barge 住家驳船, tow a barge 拖驳船
[联] boat 小船, ship 大船, cargo 货物, freight 货运, canal 运河, vessel 船只
[记] 可数：a barge。内河货运常见；house barge 为英式水上住宅

**tanker**
英/美 /ˈtæŋkə(r)/
n. 油轮；罐车
[义] A ship or road vehicle built to carry large quantities of liquid, especially oil or fuel. 运载大量液体（尤指石油或燃料）的船舶或公路车辆（oil tanker 油轮, tanker truck 油罐车, chemical tanker 化学品船）。可数。
[例] ① An oil tanker anchored offshore waiting to unload. 油轮在近海抛锚待卸。 ② Tanker trucks supply fuel to remote villages. 油罐车给偏远村庄供油。
[搭] oil tanker 油轮, tanker truck 油罐车, chemical tanker 化学品船, LNG tanker 液化天然气船
[联] ship 大船, cargo 货物, fuel 燃料, port 港口, vessel 船只, truck 卡车
[记] 可数：a tanker。tank（罐）+ -er → 载罐之船/车

**glider**
英/美 /ˈɡlaɪdə(r)/
n. 滑翔机
[义] A light aircraft without an engine that flies using rising air currents. 无引擎、靠上升气流飞行的轻型航空器（glider pilot 滑翔员, hang glider 悬挂式滑翔, sailplane 滑翔机同义）。可数。
[例] ① The glider soared silently above the ridge for an hour. 滑翔机在脊线上方静默盘旋一小时。 ② She trained as a glider pilot before switching to powered flight. 她先学滑翔再转有动力飞行。
[搭] glider pilot 滑翔员, hang glider 悬挂式滑翔, glider club 滑翔俱乐部, motor glider 动力滑翔机
[联] aircraft 航空器, airplane 飞机, pilot 飞行员, flight 飞行, wing 翼, soar 翱翔
[记] 可数：a glider。glide（滑翔）+ -er。sailplane 为同义正式词

**locomotive**
英/美 /ˌləʊkəˈməʊtɪv/
n. 机车；火车头
[义] A railway engine that pulls a train. 牵引列车的铁路机车（steam locomotive 蒸汽机车, diesel locomotive 柴油机车, electric locomotive 电力机车）。可数。
[例] ① The museum restored a Victorian steam locomotive. 博物馆修复了一台维多利亚时代蒸汽机车。 ② Diesel locomotives replaced steam on most lines by the 1960s. 六十年代前多数线路柴油机车取代蒸汽。
[源] 源自拉丁语 *loco*（在某处）+ *motivus*（移动的）→ 在轨道上自行移动之物。
[搭] steam locomotive 蒸汽机车, diesel locomotive 柴油机车, electric locomotive 电力机车, locomotive engineer 机车司机
[联] train 火车, railway 铁路, engine 引擎, carriage 车厢, freight 货运, platform 站台
[记] 可数：a locomotive。口语 engine 亦指机车

**carriage**
英/美 /ˈkærɪdʒ/
n. 火车车厢；马车
[义] Any of the separate sections of a train where passengers sit or goods are carried; a horse-drawn vehicle (旧义). 列车中供乘客就座或装货的分节车厢；马车（旧义）（railway carriage 火车车厢, first-class carriage 头等车厢, carriage door 车厢门）。可数。
[例] ① Passengers rushed to find seats in the crowded carriage. 乘客挤进拥挤车厢找座。 ② The last carriage of the train was reserved for luggage. 列车最后一节车厢留放行李。
[搭] railway carriage 火车车厢, first-class carriage 头等车厢, carriage door 车厢门, dining carriage 餐车
[联] train 火车, compartment 隔间, platform 站台, passenger 乘客, locomotive 机车, ticket 车票
[记] 可数：a carriage。BrE carriage，AmE car（railroad car）。carriage 亦指「婴儿车、运费」

**compartment**
英/美 /kəmˈpɑːtmənt/
n. 隔间；车厢包间
[义] A separate section of a train carriage, often for a small group of passengers. 火车车厢内供少数乘客使用的分隔区（ sleeping compartment 卧铺包间, luggage compartment 行李间, first-class compartment 头等隔间）。可数。
[例] ① They shared a six-seat compartment on the overnight train. 他们共享夜车六人隔间。 ② Store valuables in the locked luggage compartment. 贵重物品放锁好的行李间。
[搭] sleeping compartment 卧铺包间, luggage compartment 行李间, first-class compartment 头等隔间, compartment door 隔间门
[联] carriage 车厢, train 火车, sleeper 卧铺, passenger 乘客, luggage 行李, ticket 车票
[记] 可数：a compartment。com-（共同）+ part（部分）→ 分隔的部分

**sleeper**
英/美 /ˈsliːpə(r)/
n. 卧铺；卧铺列车
[义] A bed on a train, or a train that has beds for passengers travelling overnight. 火车上的床位，或配有床位供过夜乘客的列车（ overnight sleeper 过夜卧铺车, sleeper train 卧铺列车, sleeper berth 卧铺位）。可数。
[例] ① We booked a sleeper from Paris to Venice. 我们订了巴黎至威尼斯卧铺。 ② The top sleeper was narrow but surprisingly comfortable. 上铺虽窄却出奇舒服。
[搭] overnight sleeper 过夜卧铺车, sleeper train 卧铺列车, sleeper berth 卧铺位, sleeper car 卧铺车厢
[联] train 火车, compartment 隔间, carriage 车厢, ticket 车票, commute 通勤, passenger 乘客
[记] 可数：a sleeper。sleeper 亦指「枕木、冷门选手」，语境区分

**rail**
英/美 /reɪl/
n. 铁轨；栏杆
[义] The metal track that trains run on; a bar fixed for support or safety. 火车行驶的金属轨道；固定用作支撑或安全的横杆（ railway rail 铁轨, off the rails 出轨〔喻〕, light rail 轻轨）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① Workers replaced damaged rails after the derailment. 脱轨后工人更换损坏铁轨。 ② The city is extending light rail to the suburbs. 该市正把轻轨延伸到郊区。
[搭] railway rail 铁轨, light rail 轻轨, off the rails 出轨, rail network 铁路网, high-speed rail 高铁
[联] railway 铁路, train 火车, locomotive 机车, track 轨道, subway 地铁, platform 站台
[记] 可数/不可数。rail vs. track：rail 指钢轨本身，track 指整条线路

**sail**
英/美 /seɪl/
n. 帆
v. 航行；启航
[义] A sheet of strong cloth fixed to a boat to catch the wind and move it. 固定在船上、借风力推动船只的坚牢布片（set sail 启航, hoist the sails 升帆, under sail 张帆航行）。可数/动词。
[例] ① White sails filled as the breeze picked up. 风起时白帆鼓起。 ② The ferry sails for the island at noon. 渡轮正午启航赴岛。
[搭] set sail 启航, hoist the sails 升帆, under sail 张帆航行, sail away 扬帆而去
[联] sailboat 帆船, boat 小船, yacht 游艇, wind 风, harbour 港湾, anchor 锚
[记] 可数/动词。动词 sail 表「航行」属通用动作，本章收具名 sail 及固定搭配

**exhaust**
英/美 /ɪɡˈzɔːst/
n. 排气管；废气
v. 耗尽；使疲惫
[义] The pipe through which waste gases leave a vehicle engine; the gases themselves. 车辆引擎排出废气的管道；废气本身（ exhaust pipe 排气管, exhaust fumes 尾气, exhaust system 排气系统）。不可数/动词。
[例] ① Black exhaust poured from the old truck's tailpipe. 旧卡车的尾管冒出黑烟。 ② The garage checked the exhaust for leaks. 修理厂检查排气管是否漏气。
[搭] exhaust pipe 排气管, exhaust fumes 尾气, exhaust system 排气系统, exhaust emission 尾气排放
[联] engine 引擎, muffler 消音器, diesel 柴油, emission 排放, pollution 污染, vehicle 车辆
[记] 不可数/动词。动词 exhaust 表「耗尽、使筋疲力尽」，不同义

**muffler**
英/美 /ˈmʌflə(r)/
n. 消音器；围巾
[义] A device on a vehicle that reduces noise from the exhaust (AmE). 降低排气噪音的车辆装置（美式）（ replace the muffler 换消音器, muffler noise 排气噪音）。可数。英式 silencer。
[例] ① The mechanic said the muffler had rusted through. 机修工说消音器已锈穿。 ② A loud muffler announced his arrival before we saw the car. 还没见车，响亮的排气声就宣告他到了。
[搭] replace the muffler 换消音器, muffler noise 排气噪音, broken muffler 损坏的消音器
[联] exhaust 排气管, silencer 消音器, engine 引擎, car 汽车, noise 噪音, emission 排放
[记] 可数：a muffler。AmE muffler，BrE silencer。muffler 亦指「围巾」

**silencer**
英 /ˈsaɪlənsə(r)/ 美 /ˈsaɪlənsər/
n. 消音器
[义] A device that reduces noise from a vehicle's exhaust (BrE). 降低车辆排气噪音的装置（英式）（ exhaust silencer 排气消音器, replace the silencer 更换消音器）。可数。美式 muffler。亦指枪支消音器，语境区分。
[例] ① The silencer fell off on the motorway and had to be replaced. 消音器在高速上掉了，只得更换。 ② Garages must test exhaust silencers during the MOT. 检车时必须测排气消音器。
[搭] exhaust silencer 排气消音器, replace the silencer 更换消音器, faulty silencer 故障消音器
[联] muffler 消音器, exhaust 排气管, engine 引擎, car 汽车, noise 噪音
[记] 可数：a silencer。BrE silencer，AmE muffler

**side mirror**
英 /ˈsaɪd mɪrə(r)/ 美 /ˈsaɪd mɪrər/
n. 侧后视镜
[义] A mirror on the side of a car that lets the driver see areas beside and behind the vehicle. 车身两侧、供驾驶员看侧后方区域的镜子（adjust the side mirrors 调侧镜, side mirror blind spot 侧镜盲区, fold in the side mirrors 折叠侧镜）。可数。亦说 wing mirror（英式）。
[例] ① Check your side mirrors before merging onto the highway. 并入高速前看侧后视镜。 ② A passing truck clipped our side mirror on the narrow road. 窄路上超车的卡车刮到了我们的侧镜。
[搭] adjust the side mirrors 调侧镜, side mirror blind spot 侧镜盲区, fold in the side mirrors 折叠侧镜, broken side mirror 损坏的侧镜
[联] rearview mirror 后视镜, mirror 镜子, driver 司机, car 汽车, blind spot 盲区, lane 车道
[记] 可数：a side mirror。BrE wing mirror 同义

**ignition**
英/美 /ɪɡˈnɪʃn/
n. 点火；点火开关
[义] The system that starts a vehicle engine; the switch or key position that turns it on. 启动车辆引擎的系统；点火开关或钥匙位置（ turn the ignition 拧点火, ignition key 点火钥匙, ignition failure 点火故障）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① She turned the ignition and the engine roared to life. 她拧点火，引擎轰然启动。 ② Police found the car with the keys still in the ignition. 警方发现钥匙还插在点火开关上的车。
[源] 源自拉丁语 *ignire*（点火），*ignis*（火）→ 引燃引擎。
[族] ignite 点燃, ignition 同族
[搭] turn the ignition 拧点火, ignition key 点火钥匙, ignition failure 点火故障, ignition switch 点火开关
[联] engine 引擎, key 钥匙, start 启动, car 汽车, fuel 燃料, dashboard 仪表盘
[记] 不可数/可数。keys in the ignition 固定说法

**gearbox**
英/美 /ˈɡɪəbɒks/
n. 变速箱；齿轮箱
[义] The set of gears in a vehicle that transfers power from the engine to the wheels. 将引擎动力传至车轮的一组齿轮装置（ manual gearbox 手动变速箱, automatic gearbox 自动变速箱, gearbox oil 变速箱油）。可数。
[例] ① The gearbox jammed and the car would not shift. 变速箱卡住，车无法换挡。 ② Electric cars often use a simpler gearbox than petrol cars. 电动车变速箱通常比汽油车简单。
[搭] manual gearbox 手动变速箱, automatic gearbox 自动变速箱, gearbox oil 变速箱油, gearbox repair 变速箱维修
[联] engine 引擎, clutch 离合器, wheel 轮子, car 汽车, transmission 传动, mechanic 机修工
[记] 可数：a gearbox。gear + box。AmE 亦说 transmission（更广）

**clutch**
英/美 /klʌtʃ/
n. 离合器；抓紧
v. 紧抓
[义] The pedal or mechanism in a manual car that connects and disconnects the engine from the wheels when changing gear. 手动挡汽车中换挡时连接/断开引擎与车轮的踏板或机构（ clutch pedal 离合踏板, ride the clutch 半踩离合, clutch failure 离合器故障）。可数/动词。
[例] ① Learning to use the clutch smoothly takes practice. 平顺用离合需要练。 ② The clutch burned out on the steep hill start. 陡坡起步时离合器烧坏了。
[搭] clutch pedal 离合踏板, ride the clutch 半踩离合, clutch failure 离合器故障, release the clutch 松离合
[联] gearbox 变速箱, brake 刹车, accelerator 油门, manual 手动的, car 汽车, mechanic 机修工
[记] 可数/动词。clutch 亦指「 clutch bag 手拿包、关键时刻」

**accelerator**
英/美 /əkˈseləreɪtə(r)/
n. 油门；加速器
[义] The pedal in a car that you press to make it go faster. 汽车中踩下以加速的踏板（ accelerator pedal 油门踏板, step on the accelerator 踩油门, release the accelerator 松油门）。可数。
[例] ① He stepped on the accelerator and passed the slow truck. 他踩油门超过慢车。 ② Keep your foot off the accelerator on icy roads. 结冰路面勿踩油门。
[搭] accelerator pedal 油门踏板, step on the accelerator 踩油门, release the accelerator 松油门, stuck accelerator 油门卡住
[联] brake 刹车, clutch 离合器, pedal 踏板, driver 司机, car 汽车, speed 速度
[记] 可数：an accelerator。AmE gas pedal 更口语；BrE accelerator

**bonnet**
英 /ˈbɒnɪt/ 美 /ˈbɑːnɪt/
n. 引擎盖；童帽
[义] The metal cover over the front engine of a car (BrE). 汽车前部罩住引擎的金属盖（英式）（ open the bonnet 打开引擎盖, bonnet latch 引擎盖锁扣, under the bonnet 在引擎盖下）。可数。美式 hood。
[例] ① He lifted the bonnet to check the oil level. 他掀起引擎盖查机油。 ② Steam rose from under the bonnet after the long climb. 长坡爬升后引擎盖下冒蒸汽。
[搭] open the bonnet 打开引擎盖, bonnet latch 引擎盖锁扣, under the bonnet 在引擎盖下, bonnet release 引擎盖释放拉柄
[联] hood 引擎盖, engine 引擎, car 汽车, mechanic 机修工, vehicle 车辆
[记] 可数：a bonnet。BrE bonnet，AmE hood。bonnet 亦指「童帽、帽饰」

**hood**
英/美 /hʊd/
n. 引擎盖； hood
[义] The hinged cover over the engine of a car (AmE); a covering for the head (另一义). 汽车引擎上可掀起的盖（美式）；头罩。可数。
[例] ① Pop the hood so I can look at the battery. 掀开引擎盖让我看看电池。 ② Something was rattling under the hood at idle. 怠速时引擎盖下有部件咯咯作响。
[搭] pop the hood 掀开引擎盖, under the hood 在引擎盖下, hood latch 引擎盖锁, open the hood 打开引擎盖
[联] bonnet 引擎盖, engine 引擎, car 汽车, mechanic 机修工, vehicle 车辆
[记] 可数：a hood。AmE hood，BrE bonnet。hood 亦指「 hood 头套、街区」

**chassis**
英 /ˈʃæsi/ 美 /ˈʃæsi/
n. 底盘；车架
[义] The frame of a vehicle, including the wheels and engine, on which the body is built. 含车轮与引擎、承载车身的车辆框架（ vehicle chassis 车辆底盘, chassis number 底盘号, rolling chassis 滚动底盘）。可数。复数 chassis /ˈʃæsiːz/。
[例] ① The chassis was rusted after years on salted winter roads. 多年冬季撒盐路后底盘锈蚀。 ② Electric cars mount the battery pack into the chassis. 电动车把电池组装入底盘。
[搭] vehicle chassis 车辆底盘, chassis number 底盘号, rolling chassis 滚动底盘, chassis design 底盘设计
[联] engine 引擎, wheel 轮子, axle 车轴, frame 框架, vehicle 车辆, suspension 悬挂
[记] 可数，复数 chassis。法语借词，末尾不发音 s

**fuselage**
英 /ˈfjuːzəlɑːʒ/ 美 /ˈfjuːsəlɑːʒ/
n. 机身
[义] The main body of an aircraft, excluding the wings and tail. 飞机除翼与尾以外的主体部分（ aircraft fuselage 飞机机身, fuselage section 机身段, wide-body fuselage 宽体机身）。可数。
[例] ① Engineers tested the fuselage for cracks after the hard landing. 硬着陆后工程师检测机身裂纹。 ② Passengers sit inside the pressurized fuselage. 乘客坐在加压机身内。
[搭] aircraft fuselage 飞机机身, fuselage section 机身段, wide-body fuselage 宽体机身, fuselage damage 机身损伤
[联] airplane 飞机, aircraft 航空器, wing 翼, cockpit 驾驶舱, landing gear 起落架, pilot 飞行员
[记] 可数：a fuselage。法语 *fuselé*（纺锤形）→ 纺锤状机身

**landing gear**
英 /ˈlændɪŋ ɡɪə(r)/ 美 /ˈlændɪŋ ɡɪr/
n. 起落架
[义] The wheels and supports under an aircraft used for takeoff and landing. 飞机起降用的轮与支撑装置（ retract the landing gear 收起落架, landing gear failure 起落架故障, nose landing gear 前起落架）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① The pilot lowered the landing gear on final approach. 飞行员在最后进近放下起落架。 ② A bird strike damaged the landing gear doors. 鸟击损坏了起落架舱门。
[搭] retract the landing gear 收起落架, landing gear failure 起落架故障, nose landing gear 前起落架, deploy landing gear 放起落架
[联] airplane 飞机, runway 跑道, pilot 飞行员, takeoff 起飞, landing 着陆, aircraft 航空器
[记] 不可数/可数。固定搭配 landing gear；亦说 undercarriage（英式）

**jet bridge**
英 /ˈdʒet brɪdʒ/ 美 /ˈdʒet brɪdʒ/
n. 登机桥；廊桥
[义] A movable enclosed corridor connecting an airport terminal to an aircraft door. 连接航站楼与飞机舱门的可移动封闭通道（ boarding jet bridge 登机廊桥, jet bridge gate 廊桥登机口, walk through the jet bridge 经廊桥登机）。可数。亦说 airbridge, jetway（商标）。
[例] ① Passengers boarded through the jet bridge in the rain. 雨中乘客经廊桥登机。 ② The jet bridge failed to align with the aircraft door. 廊桥未能对准舱门。
[搭] boarding jet bridge 登机廊桥, jet bridge gate 廊桥登机口, walk through the jet bridge 经廊桥登机
[联] airport 机场, terminal 航站楼, airplane 飞机, passenger 乘客, gate 登机口, boarding 登机
[记] 可数：a jet bridge。BrE airbridge；Jetway 为常见品牌名

**propeller**
英/美 /prəˈpelə(r)/
n. 螺旋桨
[义] A device with blades that spin to move a boat or aircraft forward. 叶片旋转以推动船舶或飞机前进的装置（ ship propeller 船用螺旋桨, propeller plane 螺旋桨飞机, propeller blade 桨叶）。可数。
[例] ① The propeller churned the water as the boat reversed. 船倒退时螺旋桨搅起水花。 ② Small propeller aircraft use less fuel on short routes. 短航线螺旋桨飞机更省油。
[搭] ship propeller 船用螺旋桨, propeller plane 螺旋桨飞机, propeller blade 桨叶, twin propellers 双螺旋桨
[联] engine 引擎, boat 小船, aircraft 航空器, jet 喷气机, ship 大船, blade 叶片
[记] 可数：a propeller。propel（推进）+ -er

**zebra crossing**
英 /ˈzebrə krɒsɪŋ/ 美 /ˈziːbrə krɔːsɪŋ/
n. 斑马线；人行横道
[义] A place on a road marked with broad white stripes where vehicles must stop for pedestrians (BrE). 路面上宽白条标出、车辆须为行人让行的过街处（英式）（ pelican crossing 按钮式横道, use the zebra crossing 走斑马线, approach a zebra crossing 接近横道）。可数。
[例] ① Drivers must give way to anyone on a zebra crossing. 司机须给斑马线上的人让行。 ② The school has a zebra crossing with a flashing beacon. 学校斑马线配有闪烁警示灯。
[搭] use the zebra crossing 走斑马线, approach a zebra crossing 接近横道, zebra crossing stripes 斑马线条纹
[联] crosswalk 人行横道, pedestrian 行人, traffic light 红绿灯, street 街道, road 道路, school 学校
[记] 可数：a zebra crossing。BrE zebra crossing，AmE crosswalk。条纹似 zebra

**pedestrian crossing**
英 /pəˈdestriən ˈkrɒsɪŋ/ 美 /pəˈdestriən ˈkrɔːsɪŋ/
n. 人行横道
[义] A marked place where pedestrians may cross a road safely. 标出供行人安全过马路的地点（ signalized pedestrian crossing 信号灯横道, near a pedestrian crossing 横道附近, school pedestrian crossing 学校横道）。可数。英式统称；含 zebra crossing 等类型。
[例] ① Slow down near the pedestrian crossing by the shops. 商店旁人行横道附近请减速。 ② The council added a pedestrian crossing after the accident. 事故后市议会增设人行横道。
[搭] signalized pedestrian crossing 信号灯横道, school pedestrian crossing 学校横道, approach a pedestrian crossing 接近横道
[联] zebra crossing 斑马线, crosswalk 人行横道, pedestrian 行人, traffic light 红绿灯, road 道路
[记] 可数：a pedestrian crossing。正式统称；具体类型有 zebra / pelican crossing

**traffic jam**
英 /ˈtræfɪk dʒæm/
n. 交通堵塞；堵车
[义] A line of vehicles that cannot move or can move only very slowly. 无法移动或只能极慢移动的车龙（ stuck in a traffic jam 堵在路上, rush-hour traffic jam 高峰堵车, cause a traffic jam 造成堵车）。可数。
[例] ① We were stuck in a traffic jam for forty minutes. 我们堵车四十分钟。 ② An overturned lorry caused a traffic jam for miles. 翻覆的货车造成数英里堵车。
[搭] stuck in a traffic jam 堵在路上, rush-hour traffic jam 高峰堵车, cause a traffic jam 造成堵车, traffic jam ahead 前方堵车
[联] traffic 交通, congestion 拥堵, gridlock 交通瘫痪, commute 通勤, highway 高速公路, road 道路
[记] 可数：a traffic jam。口语 jam 亦可单独表堵车

**gridlock**
英/美 /ˈɡrɪdlɒk/
n. 交通瘫痪；僵局
[义] A situation where traffic cannot move at all because vehicles block every intersection. 车辆堵死每个路口、交通完全无法移动的状态（ downtown gridlock 市中心瘫痪, political gridlock 政治僵局〔喻〕, avoid gridlock 避免堵死）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① Gridlock paralysed the city centre for hours. 交通瘫痪使市中心数小时动弹不得。 ② One accident during rush hour can trigger gridlock across the grid. 高峰一起事故可引发整片路网格瘫痪。
[搭] downtown gridlock 市中心瘫痪, avoid gridlock 避免堵死, traffic gridlock 交通瘫痪, gridlock situation 僵局
[联] traffic jam 堵车, congestion 拥堵, traffic 交通, intersection 交叉路口, commute 通勤, city 城市
[记] 不可数/可数。grid（网格）+ lock（锁死）→ 路口互相卡死

**detour**
英/美 /ˈdiːtʊə(r)/
n. 绕行；绕道
v. 绕路
[义] A longer route taken when the usual road is closed; to go that way. 常路封闭时走的长路；绕路（ take a detour 绕道, detour sign 绕行标志, scenic detour 风景绕道）。可数/动词。
[例] ① Roadworks forced us to take a ten-mile detour. 施工迫使我们绕路十英里。 ② The GPS recalculated a detour around the flooded bridge. 导航重新规划绕过淹桥的路线。
[搭] take a detour 绕道, detour sign 绕行标志, scenic detour 风景绕道, detour route 绕行路线
[联] road 道路, highway 公路, bridge 桥梁, traffic 交通, route 路线, construction 施工
[记] 可数/动词。de-（离开）+ tour（路线）→ 偏离原路

**bypass**
英/美 /ˈbaɪpɑːs/
n.  bypass；旁路
v. 绕过
[义] A road built around a town to carry traffic without going through the centre; a surgical operation (另一义). 绕城镇而建、不经过中心的公路；心脏搭桥（另一义）。可数/动词。
[例] ① The new bypass cut twenty minutes off the journey. 新 bypass 把行程缩短二十分钟。 ② Lorries are encouraged to use the bypass rather than the high street. 鼓励货车走 bypass 而非主街。
[搭] ring road bypass 绕城 bypass, bypass road 旁路, town bypass 城镇 bypass, take the bypass 走 bypass
[联] highway 公路, motorway 高速公路, detour 绕道, traffic 交通, town 城镇, road 道路
[记] 可数/动词。by-（旁边）+ pass（通过）→ 从旁通过

**service area**
英 /ˈsɜːvɪs eəriə/ 美 /ˈsɜːrvɪs eriə/
n. 服务区
[义] A place beside a motorway or highway with fuel, food, toilets, and parking. 高速或公路旁提供加油、餐饮、厕所与停车的区域（ motorway service area 高速服务区, stop at a service area 在服务区休息, 24-hour service area 24 小时服务区）。可数。英式 motorway services 常用复数。
[例] ① We stopped at a service area for coffee and petrol. 我们在服务区停了一下买咖啡和油。 ② Service areas on long motorways are spaced every forty miles. 长高速上服务区约每四十英里一处。
[搭] motorway service area 高速服务区, stop at a service area 在服务区休息, 24-hour service area 24 小时服务区
[联] motorway 高速公路, highway 公路, petrol station 加油站, parking 停车, rest 休息, truck 卡车
[记] 可数：a service area。BrE motorway services；AmE rest stop / service plaza

**driveway**
英/美 /ˈdraɪvweɪ/
n. 车道；私家车道
[义] A short private road leading from a public road to a house or garage. 从公共道路通至住宅或车库的短私家路（ block the driveway 堵住车道, pull into the driveway 开进车道, long driveway 长车道）。可数。
[例] ① She parked in the driveway rather than on the street. 她把车停在车道上而非街上。 ② Ice made the steep driveway dangerous. 结冰使陡坡车道很危险。
[搭] block the driveway 堵住车道, pull into the driveway 开进车道, long driveway 长 driveway, driveway gate 车道大门
[联] garage 车库, car 汽车, parking 停车, road 道路, house 房屋, vehicle 车辆
[记] 可数：a driveway。drive + way → 供车开进的路

**tow bar**
英/美 /ˈtəʊ bɑː(r)/
n. 拖车杠；拖钩
[义] A metal bar fixed to the back of a car for pulling a trailer or caravan. 固定于车尾、用于拖挂 trailer 或 caravan 的金属杆（ fit a tow bar 装拖杠, tow bar hitch 拖杠连接器, tow bar wiring 拖杠线路）。可数。
[例] ① They fitted a tow bar before buying the caravan. 买拖挂房车前先装了拖杠。 ② The tow bar wiring connects trailer lights to the car. 拖杠线路把拖挂灯连到主车。
[搭] fit a tow bar 装拖杠, tow bar hitch 拖杠连接器, tow bar wiring 拖杠线路, removable tow bar 可拆卸拖杠
[联] trailer 拖车, caravan 拖挂房车, tow truck 清障车, hitch 拖挂连接器, vehicle 车辆, truck 卡车
[记] 可数：a tow bar。亦说 tow hitch

**speedometer**
英/美 /spiːˈdɒmɪtə(r)/
n. 车速表
[义] An instrument in a vehicle that shows how fast it is going. 显示车辆当前速度的仪表（ speedometer reading 车速读数, broken speedometer 损坏的车速表, check the speedometer 看车速表）。可数。
[例] ① The speedometer needle hovered just below the limit. 车速表指针徘徊在限速下方。 ② A faulty speedometer can lead to speeding tickets. 故障车速表可导致超速罚单。
[源] 源自 speed + -meter（测量）→ 测速之器。
[搭] speedometer reading 车速读数, broken speedometer 损坏的车速表, digital speedometer 数字车速表
[联] dashboard 仪表盘, odometer 里程表, driver 司机, speed limit 限速, car 汽车, mph 英里每小时
[记] 可数：a speedometer。speed + odometer 为不同仪表

**odometer**
英/美 /əʊˈdɒmɪtə(r)/
n. 里程表
[义] An instrument in a vehicle that shows the total distance it has travelled. 显示车辆累计行驶距离的仪表（ odometer reading 里程读数, roll back the odometer 调表〔违法〕, digital odometer 数字里程表）。可数。
[例] ① The odometer showed eighty thousand miles. 里程表显示八万英里。 ② Dealers must disclose accurate odometer readings when selling used cars. 卖二手车须如实披露里程。
[源] 源自希腊语 *hodos*（路）+ -meter → 测路程之器。
[搭] odometer reading 里程读数, digital odometer 数字里程表, odometer fraud 调表欺诈
[联] speedometer 车速表, dashboard 仪表盘, mileage 里程, car 汽车, vehicle 车辆, used car 二手车
[记] 可数：an odometer。mileage 为里程数本身，odometer 为仪表

**horsepower**
英/美 /ˈhɔːspaʊə(r)/
n. 马力
[义] A unit for measuring the power of an engine. 衡量引擎功率的单位（ engine horsepower 发动机马力, horsepower rating 马力标定, three hundred horsepower 三百马力）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① The sports car boasts four hundred horsepower. 跑车宣称四百马力。 ② Higher horsepower does not always mean better fuel economy. 马力高未必省油。
[搭] engine horsepower 发动机马力, horsepower rating 马力标定, brake horsepower 制动马力
[联] engine 引擎, motor 马达, car 汽车, performance 性能, acceleration 加速, vehicle 车辆
[记] 不可数/可数。缩写 hp / HP。源自 James Watt 与马匹拉磨对比

**cruise control**
英 /ˈkruːz kənˈtrəʊl/ 美 /ˈkruːz kənˈtroʊl/
n. 定速巡航
[义] A system in a vehicle that keeps it moving at a set speed without the driver pressing the accelerator. 无需驾驶员踩油门、使车辆保持设定速度的系统（ set cruise control 设定巡航, adaptive cruise control 自适应巡航, turn off cruise control 关闭巡航）。不可数。
[例] ① He set cruise control at seventy on the empty motorway. 空荡高速上他把巡航定在七十。 ② Adaptive cruise control slows the car when traffic ahead brakes. 自适应巡航在前车刹车时自动减速。
[搭] set cruise control 设定巡航, adaptive cruise control 自适应巡航, turn off cruise control 关闭巡航, cruise control button 巡航按钮
[联] accelerator 油门, highway 高速公路, motorway 高速公路, driver 司机, car 汽车, speed 速度
[记] 不可数。adaptive cruise control（ACC）为进阶功能

**autopilot**
英/美 /ˈɔːtəʊpaɪlət/
n. 自动驾驶仪； autopilot
[义] A device that steers a plane or boat on a set course without constant manual control; informal use for cars with partial automation. 无需持续人工操控、按设定航向掌舵的装置；亦泛指汽车部分自动化（ on autopilot 自动驾驶中, autopilot mode 自动模式, engage autopilot 启动自动驾驶）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① The pilot engaged autopilot during the long ocean crossing. 长途越洋飞行中飞行员启用自动驾驶。 ② Many drivers rely on lane-keeping as a kind of autopilot on highways. 许多司机在高速上靠车道保持当某种 autopilot。
[搭] on autopilot 自动驾驶中, autopilot mode 自动模式, engage autopilot 启动自动驾驶, autopilot system 自动驾驶系统
[联] pilot 飞行员, aircraft 航空器, cockpit 驾驶舱, cruise control 定速巡航, driver 司机, plane 飞机
[记] 可数/不可数。auto-（自动）+ pilot（驾驶）。口语 on autopilot 喻「机械地、不经心」

**suspension**
英/美 /səˈspenʃn/
n. 悬挂系统
[义] The springs and shock absorbers that connect a vehicle's body to its wheels, smoothing the ride. 连接车身与车轮、缓冲颠簸的弹簧与减震器系统（ suspension system 悬挂系统, rear suspension 后悬挂, suspension failure 悬挂故障）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① The pothole damaged the car's front suspension. 坑洼损坏了车前悬挂。 ② Off-road vehicles need tougher suspension than sedans. 越野车悬挂须比轿车更结实。
[搭] suspension system 悬挂系统, rear suspension 后悬挂, suspension failure 悬挂故障, air suspension 空气悬挂
[联] wheel 轮子, axle 车轴, chassis 底盘, shock absorber 减震器, car 汽车, off-road 越野
[记] 不可数/可数。suspension 亦指「暂停、停职」，语境区分

**fender**
英/美 /ˈfendə(r)/
n. 挡泥板；翼子板
[义] The panel over each wheel on a car or bicycle (AmE); the bumper on a car (BrE, 美式 bumper). 汽车或自行车每个车轮上方的挡板（美式）；〔英式〕保险杠。可数。
[例] ① A cyclist clipped the fender of a parked car. 骑行者刮到停放车辆的翼子板。 ② Rust spread from the fender into the door frame. 锈从挡泥板蔓延到门框。
[搭] front fender 前翼子板, rear fender 后翼子板, fender bender 小刮蹭〔口语〕, dented fender 凹痕挡泥板
[联] bumper 保险杠, wheel 轮子, car 汽车, bicycle 自行车, collision 碰撞, repair 维修
[记] 可数：a fender。AmE fender = 翼子板；BrE fender = bumper，勿混

**wing mirror**
英 /ˈwɪŋ mɪrə(r)/ 美 /ˈwɪŋ mɪrər/
n. 侧后视镜
[义] A mirror mounted on the wing or door of a car (BrE). 装在翼子板或车门上的后视镜（英式）（ adjust the wing mirrors 调侧镜, wing mirror glass 侧镜镜片, fold the wing mirrors 折叠侧镜）。可数。美式 side mirror。
[例] ① Check both wing mirrors before reversing out of the drive. 倒出车道前看两侧镜。 ② The wing mirror was knocked off in the car park. 侧镜在停车场被撞掉了。
[搭] adjust the wing mirrors 调侧镜, wing mirror glass 侧镜镜片, fold the wing mirrors 折叠侧镜, replace a wing mirror 更换侧镜
[联] side mirror 侧后视镜, rearview mirror 后视镜, car 汽车, driver 司机, blind spot 盲区
[记] 可数：a wing mirror。BrE wing mirror，AmE side mirror

**fuel tank**
英 /ˈfjuːl tæŋk/ 美 /ˈfjuːl tæŋk/
n. 油箱
[义] A container in a vehicle that holds fuel for the engine. 车辆中储存引擎燃料的容器（ full fuel tank 满油箱, fuel tank capacity 油箱容量, empty fuel tank 空油箱）。可数。
[例] ① The fuel tank holds about sixty litres. 油箱约容六十升。 ② A leak in the fuel tank forced them to stop immediately. 油箱泄漏迫使他们立即停车。
[搭] full fuel tank 满油箱, fuel tank capacity 油箱容量, empty fuel tank 空油箱, fuel tank cap 油箱盖
[联] fuel 燃料, petrol 汽油, gasoline 汽油, engine 引擎, petrol cap 油箱盖, car 汽车
[记] 可数：a fuel tank。plane 亦有 fuel tank；大小不同

**petrol cap**
英 /ˈpetrəl kæp/ 美 /ˈpetrəl kæp/
n. 油箱盖
[义] The cap that closes the opening where petrol is put into a car (BrE). 封闭汽车加油口的盖子（英式）（ unscrew the petrol cap 拧开油箱盖, petrol cap seal 油箱盖密封, missing petrol cap 丢失的油箱盖）。可数。美式 gas cap / fuel cap。
[例] ① He forgot to screw the petrol cap back on after filling up. 加完油他忘了拧回油箱盖。 ② A loose petrol cap can trigger a warning light on the dashboard. 油箱盖松动可触发仪表警示灯。
[搭] unscrew the petrol cap 拧开油箱盖, petrol cap seal 油箱盖密封, replace the petrol cap 更换油箱盖
[联] fuel tank 油箱, petrol 汽油, petrol station 加油站, car 汽车, fuel 燃料
[记] 可数：a petrol cap。BrE petrol cap，AmE gas cap

**catalytic converter**
英 /ˌkætəlɪtɪk kənˈvɜːtə(r)/ 美 /ˌkætəlɪtɪk kənˈvɜːrtər/
n. 催化转化器
[义] A device in a vehicle's exhaust system that reduces harmful emissions. 车辆排气系统中降低有害排放的装置（ stolen catalytic converter 被盗三元催化, catalytic converter theft 催化器盗窃, replace a catalytic converter 更换催化器）。可数。
[例] ① Thieves target catalytic converters for the metals inside. 窃贼盯上催化器内的贵金属。 ② The car failed the emissions test because of a faulty catalytic converter. 催化器故障导致尾气检测不合格。
[搭] stolen catalytic converter 被盗三元催化, catalytic converter theft 催化器盗窃, replace a catalytic converter 更换催化器
[联] exhaust 排气管, emission 排放, pollution 污染, engine 引擎, vehicle 车辆, mechanic 机修工
[记] 可数：a catalytic converter。近年盗窃案新闻高频词

**tailpipe**
英/美 /ˈteɪlpaɪp/
n. 尾排气管
[义] The pipe at the back of a vehicle through which exhaust gases exit. 车辆后部排出废气的管道（ tailpipe emission 尾气排放, tailpipe smoke 尾烟, rusted tailpipe 锈蚀尾管）。可数。
[例] ① Black smoke poured from the truck's tailpipe. 卡车尾管冒出黑烟。 ② The mechanic welded a patch onto the broken tailpipe. 机修工在破损尾管上打了补丁。
[搭] tailpipe emission 尾气排放, tailpipe smoke 尾烟, rusted tailpipe 锈蚀尾管, dual tailpipes 双尾管
[联] exhaust 排气管, muffler 消音器, emission 排放, car 汽车, pollution 污染, engine 引擎
[记] 可数：a tailpipe。tail + pipe → 尾部排气管

**radiator**
英/美 /ˈreɪdieɪtə(r)/
n. 散热器；暖气片
[义] The device in a vehicle that cools the engine by passing liquid through fins; a room heater (另一义). 液体流经散热片为引擎降温的装置；室内暖气片。可数。
[例] ① The radiator leaked and the engine overheated. 散热器漏水导致引擎过热。 ② Steam rose from the radiator cap when he opened it too soon. 过早打开时散热盖冒蒸汽。
[搭] car radiator 汽车散热器, radiator leak 散热器漏水, radiator cap 散热盖, radiator fan 散热风扇
[联] engine 引擎, coolant 冷却液, overheating 过热, mechanic 机修工, car 汽车, heat 热
[记] 可数：a radiator。radiator 亦指「暖气片」，靠语境区分

**battery**
英/美 /ˈbætəri/
n. 电池；电瓶
[义] The rechargeable unit in a car that supplies power to start the engine and run electrical systems. 汽车中供启动引擎与电气系统供电的可充电单元（ car battery 汽车电瓶, flat battery 没电的电瓶, jump-start a battery 搭电启动）。可数。
[例] ① The car would not start because of a flat battery. 电瓶没电车打不着火。 ② Cold weather shortens battery life in older vehicles. 严寒缩短旧车电瓶寿命。
[搭] car battery 汽车电瓶, flat battery 没电的电瓶, jump-start a battery 搭电启动, battery terminal 电瓶接线柱
[联] engine 引擎, ignition 点火, electric 电动的, vehicle 车辆, charger 充电器, mechanic 机修工
[记] 可数：a battery。battery 亦指「电池组、炮组」，语境区分

**mudguard**
英/美 /ˈmʌdɡɑːd/
n. 挡泥板
[义] A curved cover over a wheel that stops mud and water from being thrown up (BrE, especially on bicycles and motorcycles). 罩在车轮上方、防止泥水溅起的弧形盖（英式，尤指自行车与摩托车）（ bicycle mudguard 自行车挡泥板, front mudguard 前挡泥板, missing mudguard 缺失挡泥板）。可数。
[例] ① The mudguard snapped after hitting a pothole. 过坑后挡泥板断了。 ② Commuter bikes often have full-length mudguards for rainy rides. 通勤车常装全长挡泥板防雨。
[搭] bicycle mudguard 自行车挡泥板, front mudguard 前挡泥板, rear mudguard 后挡泥板, clip-on mudguard 夹式挡泥板
[联] fender 挡泥板, bicycle 自行车, wheel 轮子, rain 雨, motorcycle 摩托车
[记] 可数：a mudguard。BrE mudguard；AmE fender（汽车/自行车）

**dining car**
英/美 /ˈdaɪnɪŋ kɑː(r)/
n. 餐车
[义] A railway carriage where meals are served to passengers during a journey. 旅途中为乘客供餐的铁路车厢（ dining car service 餐车服务, restaurant dining car 餐厅餐车, visit the dining car 去餐车用餐）。可数。亦说 restaurant car（英式）。
[例] ① We booked a table in the dining car for dinner. 我们在餐车订了晚餐桌位。 ② The dining car closed early on the delayed overnight train. 误点的夜车餐车提早打烊。
[搭] dining car service 餐车服务, restaurant dining car 餐厅餐车, visit the dining car 去餐车用餐, dining car menu 餐车菜单
[联] carriage 车厢, train 火车, sleeper 卧铺, passenger 乘客, meal 餐食, ticket 车票
[记] 可数：a dining car。BrE restaurant car 同义；长途列车常见

**signal**
英/美 /ˈsɪɡnəl/
n. 信号；信号灯
v. 发信号
[义] A light, flag, or electronic message that controls train or road traffic; to give such a message. 控制铁路或道路交通的灯、旗或电子指示；发出此类指示（ traffic signal 交通信号, railway signal 铁路信号, turn signal 转向灯）。可数/动词。
[例] ① The train stopped at a red signal ahead. 列车在前方红灯信号处停下。 ② He forgot to use his turn signal before changing lanes. 变道前他忘了打转向灯。
[搭] traffic signal 交通信号, railway signal 铁路信号, turn signal 转向灯, signal failure 信号故障
[联] traffic light 红绿灯, train 火车, road 道路, driver 司机, intersection 交叉路口, platform 站台
[记] 可数/动词。signal 亦指「信号、征兆」，交通义靠语境

**track**
英/美 /træk/
n. 轨道；跑道
[义] One of the two metal lines that a train runs on; a prepared path for racing or walking. 火车行驶的两条金属线之一；赛跑或步行道（ railway track 铁轨, single track 单线, off the track 出轨〔喻〕）。可数。
[例] ① Leaves on the track delayed morning services. 轨道上的落叶延误了早班服务。 ② Workers inspected the track after the heavy storm. 暴风雨后工人检查轨道。
[搭] railway track 铁轨, single track 单线, track maintenance 轨道维护, on the wrong track 走错轨〔喻〕
[联] rail 铁轨, train 火车, railway 铁路, locomotive 机车, platform 站台, subway 地铁
[记] 可数：a track。track vs. rail：track 指整段线路/轨条，rail 常指钢轨

**turbine**
英/美 /ˈtɜːbaɪn/
n. 涡轮；涡轮机
[义] An engine or motor driven by a flow of gas or liquid, used in jet aircraft and some ships. 靠气流或液流驱动的引擎，用于喷气式飞机与部分船舶（ jet turbine 喷气涡轮, gas turbine 燃气轮机, turbine engine 涡轮发动机）。可数。
[例] ① The jet's turbines roared as it accelerated for takeoff. 喷气机加速起飞时涡轮轰鸣。 ② Ships with gas turbines can reach high speeds. 燃气轮机船舶可达高速。
[源] 源自拉丁语 *turbo*（旋转）→ 旋转驱动之物。
[族] turbocharger 涡轮增压, turbulent 湍流的
[搭] jet turbine 喷气涡轮, gas turbine 燃气轮机, turbine engine 涡轮发动机, wind turbine 风力涡轮机
[联] jet 喷气机, engine 引擎, aircraft 航空器, propeller 螺旋桨, ship 大船, power 动力
[记] 可数：a turbine。wind turbine 属能源设施，亦常见

**wing**
英/美 /wɪŋ/
n. 机翼；翅膀
[义] Either of the long flat parts on an aircraft that keep it in the air. 飞机两侧保持升力的长平翼（ aircraft wing 机翼, wing tip 翼尖, wing span 翼展）。可数。亦指鸟翼、建筑侧翼。
[例] ① Ice formed on the wing during the storm. 暴风雨中机翼结冰。 ② The wing was damaged when the plane clipped a hangar door. 飞机刮机库门时翼受损。
[搭] aircraft wing 机翼, wing tip 翼尖, wing span 翼展, left wing 左翼, wing design 翼型设计
[联] airplane 飞机, aircraft 航空器, fuselage 机身, pilot 飞行员, flight 飞行, tail 尾翼
[记] 可数：a wing。wing mirror 因装在翼子板得名

**pier**
英/美 /pɪə(r)/
n. 码头； pier
[义] A platform built out over water where boats can dock and people can walk. 伸入水上、供船只停靠与行人行走的平台（ fishing pier 钓鱼码头, pier parking 码头停车, walk along the pier 沿码头散步）。可数。
[例] ① Fishermen gathered at the end of the pier at dawn. 黎明时渔民聚在码头尽头。 ② The ferry ties up at the pier twice a day. 渡轮每天两次靠码头。
[搭] fishing pier 钓鱼码头, pier parking 码头停车, walk along the pier 沿码头散步, seaside pier 海滨码头
[联] wharf 码头, dock 码头, harbour 港湾, boat 小船, ferry 渡轮, marina 游艇码头
[记] 可数：a pier。pier 亦指「桥墩、支柱」，语境区分

**wharf**
英/美 /wɔːf/
n. 码头； wharf
[义] A flat platform beside water where ships load and unload goods. 水边供船舶装卸货物的平台（ cargo wharf 货运码头, wharf worker 码头工人, at the wharf 在码头）。可数。复数 wharves /wɔːvz/ 或 wharfs。
[例] ① Cranes lined the wharf to unload containers. 起重机排列码头卸集装箱。 ② Wharf workers finished the shift at midnight. 码头工人午夜交班。
[搭] cargo wharf 货运码头, wharf worker 码头工人, at the wharf 在码头, wharf area 码头区
[联] dock 码头, port 港口, pier 码头, cargo 货物, ship 大船, crane 起重机
[记] 可数：a wharf。复数 wharves。wharf 偏货运装卸

**quay**
英/美 /kiː/
n. 码头；岸壁
[义] A platform in a harbour where boats come alongside to load and unload.  harbour 中供船只靠岸装卸的平台（ loading quay 装卸岸壁, quay side 码头边, along the quay 沿岸壁）。可数。
[例] ① Cafés line the quay where yachts moor in summer. 夏天游艇停泊的岸壁旁排满咖啡馆。 ② Trucks queued along the quay waiting to load the ship. 卡车沿岸壁排队等装船。
[搭] loading quay 装卸岸壁, quay side 码头边, along the quay 沿岸壁, quay wall 岸壁
[联] harbour 港湾, dock 码头, wharf 码头, ship 大船, moor 停泊, cargo 货物
[记] 可数：a quay。发音 /kiː/。英式 harbour 常用

**berth**
英/美 /bɜːθ/
n. 泊位；卧铺
[义] A place where a ship can moor safely; a sleeping place on a train or ship. 船舶可安全停泊的位置；火车或船上的铺位（ take a berth 占泊位, berth booking 铺位预订, narrow berth 窄泊位）。可数。
[例] ① The captain steered the ship into its berth at dawn. 船长黎明时将船驶入泊位。 ② We booked a lower berth on the overnight ferry. 我们在夜渡上订了下铺。
[搭] take a berth 占泊位, berth booking 铺位预订, ship's berth 船泊位, sleeping berth 卧铺
[联] harbour 港湾, dock 码头, moor 停泊, ship 大船, sleeper 卧铺, ferry 渡轮
[记] 可数：a berth。give a wide berth 喻「远远避开」

**moor**
英/美 /mʊə(r)/
v. 停泊；系泊
[义] To attach a boat to the shore or to anchors so that it stays in place. 将船系于岸或锚，使其固定（ moor a yacht 停泊游艇, mooring rope 系泊缆, moor alongside 靠泊）。动词。
[例] ① They moored the boat in the sheltered bay overnight. 他们在避风湾过夜停泊。 ② Yachts were moored three deep along the marina wall. 游艇在 marina 墙边三层系泊。
[搭] moor a yacht 停泊游艇, mooring rope 系泊缆, moor alongside 靠泊, mooring buoy 系泊浮标
[联] anchor 锚, harbour 港湾, marina 游艇码头, yacht 游艇, boat 小船, berth 泊位
[记] moor / moored / mooring。mooring 亦作名词「系泊处、系泊费」

**dual carriageway**
英 /ˌdjuːəl ˈkærɪdʒweɪ/ 美 /ˌduːəl ˈkærɪdʒweɪ/
n. 双向分隔车行道
[义] A road with separate streams of traffic in each direction, divided by a central strip (BrE). 双向车流由中央带分隔的公路（英式）（ major dual carriageway 主干分隔路, dual carriageway limit 分隔路限速, join a dual carriageway 驶入分隔路）。可数。
[例] ① Speed limits are higher on dual carriageways than on single-lane roads. 分隔路限速高于单车道路。 ② The dual carriageway bypasses three villages. 分隔路绕过三个村庄。
[搭] major dual carriageway 主干分隔路, dual carriageway limit 分隔路限速, join a dual carriageway 驶入分隔路
[联] motorway 高速公路, highway 公路, lane 车道, traffic 交通, bypass 旁路, road 道路
[记] 可数：a dual carriageway。BrE 术语；近似 AmE divided highway

**sidewalk**
英/美 /ˈsaɪdwɔːk/
n. 人行道
[义] A paved path for pedestrians at the side of a road (AmE). 路边供行人行走的铺装通道（美式）（ sidewalk café 路边咖啡座, on the sidewalk 在人行道上, sidewalk parking 占用人行道停车）。可数。英式 pavement。
[例] ① Cyclists are not allowed to ride on the sidewalk. 不允许在人行道上骑车。 ② Snow blocked the sidewalk after the blizzard. 暴雪后雪堵人行道。
[搭] sidewalk café 路边咖啡座, on the sidewalk 在人行道上, sidewalk parking 占用人行道停车, clear the sidewalk 清理人行道

[记] 可数：a sidewalk。AmE sidewalk，BrE pavement

**pavement**
英/美 /ˈpeɪvmənt/
n. 人行道；路面
[义] A hard surface at the side of a road for pedestrians (BrE); the surface of a road (AmE). 路边供行人走的硬质路面（英式）；〔美式〕路面。可数/不可数。
[例] ① Parents warned children not to play near the pavement edge. 家长告诫孩子别在人行道边玩耍。 ② Ice made the pavement slippery for commuters. 结冰使通勤者走人行道很滑。
[搭] on the pavement 在人行道上, pavement edge 人行道边缘, slip on the pavement 在人行道上滑倒
[联] sidewalk 人行道, pedestrian 行人, street 街道, road 道路, cyclist 骑行者, crosswalk 人行横道
[记] BrE pavement = 人行道；AmE pavement = 路面。与「铺路石」义重叠

**bus lane**
英/美 /ˈbʌs leɪn/
n. 公交专用道
[义] A lane on a road reserved for buses, often during peak hours. 道路上专供公共汽车使用的车道，常限高峰时段（ bus lane camera 公交道摄像头, drive in a bus lane 占用公交道, bus lane hours 专用时段）。可数。
[例] ① Fines are heavy for driving in the bus lane during rush hour. 高峰占用公交专用道罚款很重。 ② The new bus lane cut journey times by ten minutes. 新公交道缩短行程十分钟。
[搭] bus lane camera 公交道摄像头, drive in a bus lane 占用公交道, bus lane hours 专用时段, dedicated bus lane 专用公交道

[记] 可数：a bus lane。亦可能有 taxi/cycle 共用时段，看当地标志

**cycle lane**
英/美 /ˈsaɪkl leɪn/
n. 自行车道
[义] A part of a road marked for use by cyclists only. 道路上标为仅骑行者使用的部分（ protected cycle lane 隔离自行车道, cycle lane marking 自行车道标线, share a cycle lane 共用自行车道）。可数。亦说 bike lane。
[例] ① The city painted new cycle lanes on major streets. 市里在主街划了新自行车道。 ② Motorists must not park in the cycle lane. 机动车不得占用自行车道停车。
[搭] protected cycle lane 隔离自行车道, cycle lane marking 自行车道标线, dedicated cycle lane 专用自行车道
[联] bicycle 自行车, cyclist 骑行者, lane 车道, traffic 交通, road 道路, pedestrian 行人
[记] 可数：a cycle lane。bike lane 同义；protected cycle lane 有物理隔离

**sailor**
英/美 /ˈseɪlə(r)/
n. 水手；海员
[义] A person who works on a ship, especially one who is not an officer. 在船上工作的人员，尤指非军官船员（ experienced sailor 老水手, sailor's knot 水手结, merchant sailor 商船水手）。可数。
[例] ① Sailors secured the cargo before the storm hit. 风暴来临前水手固定货物。 ② She became a sailor after leaving the navy. 退役后她当了商船海员。
[搭] experienced sailor 老水手, sailor's knot 水手结, merchant sailor 商船水手, fellow sailor 同船水手
[联] ship 大船, captain 船长, crew 船员, harbour 港湾, navy 海军, vessel 船只
[记] 可数：a sailor。sail（帆）+ -or → 与航海相关的人

**crew**
英/美 /kruː/
n. 全体船员；机组
[义] All the people working on a ship, aircraft, or train together. 同船、同机或同列车上共同工作的全体人员（ flight crew 机组, ship's crew 船员, cabin crew 客舱乘务员）。可数/集合名词。
[例] ① The crew prepared the cabin for landing. 机组为着陆准备客舱。 ② A skeleton crew kept the ferry running through the storm. 最低配员让渡轮在风暴中继续运营。
[搭] flight crew 机组, ship's crew 船员, cabin crew 客舱乘务员, crew member 机组成员
[联] pilot 飞行员, captain 船长, sailor 水手, passenger 乘客, aircraft 航空器, ship 大船
[记] 可数/集合名词。a crew of ten。crew 亦指「剧组、施工队」

**rudder**
英/美 /ˈrʌdə(r)/
n. 舵
[义] A flat piece at the back of a boat or aircraft that is turned to change direction. 船或飞机尾部用以转向的 flat 板（ ship's rudder 船舵, rudder control 舵控, lose the rudder 失去舵控）。可数。
[例] ① The helmsman turned the rudder to avoid the rocks. 舵手转舵避礁。 ② Without a rudder, the boat drifted with the current. 无舵时船随流漂。
[搭] ship's rudder 船舵, rudder control 舵控, rudder failure 舵故障, turn the rudder 转舵
[联] sail 帆, boat 小船, ship 大船, steering 转向, helm 舵柄, captain 船长
[记] 可数：a rudder。飞机亦有 rudder；steering wheel 管 car

**mast**
英/美 /mɑːst/
n. 桅杆
[义] A tall pole on a boat that supports the sails or antenna. 船上支撑帆或天线的 tall pole（ main mast 主桅, mast height 桅高, climb the mast 爬桅）。可数。
[例] ① The sail caught the wind as it rose up the mast. 帆升上桅杆迎风鼓起。 ② Lightning struck the mast during the squall. 飑线中闪电击中桅杆。
[搭] main mast 主桅, mast height 桅高, climb the mast 爬桅, mast top 桅顶
[联] sail 帆, sailboat 帆船, yacht 游艇, rope 绳索, ship 大船, wind 风
[记] 可数：a mast。mast 亦指「旗杆、天线杆」

**deck**
英/美 /dek/
n. 甲板；层面
[义] One of the floors on a ship; the open area where passengers walk on a ferry. 船上的一层 floor；渡轮上乘客行走的 open 区域（ upper deck 上层甲板, on deck 在甲板上, flight deck 飞行甲板〔航母〕）。可数。
[例] ① Passengers stood on deck to watch the coastline. 乘客站在甲板上看海岸线。 ② Crew cleaned the lower deck before arrival. 抵达前船员清理下层甲板。
[搭] upper deck 上层甲板, on deck 在甲板上, flight deck 飞行甲板, deck chair 躺椅
[联] ship 大船, ferry 渡轮, sailor 水手, captain 船长, cabin 舱室, mast 桅杆
[记] 可数：a deck。all hands on deck 喻「全体出动」

**oar**
英/美 /ɔː(r)/
n. 桨
[义] A long pole with a flat blade, used to row a boat. 带 flat 桨叶、用于划船的长杆（ row with oars 用桨划, pull an oar 划桨, oar lock 桨架）。可数。常用复数 oars。
[例] ① They pulled hard on the oars against the current. 他们逆流用力划桨。 ② One oar slipped and the dinghy spun around. 一支桨滑脱，小艇打转。
[搭] row with oars 用桨划, pull an oar 划桨, oar lock 桨架, pair of oars 一对桨
[联] paddle 桨, row 划船, boat 小船, dinghy 小艇, canoe 独木舟, sail 帆
[记] 可数，常用复数 oars。oar vs. paddle：oar 用于 rowboat，paddle 用于 canoe/kayak

**paddle**
英/美 /ˈpædl/
n. 桨； paddles
v. 划桨；涉水
[义] A short pole with a blade on both ends or one end, used to move a canoe or kayak. 单头或双头带桨叶、划 canoe/kayak 的短杆（ kayak paddle 双叶桨, paddle stroke 划桨动作, double paddle 双头桨）。可数/动词。
[例] ① She dipped the paddle silently into the still lake. 她把桨静静插入平静湖面。 ② Always secure the spare paddle to the kayak. 务必将备用桨固定于 kayak。
[搭] kayak paddle 双叶桨, paddle stroke 划桨动作, double paddle 双头桨, spare paddle 备用桨
[联] oar 桨, canoe 独木舟, kayak 皮划艇, boat 小船, row 划船, river 河
[记] 可数/动词。paddle 亦指「蹚水、乒乓球拍」

**mileage**
英/美 /ˈmaɪlɪdʒ/
n. 里程；英里数
[义] The number of miles a vehicle has travelled; distance travelled per unit of fuel. 车辆已行驶的英里数；单位燃料行驶距离（ high mileage 高里程, mileage rate 里程费率, good mileage 省油）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① The used car had low mileage for its age. 这辆二手车里程与其车龄相比很低。 ② Hybrid engines often deliver better mileage in city traffic. 混动车在市区往往更省油。
[搭] high mileage 高里程, low mileage 低里程, good mileage 省油, mileage rate 里程费率
[联] odometer 里程表, car 汽车, fuel 燃料, hybrid 混动车, journey 行程, distance 距离
[记] 不可数/可数。mileage 亦指「好处、利用价值」（get mileage from）

**trolley**
英/美 /ˈtrɒli/
n. 电车；手推车
[义] A bus powered by electricity from overhead wires (AmE); a small cart on wheels. 靠架空线供电的巴士（美式）；带轮小 cart（ shopping trolley 购物手推车〔英式〕, airport trolley 机场行李车）。可数。
[例] ① We took the trolley downtown to avoid parking. 我们乘电车进城以省停车。 ② San Francisco's cable cars and trolleys are tourist icons. 旧金山缆索车与电车是旅游标志。
[搭] shopping trolley 购物手推车, airport trolley 机场行李车, trolley bus 无轨电车, take the trolley 乘电车
[联] tram 有轨电车, streetcar 有轨电车, bus 公共汽车, public transport 公共交通, cart 手推车
[记] 可数：a trolley。AmE trolley 常指 streetcar；BrE trolley = 手推车

**rickshaw**
英/美 /ˈrɪkʃɔː/
n. 人力车； rickshaw
[义] A light vehicle pulled by a person on foot or by bicycle, used especially in parts of Asia. 由人拉或自行车驱动、尤见于亚洲部分地区的轻型车（ cycle rickshaw 三轮车, auto rickshaw 机动三轮, rickshaw puller 拉车人）。可数。
[例] ① Tourists negotiated a fare for a short rickshaw ride. 游客议价短途坐人力车。 ② Auto rickshaws weave through traffic in the old quarter. 机动三轮在老城区车流中穿梭。
[搭] cycle rickshaw 三轮车, auto rickshaw 机动三轮, rickshaw puller 拉车人, rickshaw ride 坐人力车
[联] taxi 出租车, bicycle 自行车, traffic 交通, fare 车费, passenger 乘客, Asia 亚洲
[记] 可数：a rickshaw。auto rickshaw 在南亚极常见

**hot-air balloon**
英 /ˌhɒt eə bəˈluːn/ 美 /ˌhɑːt er bəˈluːn/
n. 热气球
[义] A large balloon filled with hot air that carries a basket for passengers. 充热空气、下挂载人吊篮的大型气球（ hot-air balloon ride 热气球之旅, hot-air balloon festival 热气球节, launch a hot-air balloon 放热气球）。可数。
[例] ① They watched dozens of hot-air balloons rise at sunrise. 他们看数十热气球日出升空。 ② A hot-air balloon drifted silently over the valley. 热气球静静飘过山谷。
[搭] hot-air balloon ride 热气球之旅, hot-air balloon festival 热气球节, launch a hot-air balloon 放热气球
[联] aircraft 航空器, flight 飞行, pilot 飞行员, basket 吊篮, wind 风, festival 节日
[记] 可数：a hot-air balloon。与 gas balloon（氢/氦）不同

**skateboard**
英/美 /ˈskeɪtbɔːd/
n. 滑板
v. 玩滑板
[义] A short narrow board with wheels at each end, ridden standing up. 两端装轮、站立滑行的短窄板（ ride a skateboard 玩滑板, skateboard park 滑板公园, electric skateboard 电动滑板）。可数/动词。
[例] ① Teenagers skated along the waterfront on skateboards. 少年沿滨水区滑滑板。 ② The city built a skateboard park under the bridge. 市里在桥下建了滑板公园。
[搭] ride a skateboard 玩滑板, skateboard park 滑板公园, electric skateboard 电动滑板, skateboard ramp 滑板坡道
[联] scooter 滑板车, bicycle 自行车, wheel 轮子, helmet 头盔, pavement 人行道, traffic 交通
[记] 可数/动词。skateboard vs. scooter：后者常带把手或更小

**four-wheel drive**
英 /ˌfɔː wiːl ˈdraɪv/ 美 /ˌfɔːr wiːl ˈdraɪv/
n. 四轮驱动
[义] A system that sends power to all four wheels of a vehicle for better grip on rough ground. 向四个车轮同时传动力、便于坏路抓地的系统（ four-wheel-drive vehicle 四驱车, switch to four-wheel drive 切换四驱, four-wheel-drive mode 四驱模式）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① Switch to four-wheel drive before climbing the muddy track. 上泥路前切换四驱。 ② Four-wheel drive helps on snow but does not replace winter tyres. 四驱助行雪地但不替代冬季胎。
[搭] four-wheel-drive vehicle 四驱车, switch to four-wheel drive 切换四驱, four-wheel-drive mode 四驱模式
[联] SUV 越野车, jeep 吉普, off-road 越野, wheel 轮子, traction 牵引, mud 泥
[记] 不可数/可数。缩写 4WD；AWD（all-wheel drive）常指常时四驱

**ATV**
英 /ˌeɪ tiː ˈviː/ 美 /ˌeɪ tiː ˈviː/
n. 全地形车
[义] A small open motor vehicle with four thick tyres, designed for rough ground (all-terrain vehicle). 四轮粗胎、适应坏路的小型开放机动车（ ride an ATV 骑全地形车, ATV trail 全地形车道, ATV rental 租赁）。可数。
[例] ① Rangers patrolled the forest on ATVs. 护林员骑全地形车巡逻森林。 ② ATV accidents rise when riders skip helmets. 不戴头盔时全地形车事故上升。
[搭] ride an ATV 骑全地形车, ATV trail 全地形车道, ATV rental 租赁, ATV helmet 全地形车头盔
[联] four-wheel drive 四驱, jeep 吉普, off-road 越野, motorcycle 摩托车, helmet 头盔, trail 小径
[记] 可数：an ATV。读字母 A-T-V；全称 all-terrain vehicle

**hitch**
英/美 /hɪtʃ/
n. 拖挂装置
v. 挂接
[义] A device that connects a trailer to a vehicle; to connect them. 连接拖挂与车辆的装置；挂接（ trailer hitch 拖挂器, hitch a trailer 挂拖挂, hitch pin 连接销）。可数/动词。
[例] ① He backed the car to align the hitch with the trailer. 他倒车对准拖挂与 trailer 连接器。 ② Make sure the hitch is locked before motorway driving. 上高速前确认拖挂已锁好。
[搭] trailer hitch 拖挂器, hitch a trailer 挂拖挂, hitch pin 连接销, tow hitch 拖钩
[联] tow bar 拖车杠, trailer 拖车, caravan 拖挂房车, vehicle 车辆, truck 卡车
[记] 可数/动词。hitch 亦指「 hitchhike 搭便车、小故障」

**congestion**
英/美 /kənˈdʒestʃən/
n. 拥堵
[义] The state of too much traffic moving slowly or not at all on roads. 道路上车辆过多、缓行或停滞的状态（ traffic congestion 交通拥堵, congestion charge 拥堵费, ease congestion 缓解拥堵）。不可数。
[例] ① Congestion worsens every year on the approach to the city. 进城方向拥堵逐年加剧。 ② London's congestion charge reduced traffic in the centre. 伦敦拥堵费减少了市中心车流。
[搭] traffic congestion 交通拥堵, congestion charge 拥堵费, ease congestion 缓解拥堵, congestion pricing 拥堵定价
[联] traffic jam 堵车, gridlock 交通瘫痪, traffic 交通, commute 通勤, road 道路, rush hour 高峰
[记] 不可数。con-（共同）+ gest（携带）→ 挤在一起

**milepost**
英/美 /ˈmaɪlpəʊst/
n. 里程标；里程碑
[义] A post beside a road or railway showing the distance in miles from a fixed point. 公路或铁路旁标示距某固定点英里数的桩（ highway milepost 公路里程标, milepost marker 里程标记, pass milepost 经过某里程）。可数。
[例] ① Mileposts along the highway helped them estimate arrival time. 公路里程标帮他们估算到达时间。 ② The railway still uses stone mileposts on the scenic line. 风景线铁路仍用石制里程标。
[搭] highway milepost 公路里程标, milepost marker 里程标记, railway milepost 铁路里程标
[联] mileage 里程, highway 公路, railway 铁路, road 道路, distance 距离, sign 路标
[记] 可数：a milepost。milepost 亦喻「里程碑」，交通义为本义

**shock absorber**
英/美 /ˈʃɒk əbˌzɔːbə(r)/
n. 减震器
[义] A device that reduces the bouncing of a vehicle's springs when it hits bumps. 减少车辆过坎时弹簧弹跳的设备（ front shock absorber 前减震, worn shock absorber 磨损减震器, replace shock absorbers 更换减震）。可数。
[例] ① Worn shock absorbers make the ride feel bouncy on rough roads. 减震器磨损会在坏路上颠得厉害。 ② The garage replaced all four shock absorbers during the service. 保养时修理厂换了四个减震器。
[搭] front shock absorber 前减震, worn shock absorber 磨损减震器, replace shock absorbers 更换减震, shock absorber mount 减震器支架
[联] suspension 悬挂, spring 弹簧, wheel 轮子, chassis 底盘, car 汽车, mechanic 机修工
[记] 可数：a shock absorber。口语 shocks 指减震器（复数）

**transmission**
英/美 /trænzˈmɪʃn/
n. 变速箱；传动装置
[义] The system that passes power from a vehicle's engine to its wheels; the gears and parts that do this. 将引擎动力传至车轮的系统及相关部件（ automatic transmission 自动变速箱, transmission fluid 变速箱油, transmission failure 变速箱故障）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① The car shuddered when the transmission changed gear. 换挡时车身一抖。 ② Check the transmission fluid level every few months. 每隔几个月检查变速箱油位。
[搭] automatic transmission 自动变速箱, manual transmission 手动变速箱, transmission fluid 变速箱油, transmission failure 变速箱故障
[联] gearbox 变速箱, engine 引擎, clutch 离合器, wheel 轮子, car 汽车, mechanic 机修工
[记] 不可数/可数。AmE transmission ≈ BrE gearbox（手动/自动语境下）

**differential**
英/美 /ˌdɪfəˈrenʃl/
n. 差速器
[义] A set of gears that allows the wheels on the same axle to turn at different speeds when cornering. 使同一车轴上两轮转弯时转速可不同的齿轮组（ rear differential 后差速, differential oil 差速器油, limited-slip differential 限滑差速）。可数。
[例] ① A whining noise from the rear differential worried the mechanic. 后差速器异响让机修工担心。 ② Four-wheel-drive systems often include front and rear differentials. 四驱系统常含前后差速器。
[搭] rear differential 后差速, differential oil 差速器油, limited-slip differential 限滑差速, differential repair 差速器维修
[联] axle 车轴, wheel 轮子, four-wheel drive 四驱, transmission 变速箱, car 汽车, mechanic 机修工
[记] 可数：a differential。differential 亦指「差别、微分」，语境区分

**spoiler**
英/美 /ˈspɔɪlə(r)/
n. 扰流板
[义] A shaped panel on a car that reduces lift or improves stability at speed. 车身上用以减升力或提高高速稳定性的整流板（ rear spoiler 尾翼, front spoiler 前唇, factory spoiler 原厂尾翼）。可数。
[例] ① The sports model comes with a small rear spoiler as standard. 运动款标配小尾翼。 ② A loose spoiler rattled on the motorway. 尾翼在高速上松脱作响。
[搭] rear spoiler 尾翼, front spoiler 前唇, factory spoiler 原厂尾翼, carbon spoiler 碳纤维尾翼
[联] car 汽车, aerodynamics 空气动力学, sedan 轿车, racing 赛车, vehicle 车辆
[记] 可数：a spoiler。spoiler 亦指「剧透」，完全不同义

**handbrake**
英/美 /ˈhændbreɪk/
n. 手刹；驻车制动
[义] A brake in a vehicle operated by hand to keep it stationary when parked (BrE). 停车时用手操作的制动器（英式）（ pull up the handbrake 拉手刹, handbrake cable 手刹拉线, leave the handbrake on 手刹未松）。可数。美式 parking brake。
[例] ① He forgot to release the handbrake and smelled burning rubber. 他忘了松手刹，闻到橡胶糊味。 ② Pull the handbrake before leaving the car on the hill. 坡道停车前请拉手刹。
[搭] pull up the handbrake 拉手刹, handbrake cable 手刹拉线, leave the handbrake on 手刹未松, handbrake turn 手刹漂移
[联] brake 刹车, parking 停车, car 汽车, hill 坡道, driver 司机, pedal 踏板
[记] 可数：a handbrake。BrE handbrake，AmE parking brake / emergency brake

**sunroof**
英/美 /ˈsʌnruːf/
n. 天窗
[义] A panel in the roof of a car that can be opened to let in air and light. 车顶可打开以通风采光的 panel（ open the sunroof 开天窗, panoramic sunroof 全景天窗, sunroof leak 天窗漏水）。可数。
[例] ① They opened the sunroof on the sunny coastal drive. 沿海晴日驾车时他们打开天窗。 ② Water dripped through the sunroof after the seals aged. 密封老化后天窗漏水。
[搭] open the sunroof 开天窗, panoramic sunroof 全景天窗, sunroof leak 天窗漏水, sliding sunroof 滑动天窗
[联] car 汽车, roof 车顶, windshield 挡风玻璃, convertible 敞篷车, vehicle 车辆
[记] 可数：a sunroof。panoramic sunroof 为大面积玻璃顶

**convertible**
英/美 /kənˈvɜːtəbl/
n. 敞篷车
adj. 可转换的
[义] A car with a roof that can be folded down or removed. 车顶可折下或拆下的汽车（ sports convertible 敞篷跑车, convertible top 软顶, drive a convertible 开敞篷）。可数/形容词。
[例] ① They rented a convertible for the coastal highway trip. 他们租敞篷车走沿海公路。 ② Rain forced them to raise the convertible top quickly. 下雨迫使他们快收软顶。
[搭] sports convertible 敞篷跑车, convertible top 软顶, drive a convertible 开敞篷, hard-top convertible 硬顶敞篷
[联] car 汽车, sunroof 天窗, sedan 轿车, road trip 公路旅行, vehicle 车辆
[记] 可数/形容词。top down 指敞篷打开

**crossover**
英/美 /ˈkrɒsəʊvə(r)/
n. 跨界车
[义] A car that combines features of an SUV and a hatchback, often built on a car platform. 兼具 SUV 与掀背特征、常基于轿车平台的车型（ compact crossover 紧凑跨界, crossover SUV 跨界 SUV, family crossover 家庭跨界车）。可数。
[例] ① Crossovers have overtaken traditional sedans in many markets. 许多市场跨界车已超过传统轿车。 ② She chose a compact crossover for easier city parking. 她选紧凑跨界便于城里停车。
[搭] compact crossover 紧凑跨界, crossover SUV 跨界 SUV, family crossover 家庭跨界车, crossover market 跨界车市场
[联] SUV 越野车, hatchback 掀背车, sedan 轿车, car 汽车, vehicle 车辆
[记] 可数：a crossover。crossover 亦指「跨界合作、转场」，语境区分

**coupe**
英/美 /ˈkuːpeɪ/
n. 双门轿跑
[义] A car with a fixed roof and two doors, often with a sporty shape. 固定车顶、两门、常呈运动造型的汽车（ sports coupe 双门跑车, coupe model 轿跑款, two-door coupe 双门轿跑）。可数。
[例] ① The coupe sat lower than the sedan version of the same model. 同款轿跑比三厢版更低。 ② He traded practicality for a sleek two-door coupe. 他用实用性换了一辆线条流畅的双门轿跑。
[搭] sports coupe 双门跑车, two-door coupe 双门轿跑, coupe model 轿跑款, luxury coupe 豪华轿跑
[联] sedan 轿车, convertible 敞篷车, car 汽车, sports car 跑车, vehicle 车辆
[记] 可数：a coupe。发音 /ˈkuːpeɪ/；法语借词

**estate car**
英 /ɪˈsteɪt kɑː(r)/ 美 /ɪˈsteɪt kɑːr/
n. 旅行车； estate
[义] A car with a long rear area for luggage and a rear door that opens upward (BrE). 后部加长、行李区大、后门上掀的汽车（英式）（ family estate 家庭旅行车, estate car boot 旅行车行李箱, load the estate 装旅行车）。可数。美式 station wagon。
[例] ① They packed the estate car for the camping holiday. 他们为露营假日塞满旅行车。 ② An estate car suits dog owners who need boot space. 旅行车适合需要后备箱空间的狗主人。
