英/美 /ˈruːf ræk/
n. 车顶行李架
[义] A frame fixed to the roof of a car for carrying luggage, bikes, or other loads. 固定于车顶、载运行李、自行车或其他货物的 frame（ install a roof rack 装车顶架, roof rack load 车顶荷载, roof rack straps 车顶绑带）。可数。
[例] ① Skis were strapped to the roof rack for the trip to the Alps. 去阿尔卑斯行程滑雪板绑在车顶架上。 ② Check the roof rack bolts before motorway driving. 上高速前检查车顶架螺栓。
[搭] install a roof rack 装车顶架, roof rack load 车顶荷载, roof rack straps 车顶绑带, roof rack box 车顶箱
[联] car 汽车, luggage 行李, bicycle 自行车, ski 滑雪, vehicle 车辆, strap 绑带
[记] 可数：a roof rack。超载或安装不当影响安全

**bike rack**
英/美 /ˈbaɪk ræk/
n. 自行车架
[义] A frame for holding bicycles on a vehicle, building, or street. 固定在车辆、建筑或街上停放自行车的 frame（ car bike rack 车载自行车架, street bike rack 路边自行车架, mount a bike rack 安装车架）。可数。
[例] ① The bus has a bike rack on the front for commuters. 巴士前部有通勤者用的自行车架。 ② Secure your bike to the rack with a strong lock. 用牢固的锁把车锁到架上。
[搭] car bike rack 车载自行车架, street bike rack 路边自行车架, mount a bike rack 安装车架, rear bike rack 后载车架
[联] bicycle 自行车, bike 自行车, rack 架子, lock 锁, commute 通勤, bus 公共汽车
[记] 可数：a bike rack。bus bike rack 在欧美公交上很常见

**blimp**
英/美 /blɪmp/
n. 软式飞艇
[义] A large airship without an internal rigid frame, filled with gas lighter than air. 无内部刚性骨架、充比空气轻的气体的大型飞艇（ advertising blimp 广告飞艇, blimp ride 乘飞艇, Goodyear blimp 固特异飞艇）。可数。
[例] ① A blimp hovered over the stadium during the final. 决赛期间软式飞艇在体育场上空盘旋。 ② Blimps move slowly but offer stable camera platforms. 飞艇慢但可作稳定航拍平台。
[搭] advertising blimp 广告飞艇, blimp ride 乘飞艇, airship crew 飞艇机组, blimp hangar 飞艇库
[联] aircraft 航空器, balloon 气球, hot-air balloon 热气球, pilot 飞行员, flight 飞行
[记] 可数：a blimp。blimp vs. zeppelin：后者有硬骨架

**freight car**
英/美 /ˈfreɪt kɑː(r)/
n. 货运车厢
[义] A railway wagon used for carrying goods rather than passengers (AmE). 铁路运载货物而非乘客的车厢（美式）（ freight car consist 货运编组, empty freight car 空货车, freight car loading 装车）。可数。英式 goods wagon / goods van。
[例] ① Long freight car trains rumble through the valley at night. 长列货车夜间隆隆穿过山谷。 ② Workers sealed the freight car before departure. 出发前工人封了货运车厢。
[搭] freight car consist 货运编组, empty freight car 空货车, freight car loading 装车, box freight car 棚车
[联] freight 货运, freight train 货运列车, cargo 货物, railway 铁路, locomotive 机车, wagon 车皮
[记] 可数：a freight car。BrE goods wagon；boxcar 为美式棚车

**pothole**
英/美 /ˈpɒthəʊl/
n. 路面坑洞
[义] A hole in a road surface caused by wear and weather. 路面因磨损与天气形成的坑（ hit a pothole 轧坑, pothole damage 坑洞损车, pothole repair 补坑）。可数。
[例] ① The suspension broke after he hit a deep pothole. 轧深坑后悬挂坏了。 ② Councils face backlogs of pothole repairs each spring. 市政每年春天积压大量补坑任务。
[搭] hit a pothole 轧坑, pothole damage 坑洞损车, pothole repair 补坑, avoid potholes 避坑
[联] road 道路, suspension 悬挂, tyre 轮胎, traffic 交通, repair 维修, driver 司机
[记] 可数：a pothole。pot（锅）+ hole → 路面「锅状」坑

**tailgate**
英/美 /ˈteɪlɡeɪt/
n. 尾门
v. 跟车过近
[义] The door at the back of a pickup or SUV that opens downward or sideways; to drive too close behind another vehicle. 皮卡或 SUV 后部向下或侧开之门；跟车过近。可数/动词。
[例] ① They loaded coolers through the open tailgate. 他们从打开的尾门装入冷藏箱。 ② The lorry was tailgating the car on the motorway. 高速上卡车跟小车过近。
[搭] open the tailgate 打开尾门, tailgate party 车尾派对〔AmE〕, tailgate too closely 跟车过近, tailgate lift 尾板升降
[联] pickup 皮卡, SUV 越野车, trunk 后备箱, truck 卡车, traffic 交通, safety 安全
[记] 可数/动词。tailgate party 为美式赛前聚会；动词 tailgate 指危险跟车

**toll booth**
英/美 /ˈtəʊl buːð/
n. 收费站
[义] A small building or kiosk where drivers pay to use a toll road or bridge. 司机付费使用收费公路或桥梁的小屋或 kiosk（ stop at a toll booth 在收费站停, toll booth queue 收费站排队, automated toll booth 自动收费亭）。可数。
[例] ① We waited in a long queue at the toll booth. 我们在收费站排了长队。 ② Electronic tags let cars pass toll booths without stopping. 电子标签让车辆无需停车通过收费站。
[搭] stop at a toll booth 在收费站停, toll booth queue 收费站排队, automated toll booth 自动收费亭, toll booth operator 收费员
[联] toll road 收费公路, highway 公路, bridge 桥梁, fare 费用, traffic 交通, motorway 高速公路
[记] 可数：a toll booth。亦说 toll plaza（美式大型收费广场）

**parking meter**
英/美 /ˈpɑːkɪŋ miːtə(r)/
n. 停车计时器
[义] A device at the roadside where you pay for the time a vehicle may park. 路边按停车时间付费的装置（ feed the parking meter 投币/刷卡, parking meter zone 计时停车区, expired parking meter 计时过期）。可数。
[例] ① He rushed back before the parking meter expired. 计时器到期前他赶了回去。 ② The city is replacing coin parking meters with apps. 市里用 App 替换投币停车计时器。
[搭] feed the parking meter 投币/刷卡, parking meter zone 计时停车区, expired parking meter 计时过期, parking meter ticket 超时罚单
[联] parking 停车, car 汽车, street 街道, fine 罚款, ticket 罚单, curb 路缘
[记] 可数：a parking meter。pay-by-phone 停车渐普及

**traffic cone**
英/美 /ˈtræfɪk kəʊn/
n. 交通锥；路锥
[义] A bright orange cone placed on roads to mark hazards or channel traffic. 置于路面、标示危险或引导车流的橙色锥（ roadworks traffic cones 施工路锥, traffic cone lane 锥桶隔离车道, knock over a traffic cone 撞倒路锥）。可数。
[例] ① Traffic cones narrowed the lane around the pothole repair. 路锥在补坑处收窄车道。 ② Workers laid out traffic cones before resurfacing the road. 工人重铺路面前先摆路锥。
[搭] roadworks traffic cones 施工路锥, traffic cone lane 锥桶隔离车道, orange traffic cone 橙色路锥
[联] road 道路, construction 施工, traffic 交通, lane 车道, safety 安全, sign 标志
[记] 可数：a traffic cone。口语 cone 即可

**roadblock**
英/美 /ˈrəʊdblɒk/
n. 路障； checkpoint
[义] A barrier set up across a road to stop vehicles, often by police or military. 横在路中拦截车辆的路障，常为警或军设置（ police roadblock 警察路障, hit a roadblock 遇路障, set up a roadblock 设卡）。可数。
[例] ① Police set up a roadblock on the motorway exit. 警察在高速出口设卡。 ② The convoy slowed at an unexpected roadblock. 车队在意外路障前减速。
[搭] police roadblock 警察路障, hit a roadblock 遇路障, set up a roadblock 设卡, military roadblock 军事路障
[联] checkpoint 检查站, police 警察, traffic 交通, road 道路, barrier 障碍, security 安全
[记] 可数：a roadblock。road + block → 封路

**checkpoint**
英/美 /ˈtʃekpɔɪnt/
n. 检查站
[义] A place where travellers or vehicles are stopped for inspection. 对旅客或车辆进行检查的停靠点（ border checkpoint 边境检查站, security checkpoint 安检站, pass a checkpoint 过检查站）。可数。
[例] ① We queued at the border checkpoint for an hour. 我们在边境检查站排了一小时。 ② Soldiers waved the lorry through the checkpoint. 士兵示意卡车通过检查站。
[搭] border checkpoint 边境检查站, security checkpoint 安检站, pass a checkpoint 过检查站, checkpoint guard 检查站警卫
[联] roadblock 路障, border 边境, passport 护照, police 警察, traffic 交通, security 安全
[记] 可数：a checkpoint。机场、边境、军事场景均用

**border crossing**
英/美 /ˈbɔːdə krɒsɪŋ/
n. 边境口岸
[义] A place where you legally enter or leave one country for another by road or rail. 公路或铁路合法出入境的地点（ busy border crossing 繁忙口岸, border crossing delay 口岸延误, international border crossing 国际边境口岸）。可数。
[例] ① Documents were checked at the border crossing. 边境口岸核查了证件。 ② Truckers avoid the busy border crossing at weekends. 卡车司机周末避开繁忙口岸。
[搭] busy border crossing 繁忙口岸, border crossing delay 口岸延误, international border crossing 国际边境口岸
[联] checkpoint 检查站, passport 护照, customs 海关, border 边境, lorry 卡车, queue 排队
[记] 可数：a border crossing。visa、customs 细节多属 07-旅行与住宿

**turbocharger**
英/美 /ˈtɜːbəʊtʃɑːdʒə(r)/
n. 涡轮增压器
[义] A device that uses exhaust gases to force extra air into an engine, increasing power. 利用废气将额外空气压入引擎以提高功率的装置（ turbocharger lag 涡轮迟滞, twin turbocharger 双涡轮, turbocharged engine 涡轮增压发动机）。可数。
[例] ① Turbocharger lag made the car hesitate before accelerating. 涡轮迟滞使加速前一顿。 ② The mechanic replaced a failed turbocharger. 机修工更换了故障涡轮增压器。
[搭] turbocharger lag 涡轮迟滞, twin turbocharger 双涡轮, turbocharged engine 涡轮增压发动机, turbocharger repair 涡轮维修
[联] engine 引擎, exhaust 排气管, turbine 涡轮, car 汽车, performance 性能, mechanic 机修工
[记] 可数：a turbocharger。口语 turbo 指涡轮或涡轮车

**fuel injection**
英/美 /ˈfjuːl ɪnˌdʒekʃn/
n. 燃油喷射
[义] A system that sprays fuel directly into an engine's cylinders or intake. 将燃油直接喷入气缸或进气道的系统（ fuel injection system 燃油喷射系统, electronic fuel injection 电子燃油喷射, fuel injection failure 喷射故障）。不可数。
[例] ① Modern cars rely on precise fuel injection for efficiency. 现代车靠精确燃油喷射省油。 ② Fuel injection problems can cause rough idling. 燃油喷射故障可导致怠速不稳。
[搭] fuel injection system 燃油喷射系统, electronic fuel injection 电子燃油喷射, fuel injection failure 喷射故障
[联] engine 引擎, fuel 燃料, petrol 汽油, diesel 柴油, carburettor 化油器, mechanic 机修工
[记] 不可数。缩写 EFI；已 largely 取代 carburettor

**alternator**
英/美 /ˈɔːltəneɪtə(r)/
n. 发电机
[义] A device in a vehicle that generates electricity to charge the battery and power electrical systems while the engine runs. 引擎运转时为电池充电并供电气系统用电的装置（ replace the alternator 更换发电机, alternator belt 发电机皮带, faulty alternator 故障发电机）。可数。
[例] ① The battery warning light pointed to a dead alternator. 电池警示灯指向发电机失效。 ② A squealing alternator belt needed tightening. 发电机皮带尖叫需收紧。
[搭] replace the alternator 更换发电机, alternator belt 发电机皮带, faulty alternator 故障发电机, alternator output 发电机输出
[联] battery 电瓶, engine 引擎, car 汽车, electrical 电气的, mechanic 机修工, belt 皮带
[记] 可数：an alternator。alternator vs. starter：后者负责启动

**tachometer**
英/美 /tæˈkɒmɪtə(r)/
n. 转速表
[义] An instrument showing how fast an engine is turning, usually in revolutions per minute. 显示引擎转速、通常为每分钟转数的仪表（ tachometer needle 转速指针, redline on the tachometer 转速红线, digital tachometer 数字转速表）。可数。
[例] ① He shifted gear as the tachometer approached the redline. 转速近红线时他换挡。 ② The tachometer twitched at idle before stalling. 熄火前怠速时转速表抖动。
[源] 源自希腊语 *tachys*（快）+ -meter（测量）→ 测转速之器。
[搭] tachometer needle 转速指针, redline on the tachometer 转速红线, digital tachometer 数字转速表
[联] dashboard 仪表盘, engine 引擎, speedometer 车速表, car 汽车, gear 档位, RPM 转每分钟
[记] 可数：a tachometer。口语 tach /ˈtæk/；RPM = revolutions per minute

**apron**
英/美 /ˈeɪprən/
n. 停机坪
[义] The paved area at an airport where aircraft park, load, and unload. 机场供飞机停放、装卸的铺装区域（ airport apron 机场停机坪, apron bus 机坪摆渡车, on the apron 在停机坪上）。可数。亦指「围裙」，语境区分。
[例] ① Ground staff directed the plane to a stand on the apron. 地勤引导飞机至停机坪机位。 ② Fuel trucks crossed the busy apron all morning. 上午加油车在繁忙停机坪穿梭。
[搭] airport apron 机场停机坪, apron bus 机坪摆渡车, on the apron 在停机坪上, apron stand 机坪机位
[联] airport 机场, airplane 飞机, runway 跑道, taxiway 滑行道, terminal 航站楼, ground crew 地勤
[记] 可数：an apron。aviation apron 为航空专用义

**control tower**
英/美 /kənˈtrəʊl taʊə(r)/
n. 塔台
[义] A building at an airport from which air traffic controllers direct planes on the ground and in the air nearby. 机场内管制员指挥地面与近空飞机的塔楼（ airport control tower 机场塔台, control tower clearance 塔台放行, contact the control tower 联系塔台）。可数。
[例] ① The pilot received clearance from the control tower to take off. 飞行员获塔台放行起飞。 ② Lights blinked on the control tower through the fog. 雾中塔台灯光闪烁。
[搭] airport control tower 机场塔台, control tower clearance 塔台放行, contact the control tower 联系塔台
[联] airport 机场, runway 跑道, pilot 飞行员, air traffic 空中交通, taxiway 滑行道, flight 航班
[记] 可数：a control tower。ATC = air traffic control

**guard van**
英/美 /ˈɡɑːd væn/
n. 守车；列车尾部车箱
[义] The carriage at the end of a freight or passenger train where the guard travels (BrE). 货运或客运列车尾部供列车员乘值的车厢（英式）（ guard van door 守车门, travel in the guard van 在守车值乘, guard van brake 守车制动）。可数。美式 caboose。
[例] ① The guard waved from the guard van as the train departed. 列车出发时列车员在守车内挥手。 ② Historic railways still run trains with a guard van. 历史铁路仍运行带守车的列车。
[搭] guard van door 守车门, travel in the guard van 在守车值乘, guard van brake 守车制动
[联] train 火车, carriage 车厢, railway 铁路, freight 货运, guard 列车员, locomotive 机车
[记] 可数：a guard van。BrE guard van，AmE caboose（货运）

**boxcar**
英/美 /ˈbɒkskɑː(r)/
n. 棚车
[义] A closed railway freight wagon with sliding doors on the sides. 两侧带滑动门、封闭的铁路货车（ empty boxcar 空棚车, boxcar load 棚车载货, boxcar door 棚车门）。可数。英式 covered wagon。
[例] ① Boxcars lined the siding loaded with lumber. 侧线上一节节课棚车装满木材。 ② Workers sealed the boxcar before the long haul. 长途运输前工人封了棚车。
[搭] empty boxcar 空棚车, boxcar load 棚车载货, boxcar door 棚车门, refrigerated boxcar 冷藏棚车
[联] freight car 货运车厢, freight train 货运列车, cargo 货物, railway 铁路, wagon 车皮, locomotive 机车
[记] 可数：a boxcar。BrE covered wagon 同义

**flatcar**
英/美 /ˈflætkɑː(r)/
n. 平车
[义] A railway wagon with a flat platform and no sides or roof, for carrying heavy or oversized loads. 无侧壁与顶、运重型或超限货物的铁路平车（ flatcar load 平车装载, flatcar transport 平车运输, empty flatcar 空平车）。可数。
[例] ① A tractor sat chained to a flatcar in the yard. 站场里一台拖拉机拴在平车上。 ② Flatcars carry containers between ports and inland depots. 平车在港口与内陆场站间运集装箱。
[搭] flatcar load 平车装载, flatcar transport 平车运输, empty flatcar 空平车, flatcar shipment 平车发运
[联] freight car 货运车厢, freight train 货运列车, cargo 货物, container 集装箱, railway 铁路, truck 卡车
[记] 可数：a flatcar。flat + car → 平板货车

**tank car**
英/美 /ˈtæŋk kɑː(r)/
n. 罐车
[义] A railway wagon shaped like a tank, for carrying liquids or gases. 罐形铁路货车，运载液体或气体（ oil tank car 油罐车, chemical tank car 化学品罐车, tank car leak 罐车泄漏）。可数。亦指公路 tanker，语境区分。
[例] ① A long train of tank cars moved crude oil to the refinery. 长列罐车把原油运往炼油厂。 ② Inspectors checked each tank car for leaks. 检查员逐节查罐车泄漏。
[搭] oil tank car 油罐车, chemical tank car 化学品罐车, tank car leak 罐车泄漏, tank car fleet 罐车队
[联] tanker 油轮, freight train 货运列车, cargo 货物, fuel 燃料, railway 铁路, chemical 化学品
[记] 可数：a tank car。铁路 tank car vs. road tanker

**central reservation**
英 /ˌsentrəl ˌrezəˈveɪʃn/ 美 /ˌsentrəl ˌrezərˈveɪʃn/
n. 中央隔离带
[义] The strip separating traffic moving in opposite directions on a dual carriageway (BrE). 双向分隔道路上隔开对向车流的条带（英式）（ hit the central reservation 撞上隔离带, central reservation barrier 中央护栏, cross the central reservation 穿越隔离带〔违法〕）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① The car spun onto the central reservation after the skid. 打滑后车旋进中央隔离带。 ② Trees on the central reservation reduce headlight glare. 中央隔离带的树减对头灯眩光。
[搭] hit the central reservation 撞上隔离带, central reservation barrier 中央护栏, cross the central reservation 穿越隔离带
[联] median 中央隔离带, dual carriageway 双向分隔路, motorway 高速公路, traffic 交通, barrier 护栏
[记] 不可数/可数。BrE central reservation，AmE median / median strip

**curb**
英/美 /kɜːrb/
n. 路缘
[义] The edge of a pavement where it meets the road (AmE spelling; BrE kerb). 人行道与路面交接的 edge（美式拼写；英式 kerb）（ park at the curb 路边停车, curb parking 路缘停车, hit the curb 轧路缘）。可数。
[例] ① She parallel parked tight against the curb. 她平行泊车紧贴路缘。 ② Snow piled along the curb blocked drainage. 路缘积雪堵了排水。
[搭] park at the curb 路边停车, curb parking 路缘停车, hit the curb 轧路缘, curb ramp 路缘坡道
[联] pavement 人行道, sidewalk 人行道, street 街道, parking 停车, wheel 轮子, tyre 轮胎
[记] 可数：a curb。BrE kerb；curb 亦作动词「抑制」

**pedestrian bridge**
英/美 /pəˈdestriən brɪdʒ/
n. 人行天桥
[义] A bridge built for people on foot to cross a busy road or railway. 供行人跨越繁忙公路或铁路的桥（ foot pedestrian bridge 步行天桥, pedestrian bridge access 天桥通道, cross via the pedestrian bridge 经天桥过街）。可数。
[例] ① Students use the pedestrian bridge over the motorway. 学生走 motorway 上的人行天桥。 ② The pedestrian bridge lifts for ships on the canal below. 运河下方人行桥可为船只抬起。
[搭] foot pedestrian bridge 步行天桥, pedestrian bridge access 天桥通道, cross via the pedestrian bridge 经天桥过街

**give way**
英/美 /ˌɡɪv ˈweɪ/
n. 让行（标志/规则）
v. 让行； yield
[义] The rule or road sign requiring a driver to allow other traffic to go first (BrE). 要求驾驶员让其他交通先行的规则或路标（英式）（ give way sign 让行标志, give way line 让行线, give way to traffic 给车流让行）。不可数/动词。美式 yield。
[例] ① Give way to traffic already on the roundabout. 给环岛内已有车流让行。 ② A hidden give way sign caused confusion at the junction. 隐蔽让行标志使路口混乱。

**no entry**
英/美 /ˌnəʊ ˈentri/
n. 禁止进入
[义] A sign or rule that forbids vehicles from entering a road or area. 禁止车辆进入某道路或区域的标志或规则（ no entry sign 禁止进入标志, no entry zone 禁入区, ignore a no entry sign 无视禁入标志）。不可数。
[例] ① The one-way street had a clear no entry sign at this end. 单行道此端有清晰禁入标志。 ② Drivers who ignore no entry signs risk heavy fines. 无视禁入标志可重罚。

**headrest**
英/美 /ˈhedrest/
n. 头枕
[义] A padded support for the head on a car seat, designed to reduce whiplash injury. 汽车座椅上减轻挥鞭样伤的头部软垫支撑（ adjustable headrest 可调头枕, rear headrest 后排头枕, headrest height 头枕高度）。可数。
[例] ① Adjust the headrest so it sits level with the top of your head. 调节头枕与头顶齐平。 ② Missing headrests failed the safety inspection. 缺少头枕未通过安全检查。

**armrest**
英/美 /ˈɑːmrest/
n. 扶手

[例] ① He lifted the centre armrest to let the child sit in the middle. 他抬起中央扶手让孩子坐中间。 ② The worn armrest leather needed replacing. 扶手皮革磨损需更换。

英/美 /ˈdʒʌmpə keɪblz/
n. 搭电线
[义] Two thick cables used to connect one car's battery to another to start an engine that will not start. 连接两车电池、启动打不着火引擎的两根粗电缆（ connect jumper cables 接搭电线, jumper cable clamp 搭电夹, pair of jumper cables 一副搭电线）。复数。
[例] ① A stranger offered jumper cables when our battery died. 电瓶没电时路人提供了搭电线。 ② Connect the jumper cables red to positive, black to negative. 搭电线红接正极、黑接负极。
[搭] connect jumper cables 接搭电线, jumper cable clamp 搭电夹, pair of jumper cables 一副搭电线
[联] battery 电瓶, car 汽车, engine 引擎, breakdown 故障, mechanic 机修工, jump-start 搭电启动

**tow rope**
英/美 /ˈtəʊ rəʊp/
n. 拖绳

[例] ① They attached a tow rope and pulled the jeep from the mud. 他们系拖绳把 jeep 从泥里拉出。 ② Using a tow rope on the motorway is dangerous and often illegal. 高速上用拖绳危险且常违法。

[例] ① He jacked up the car and swapped the flat tyre. 他用千斤顶顶车换了瘪胎。 ② Always use the jack on firm, level ground. 千斤顶须放在坚实平地。

[例] ① The sports car cornered flat through the bends. 跑车在弯中平稳过弯。 ② Insurance for a sports car costs more than for a family saloon. 跑车保险比家庭三厢贵。

[例] ① The muscle car roared away from the lights. 肌肉车红绿灯前轰鸣离去。 ② Collectors pay premium prices for original muscle cars. 藏家为原版肌肉车付高价。

[例] ① He flicked the indicator and moved into the right lane. 他拨转向灯并入右道。 ② Failing to use an indicator is a common minor offence. 不打转向灯是常见轻微违法。

[例] ① A camera issued him a speeding ticket in the mail. 摄像头寄来超速罚单。 ② Speeding tickets raise insurance premiums for young drivers. 超速罚单抬高年轻司机保费。

英/美 /ˈkɑː siːt/
n. 儿童安全座椅
[义] A special seat for a child, secured in a car for safety. 固定在车内、保障儿童安全的专用座椅（ install a car seat 安装儿童座椅, rear-facing car seat 后向座椅, car seat belt path 安全带路径）。可数。
[例] ① Always install the car seat according to the manual. 务必按说明书安装儿童座椅。 ② The car seat must match the child's height and weight. 安全座椅须符合儿童身高体重。
[搭] install a car seat 安装儿童座椅, rear-facing car seat 后向座椅, forward-facing car seat 前向座椅, car seat base 座椅底座
[联] seat belt 安全带, child 儿童, car 汽车, safety 安全, passenger 乘客, ISOFIX 接口
[记] 可数：a car seat。car seat 亦泛指「汽车座椅」，语境区分

**car ferry**
英/美 /ˈkɑː ˈferi/
n. 汽车渡轮
[义] A ferry designed to carry passengers and their vehicles across water. 运载乘客及其车辆过水的渡轮（ car ferry deck 汽车甲板, drive-on car ferry 滚装渡轮, car ferry booking 订车位）。可数。
[例] ① We queued two hours for the car ferry to the island. 去岛上汽车渡轮排队两小时。 ② The car ferry sailed despite rough seas. 渡轮在浪大中仍启航。
[搭] car ferry deck 汽车甲板, drive-on car ferry 滚装渡轮, car ferry booking 订车位, overnight car ferry 过夜汽车渡轮
[联] ferry 渡轮, car 汽车, deck 甲板, harbour 港湾, passenger 乘客, ro-ro 滚装
[记] 可数：a car ferry。booking、票价细节可参见 07-旅行与住宿

**roll-on roll-off**
英/美 /ˌrəʊl ɒn ˌrəʊl ɒf/
n. 滚装运输
[义] A method of loading vehicles onto ships by driving them on and off (abbrev. ro-ro). 车辆自行驶上驶下船舶的装载方式（缩写 ro-ro）（ roll-on roll-off ferry 滚装渡轮, roll-on roll-off port 滚装港, roll-on roll-off cargo 滚装货）。不可数/形容词。
[例] ① Roll-on roll-off ferries link the islands daily. 滚装渡轮每日连接各岛。 ② The port expanded its roll-on roll-off capacity for lorries. 港口扩建货车滚装能力。
[搭] roll-on roll-off ferry 滚装渡轮, roll-on roll-off port 滚装港, roll-on roll-off vessel 滚装船, ro-ro ferry 滚装渡轮
[联] car ferry 汽车渡轮, ferry 渡轮, lorry 卡车, cargo 货物, port 港口, deck 甲板
[记] 不可数/形容词。缩写 ro-ro / RORO

**cabin**
英/美 /ˈkæbɪn/
n. 舱室
[义] A small room on a ship or aircraft; the passenger area of a plane. 船或飞机上的小房间；飞机客舱（ ship cabin 船舱, aircraft cabin 客舱, cabin crew 客舱乘务员）。可数。
[例] ① They booked a cabin with a sea view on the ferry. 他们订了带海景的渡轮船舱。 ② Cabin crew demonstrated safety procedures before takeoff. 起飞前客舱组演示安全程序。
[搭] ship cabin 船舱, aircraft cabin 客舱, cabin crew 客舱乘务员, cabin baggage 随身行李
[联] deck 甲板, ship 大船, airplane 飞机, passenger 乘客, crew 乘务组, cockpit 驾驶舱
[记] 可数：a cabin。cabin 亦指「小木屋、驾驶舱〔旧〕」，语境区分

**MOT**
英 /ˌem əʊ ˈtiː/ 美 /ˌem oʊ ˈtiː/
n. 车辆年检
[义] An annual test of vehicle safety and emissions required for cars over three years old in the UK (Ministry of Transport test). 英国对三年以上车龄车辆进行的年度安全与排放检测（ pass the MOT 通过年检, MOT certificate 年检证书, MOT failure 年检不合格）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① The garage quoted repairs needed to pass the MOT. 修理厂报价通过年检所需维修。 ② An expired MOT invalidates insurance on public roads. 年检过期则公路保险失效。
[搭] pass the MOT 通过年检, MOT certificate 年检证书, MOT failure 年检不合格, MOT test centre 检测站
[联] car 汽车, vehicle 车辆, inspection 检查, emission 排放, mechanic 机修工, insurance 保险
[记] 读字母 M-O-T；BrE 专用；AmE 类似 inspection / smog check（因州而异）

**keel**
英/美 /kiːl/
n. 龙骨
[义] The long structural beam along the bottom of a ship's hull. 沿船体底部纵向的结构梁（ keel laying 龙骨铺设, on an even keel 平稳〔喻〕, keel damage 龙骨损伤）。可数。
[例] ① The yacht's keel was damaged in the shallow grounding. 游艇在浅滩搁浅时龙骨受损。 ② Traditional ships were built around a wooden keel. 传统船舶围绕木龙骨建造。
[搭] keel laying 龙骨铺设, on an even keel 平稳, keel damage 龙骨损伤, fin keel 鳍式龙骨
[联] hull 船体, bow 船首, stern 船尾, ship 大船, sailboat 帆船, yacht 游艇
[记] 可数：a keel。on an even keel 喻「平稳、稳定」

**weigh station**
英/美 /ˈweɪ steɪʃn/
n. 称重站
[义] A checkpoint where trucks must stop to be weighed (AmE). 卡车须停靠称重的检查站（美式）（ truck weigh station 货车称重站, pass a weigh station 过称重站, weigh station bypass 称重站绕行）。可数。英式 weighbridge 更常说地磅本身。
[例] ① The lorry pulled into the weigh station near the state line. 货车在州界附近称重站驶入。 ② Overloaded trucks face fines at weigh stations. 超载货车在称重站会被罚款。
[搭] truck weigh station 货车称重站, pass a weigh station 过称重站, weigh station bypass 称重站绕行, portable weigh station 移动称重站
[联] weighbridge 地磅, lorry 卡车, truck 卡车, freight 货运, highway 公路, load 载重
[记] 可数：a weigh station。AmE weigh station，BrE 多用 weighbridge 指设备

**pelican crossing**
英 /ˈpelɪkən ˈkrɒsɪŋ/ 美 /ˈpelɪkən ˈkrɔːsɪŋ/
n. Pelican 式人行横道
[义] A pedestrian crossing with traffic lights operated by push buttons (BrE). 行人按按钮控制信号灯的过街处（英式）（ use a pelican crossing 使用 Pelican 横道, pelican crossing lights Pelican 信号灯, wait at a pelican crossing 在横道等候）。可数。
[例] ① Press the button and wait for the green figure at the pelican crossing. 按按钮后在 Pelican 横道等绿色行人灯。 ② Drivers must stop when the pelican crossing lights turn red. 横道灯变红时司机必须停车。
[搭] use a pelican crossing 使用 Pelican 横道, pelican crossing lights Pelican 信号灯, wait at a pelican crossing 在横道等候
[联] zebra crossing 斑马线, pedestrian crossing 人行横道, traffic light 红绿灯, crosswalk 人行横道, pedestrian 行人
[记] 可数：a pelican crossing。Pelican = PEdestrian LIght CONtrolled；另有 puffin、toucan 等变体

**dead end**
英/美 /ˌded ˈend/
n. 死胡同；尽头路
[义] A street closed at one end with no through route. 一端封闭、无贯通出口的街道（ live on a dead end 住在死胡同, dead end street 死路, turn down a dead end 拐进死胡同）。可数。英式 cul-de-sac 更正式。
[例] ① The sat nav sent us down a dead end blocked by construction. 导航把我们带进施工堵死的死胡同。 ② Dead end streets often have less through traffic. 死胡同通常少有穿行车流。
[搭] live on a dead end 住在死胡同, dead end street 死路, turn down a dead end 拐进死胡同, no dead end exit 无出口
[联] cul-de-sac 尽端路, street 街道, road 道路, turn 转弯, traffic 交通, neighbourhood 社区
[记] 可数：a dead end。dead end 亦喻「僵局、绝路」

**roadworks**
英/美 /ˈrəʊdwɜːks/
n. 道路施工
[义] Repairs or building work on a road that often causes delays. 路上修缮或施工，常致延误（ roadworks ahead 前方施工, roadworks diversion 施工绕行, temporary roadworks 临时施工）。复数/不可数。
[例] ① Roadworks added twenty minutes to the commute. 施工让通勤多了二十分钟。 ② Signs warned of lane closures due to roadworks. 路标警示施工导致车道封闭。
[搭] roadworks ahead 前方施工, roadworks diversion 施工绕行, temporary roadworks 临时施工, roadworks cone 施工路锥
[联] traffic cone 路锥, detour 绕道, lane closure 车道封闭, construction 施工, traffic jam 堵车, highway 公路
[记] 复数/不可数。BrE roadworks，AmE road work / construction

**tailback**
英/美 /ˈteɪlbæk/
n. 车龙；排队车流
[义] A long line of traffic waiting to move, stretching back from a blockage (BrE). 自阻塞点向后延伸的长队车流（英式）（ five-mile tailback 五英里车龙, tailback on the motorway 高速车龙, cause a tailback 造成车龙）。可数。
[例] ① A broken-down lorry caused a ten-mile tailback. 故障货车造成十英里车龙。 ② Tailbacks cleared slowly after the crash was cleared. 事故清理后车龙缓慢消散。
[搭] five-mile tailback 五英里车龙, tailback on the motorway 高速车龙, cause a tailback 造成车龙, long tailback 长车龙
[联] traffic jam 堵车, gridlock 交通瘫痪, congestion 拥堵, queue 排队, motorway 高速公路, accident 事故
[记] 可数：a tailback。BrE tailback，AmE backup / traffic backup

**contraflow**
英/美 /ˈkɒntrəfləʊ/
n. 反向通行
[义] A temporary arrangement where traffic uses lanes normally reserved for the opposite direction. 车辆临时使用对向车道的安排（ contraflow lane 反向车道, contraflow system 反向通行系统, contraflow bus lane 反向公交道）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① Contraflow was introduced while the bridge was repaired. 修桥期间实行反向通行。 ② Drivers unfamiliar with contraflow often hesitate at merges. 不熟悉反向通行的司机在汇入时常犹豫。
[搭] contraflow lane 反向车道, contraflow system 反向通行系统, contraflow bus lane 反向公交道, temporary contraflow 临时反向通行
[联] lane 车道, roadworks 道路施工, traffic 交通, merge 汇入, motorway 高速公路, diversion 绕行
[记] 不可数/可数。contra-（相反）+ flow（流）

**passing lane**
英/美 /ˈpɑːsɪŋ leɪn/
n. 超车道
[义] A lane on a multi-lane road where faster vehicles overtake slower ones. 多车道公路上供较快车辆超越慢车的车道（ keep to the passing lane 保持超车道, passing lane only 仅用于超车, illegal passing lane use 违法占用超车道）。可数。
[例] ① Slower drivers should move out of the passing lane. 慢车应离开超车道。 ② He overtook three trucks in the passing lane. 他在超车道超越三辆卡车。
[搭] keep to the passing lane 保持超车道, passing lane only 仅用于超车, left passing lane 左侧超车道
[联] lane 车道, overtake 超车, highway 公路, motorway 高速公路, traffic 交通, slow 慢的
[记] 可数：a passing lane。AmE passing lane；BrE overtaking lane

**valet parking**
英/美 /ˈvæleɪ ˈpɑːkɪŋ/
n. 代客泊车
[义] A service where an attendant parks your car for you, common at hotels and restaurants. 服务员代客停车的服务，常见于酒店与餐厅（ valet parking service 代客泊车服务, use valet parking 使用代泊, valet parking tip 代泊小费）。不可数。
[例] ① The hotel offers complimentary valet parking for guests. 酒店为客人提供免费代客泊车。 ② He tipped the valet parking attendant five dollars. 他给代泊服务员五美元小费。
[搭] valet parking service 代客泊车服务, use valet parking 使用代泊, valet parking tip 代泊小费, valet parking stand 代泊站
[联] parking 停车, hotel 酒店, car 汽车, attendant 服务员, garage 车库, tip 小费
[记] 不可数。valet 亦可指「贴身男仆」；代泊常见于北美酒店

**accessible parking**
英/美 /əkˈsesəbl ˈpɑːkɪŋ/
n. 无障碍停车位
[义] Parking spaces reserved for people with disabilities, usually marked with a symbol. 供残障人士使用的专用停车位，通常有标识（ accessible parking bay 无障碍车位, accessible parking permit 无障碍停车许可, misuse accessible parking 占用无障碍位）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① Accessible parking bays must stay clear for permit holders. 无障碍车位须留给持证者。 ② Fines are steep for parking in accessible parking without a badge. 无标识占用无障碍位罚款很重。
[搭] accessible parking bay 无障碍车位, accessible parking permit 无障碍停车许可, accessible parking space 无障碍停车位

**roof box**
英/美 /ˈruːf bɒks/
n. 车顶箱
[义] A lockable storage box mounted on a car roof rack for extra luggage. 装于车顶架、可上锁的额外行李箱（ fit a roof box 装车顶箱, roof box capacity 车顶箱容量, roof box wind noise 风噪）。可数。
[例] ① They packed skis in the roof box for the alpine trip. 滑雪行程他们把雪板装进车顶箱。 ② The roof box increased fuel consumption on the motorway. 车顶箱提高了高速油耗。
[搭] fit a roof box 装车顶箱, roof box capacity 车顶箱容量, roof box wind noise 风噪, lockable roof box 可锁车顶箱
[联] roof rack 车顶架, luggage 行李, car 汽车, ski 滑雪, travel 旅行, vehicle 车辆

**life jacket**
英/美 /ˈlaɪf dʒækɪt/
n. 救生衣
[义] A sleeveless jacket filled with air or foam that keeps you afloat in water. 无袖、充气或泡沫填充、使人在水中漂浮的背心（ wear a life jacket 穿救生衣, inflatable life jacket 充气救生衣, child life jacket 儿童救生衣）。可数。
[例] ① Passengers must wear life jackets on the small ferry. 小渡轮上乘客必须穿救生衣。 ② The child life jacket kept her chin above the water. 儿童救生衣使她下巴保持在水面以上。
[搭] wear a life jacket 穿救生衣, inflatable life jacket 充气救生衣, child life jacket 儿童救生衣, life jacket drill 救生衣演练
[联] lifeboat 救生艇, boat 小船, ferry 渡轮, safety 安全, water 水, passenger 乘客
[记] 可数：a life jacket。life vest 同义；boat 上常强制配备

**gangway**
英/美 /ˈɡæŋweɪ/
n. 舷梯；通道
[义] A movable bridge or ramp for boarding or leaving a ship; a passage on a ship or aircraft. 登离船的可移动桥或跳板；船或飞机上的通道（ gangway up 收舷梯, clear the gangway 让开通道, passenger gangway 旅客舷梯）。可数。
[例] ① Passengers queued along the gangway to board the cruise ship. 乘客沿舷梯排队登邮轮。 ② Crew shouted «Clear the gangway» as the stretcher passed. 担架经过时船员喊「让开通道」。
[搭] gangway up 收舷梯, clear the gangway 让开通道, passenger gangway 旅客舷梯, ship gangway 登船舷梯
[联] ferry 渡轮, ship 大船, dock 码头, passenger 乘客, boarding 登机/登船, deck 甲板
[记] 可数：a gangway。boarding 手续细节可参见 07-旅行与住宿

**starboard**
英/美 /ˈstɑːbəd/
n. 右舷
adj. 右舷的
[义] The right-hand side of a ship or aircraft when facing forward. 面向船/机前方时的右侧（ starboard side 右舷, starboard engine 右舷引擎, to starboard 在右舷）。不可数/形容词。左舷为 port，与「港口」port 同形，见 [记]。
[例] ① The dinghy was tied to the starboard side of the yacht. 小艇系在游艇右舷。 ② Look to starboard—you can see the lighthouse. 看右舷，能看见灯塔。
[搭] starboard side 右舷, starboard engine 右舷引擎, to starboard 在右舷, starboard light 右舷灯
[联] bow 船首, stern 船尾, port 左舷/港口, hull 船体, sailor 水手, navigation 航行
[记] 不可数/形容词。左舷 port 与「港口」port 同词，本章港口义见 port 条；航海语境靠上下文

**tuk-tuk**
英/美 /ˈtʊk tʊk/
n. 突突车
[义] A small three-wheeled motor vehicle used as a taxi, especially in South and Southeast Asia. 小型三轮机动车，尤在南亚与东南亚作出租车（ take a tuk-tuk 坐突突车, tuk-tuk driver 突突车司机, tuk-tuk fare 车费）。可数。
[例] ① We negotiated a tuk-tuk fare to the night market. 我们议价坐突突车去夜市。 ② Tuk-tuks weave through traffic in Bangkok's old quarter. 曼谷老城突突车在车流中穿梭。
[搭] take a tuk-tuk 坐突突车, tuk-tuk driver 突突车司机, tuk-tuk fare 车费, motorised tuk-tuk 机动突突车
[联] rickshaw 人力车, taxi 出租车, fare 车费, traffic 交通, Asia 亚洲, passenger 乘客
[记] 可数：a tuk-tuk。拟声名；亦拼 tuktuk

**e-bike**
英/美 /ˈiː baɪk/
n. 电动自行车
[义] A bicycle with an electric motor that assists pedalling. 带辅助踩踏电动机的自行车（ rent an e-bike 租电动自行车, e-bike battery 电动车电池, e-bike lane 电单车道）。可数。
[例] ① She commutes on an e-bike to avoid sweating in summer. 她夏天骑电动自行车通勤以免出汗。 ② The city added charging points for shared e-bikes. 该市为共享电单车增设充电点。
[搭] rent an e-bike 租电动自行车, e-bike battery 电动车电池, e-bike lane 电单车道, pedal-assist e-bike 助力电单车
[联] bicycle 自行车, bike 自行车, battery 电池, charging point 充电点, commute 通勤, cyclist 骑行者
[记] 可数：an e-bike。e- = electric；与 e-scooter 区分

**pit stop**
英/美 /ˈpɪt stɒp/
n. 进站
[义] A brief stop in motor racing for fuel and tyre changes; any short stop on a journey. 赛车中加油换胎的短暂停；旅途中的短停（ make a pit stop 短暂停一下, pit stop break 休息停, racing pit stop 赛车进站）。可数。
[例] ① We made a pit stop for coffee halfway through the drive. 车程中途停车买咖啡。 ② The racing team completed a pit stop in under two seconds. 车队两秒内完成进站。
[搭] make a pit stop 短暂停一下, pit stop break 休息停, racing pit stop 赛车进站, motorway pit stop 高速休息停
[联] tyre 轮胎, fuel 燃料, racing 赛车, rest stop 休息站, journey 行程, motorway 高速公路
[记] 可数：a pit stop。源自赛车 pit（维修区）

**write-off**
英/美 /ˈraɪt ɒf/
n. 全损车；报废
[义] A vehicle so badly damaged that repairing it costs more than its value; the act of declaring it a total loss. 损坏过重、维修费超过车辆价值的车辆；宣告全损（ insurance write-off 保险全损, car write-off 报废车, category write-off 分级全损）。可数。
[例] ① The insurer declared the car a write-off after the collision. 碰撞后保险公司判定车辆全损。 ② Write-offs are sold at auction for parts. 全损车在拍卖会上拆件出售。
[搭] insurance write-off 保险全损, car write-off 报废车, category write-off 分级全损, repair or write-off 修或报废
[联] accident 事故, insurance 保险, car 汽车, salvage 残值处理, repair 维修, damage 损坏
[记] 可数：a write-off。write-off 亦指「销账、一笔勾销」

**scrapyard**
英/美 /ˈskræpjɑːd/
n. 废车场
[义] A place where old vehicles and metal are collected for recycling or sale as parts. 收集旧车与金属以回收或拆件出售的场所（ car scrapyard 废车场, scrapyard parts 拆车件, send to the scrapyard 送报废场）。可数。美式 junkyard。
[例] ① He found a replacement door at a local scrapyard. 他在本地废车场找到替换车门。 ② The written-off van went to the scrapyard. 全损厢货进了废车场。
[搭] car scrapyard 废车场, scrapyard parts 拆车件, send to the scrapyard 送报废场, scrapyard dealer 废车商
[联] write-off 全损车, car 汽车, salvage 残值处理, recycle 回收, mechanic 机修工, parts 零件
[记] 可数：a scrapyard。AmE junkyard 同义

**backup camera**
英/美 /ˈbækʌp ˌkæmərə/
n. 倒车影像
[义] A camera on the back of a vehicle showing the area behind when reversing. 车辆后部、倒车时显示后方区域的摄像机（ backup camera display 倒车影像屏, factory backup camera 原厂倒车影像, backup camera guideline 辅助线）。可数。
[例] ① The backup camera helped her park in the tight space. 倒车影像助她在窄位停车。 ② New cars often require a backup camera by law. 新车常依法须配倒车影像。
[搭] backup camera display 倒车影像屏, factory backup camera 原厂倒车影像, backup camera guideline 辅助线, rear backup camera 后视摄像头
[联] car 汽车, parking 停车, reverse 倒车, dashboard 仪表盘, safety 安全, sensor 传感器
[记] 可数：a backup camera。BrE reversing camera 同义

**parking sensor**
英/美 /ˈpɑːkɪŋ ˌsensə(r)/
n. 倒车雷达
[义] A device that beeps when the car is close to an obstacle while parking. 停车接近障碍时发出蜂鸣的装置（ front parking sensor 前雷达, parking sensor beep 雷达蜂鸣, faulty parking sensor 故障雷达）。可数。常用复数 parking sensors。
[例] ① Parking sensors screamed as he reversed toward the wall. 倒向墙时倒车雷达狂叫。 ② Front parking sensors help in tight city garages. 前雷达在狭窄地下车库有用。
[搭] front parking sensor 前雷达, parking sensor beep 雷达蜂鸣, faulty parking sensor 故障雷达, ultrasonic parking sensor 超声波雷达
[联] backup camera 倒车影像, parking 停车, car 汽车, reverse 倒车, beep 蜂鸣, safety 安全
[记] 可数，常用复数 parking sensors

**ABS**
英 /ˌeɪ biː ˈes/ 美 /ˌeɪ biː ˈes/
n. 防抱死制动系统
[义] A system that stops wheels locking when you brake hard (anti-lock braking system). 急刹时防止车轮抱死的系统（ ABS light ABS 警示灯, ABS failure ABS 故障, ABS brake ABS 刹车）。不可数。
[例] ① ABS helped her keep steering control on the wet road. ABS 助她在湿滑路面仍可控方向。 ② The ABS light stayed on after the sensor failed. 传感器故障后 ABS 灯常亮。
[搭] ABS light ABS 警示灯, ABS failure ABS 故障, ABS sensor ABS 传感器, anti-lock braking system 防抱死制动系统
[联] brake 刹车, tyre 轮胎, skid 打滑, car 汽车, safety 安全, traction control 牵引力控制
[记] 读字母 A-B-S；anti-lock braking system 全称

**traction control**
英/美 /ˈtrækʃn kənˈtrəʊl/
n. 牵引力控制
[义] A system that stops driven wheels spinning on slippery surfaces. 防止驱动轮在滑溜路面空转的系统（ traction control system 牵引力控制系统, traction control light 牵引力控制灯, disable traction control 关闭牵引力控制）。不可数。
[例] ① Traction control cut power when the wheels spun on ice. 冰面车轮打滑时牵引力控制切断动力。 ② Rally drivers sometimes disable traction control on gravel. 拉力车手在 gravel 上有时关闭牵引力控制。
[搭] traction control system 牵引力控制系统, traction control light 牵引力控制灯, disable traction control 关闭牵引力控制
[联] ABS 防抱死, wheel 轮子, skid 打滑, four-wheel drive 四驱, car 汽车, wet 湿的
[记] 不可数。常缩写 TCS；与 ABS、ESP 同属主动安全

**charging cable**
英/美 /ˈtʃɑːdʒɪŋ ˌkeɪbl/
n. 充电线
[义] A cable used to connect an electric vehicle to a charging point. 连接电动车与充电点的电缆（ plug in the charging cable 插入充电线, charging cable lock 充电线锁, portable charging cable 便携充电线）。可数。
[例] ① She kept a charging cable in the boot for emergencies. 她在后备箱备充电线应急。 ② Public charging cables vary by connector type. 公共充电线接头类型各异。
[搭] plug in the charging cable 插入充电线, charging cable lock 充电线锁, portable charging cable 便携充电线, Type 2 charging cable Type 2 充电线
[联] charging point 充电点, charging station 充电站, electric vehicle 电动车, battery 电池, plug 插头
[记] 可数：a charging cable。CCS、Type 2、CHAdeMO 等为接口标准

**L-plate**
英/美 /ˈel pleɪt/
n. L 牌； learner plate
[义] A sign on a car showing the driver has not yet passed the full driving test (UK). 表明司机尚未通过完整驾考的车牌标识（英国）（ display L-plates 展示 L 牌, L-plate driver 学驾司机, remove L-plates 取下 L 牌）。可数。常用复数 L-plates。
[例] ① Learners must display L-plates on front and rear. 学驾者须在前后展示 L 牌。 ② She passed the test and removed the L-plates the same day. 她当天通过考试就取下 L 牌。
[搭] display L-plates 展示 L 牌, L-plate driver 学驾司机, remove L-plates 取下 L 牌, red L-plate 红色 L 牌
[联] driver 司机, learner 学习者, driving test 驾考, car 汽车, P-plate P 牌, license 执照
[记] 可数，常用复数 L-plates。BrE/爱尔兰等；通过后常换 P-plate（部分地區）

**road tax**
英/美 /ˈrəʊd tæks/
n. 道路税
[义] Tax paid for the right to use a vehicle on public roads (BrE; officially vehicle excise duty). 在公共道路使用车辆须缴的税（英式；正式名 vehicle excise duty）（ pay road tax 缴道路税, road tax disc 路税标〔旧制〕, road tax exemption 免税）。不可数。
[例] ① Electric cars currently enjoy lower road tax in the UK. 英国电动车目前道路税较低。 ② Failure to pay road tax can lead to clamping. 未缴道路税可被锁车。
[搭] pay road tax 缴道路税, road tax exemption 免税, vehicle excise duty 车辆消费税, road tax band 路税档位
[联] car 汽车, vehicle 车辆, registration 登记, MOT 年检, government 政府, license 执照
[记] 不可数。BrE road tax / VED；AmE 无直接对应，各州 registration fee 不同

**vehicle registration**
英/美 /ˈviːəkl ˌredʒɪˈstreɪʃn/
n. 车辆登记
[义] The official record of a vehicle and its owner with the authorities. 向主管部门登记车辆及车主信息的正式记录（ vehicle registration number 登记号码, vehicle registration document 登记文件, renew vehicle registration 续登记）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① The vehicle registration number matches the plates. 登记号码与车牌一致。 ② Keep the vehicle registration document in the car. 把车辆登记文件放在车内。
[搭] vehicle registration number 登记号码, vehicle registration document 登记文件, renew vehicle registration 续登记, vehicle registration fee 登记费
[联] license plate 车牌, number plate 车牌, car 汽车, owner 车主, document 文件, DMV 车管局
[记] 不可数/可数。AmE 多在 DMV 办理；BrE log book（V5C）

**transponder**
英/美 /trænˈspɒndə(r)/
n. 应答器； toll transponder
[义] A device in a vehicle that sends a signal to receive automatic toll charges or aircraft identification. 车内发送信号以自动扣过路费或航空识别的装置（ toll transponder 收费应答器, transponder tag 应答标签, aircraft transponder 航空应答机）。可数。
[例] ① A toll transponder lets you pass booths without stopping. 收费应答器可不停通过收费站。 ② Aircraft transponders help radar identify flights. 航空应答机助雷达识别航班。
[搭] toll transponder 收费应答器, transponder tag 应答标签, aircraft transponder 航空应答机, E-ZPass transponder E-ZPass 标签
[联] toll road 收费公路, toll booth 收费站, airplane 飞机, radar 雷达, highway 公路, fee 费用
[记] 可数：a transponder。trans- + respond → 自动回应信号

**pannier**
英/美 /ˈpæniə(r)/
n. 驮包；挂篮
[义] A bag or basket fixed on each side of a bicycle or motorcycle for carrying loads. 固定于自行车或摩托车两侧、用于载货的包或篮（ bicycle pannier 自行车驮包, pannier rack 驮包架, waterproof pannier 防水驮包）。可数。常用复数 panniers。
[例] ① She loaded groceries into the panniers and cycled home. 她把杂货装进驮包骑车回家。 ② Waterproof panniers kept the laptop dry in the rain. 防水驮包让笔记本雨中保持干燥。
[搭] bicycle pannier 自行车驮包, pannier rack 驮包架, waterproof pannier 防水驮包, rear pannier 后驮包
[联] bicycle 自行车, bike 自行车, rack 架子, luggage 行李, commute 通勤, cyclist 骑行者
[记] 可数，常用复数 panniers。摩托车侧箱亦称 pannier

**kickstand**
英/美 /ˈkɪkstænd/
n. 撑脚架
[义] A metal support hinged to a bicycle or motorcycle that holds it upright when parked. 铰接于自行车或摩托车、停车时保持直立的金属支架（ put down the kickstand 放下撑脚, kickstand collapse 撑脚倒塌, centre kickstand 中撑）。可数。
[例] ① He forgot the kickstand and the bike toppled over. 他忘了撑脚，车倒了。 ② A centre kickstand suits heavy loaded touring bikes. 中撑适合载重旅行自行车。
[搭] put down the kickstand 放下撑脚, kickstand collapse 撑脚倒塌, centre kickstand 中撑, side kickstand 侧撑
[联] bicycle 自行车, motorcycle 摩托车, bike 自行车, park 停放, wheel 轮子
[记] 可数：a kickstand。kick + stand

**channel marker**
英/美 /ˈtʃænl ˌmɑːkə(r)/
n. 航道标
[义] A buoy or post showing the safe path for boats in a channel. 标示水道安全航线的浮标或桩（ navigation channel marker 航行航道标, red channel marker 红色航道标, follow the channel markers 沿标航行）。可数。
[例] ① Red and green channel markers lined the harbour entrance. 港入口排列红绿航道标。 ② The skipper followed channel markers through the shallow bay. 船长沿航道标穿过浅湾。
[搭] navigation channel marker 航行航道标, red channel marker 红色航道标, follow the channel markers 沿标航行
[联] buoy 浮标, harbour 港湾, channel 航道, navigation 航行, boat 小船, sailor 水手
[记] 可数：a channel marker。红/绿标遵循当地航行规则

**wake**
英/美 /weɪk/
n. 尾流；航迹
[义] The track of waves left behind a moving boat. 行驶中船只留下的波浪轨迹（ boat wake 船尾流, in the wake of 紧随……之后〔喻〕, wake boarding 尾波滑水）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① Smaller boats must avoid the wake of the ferry. 小船须避开渡轮尾流。 ② Wake boarding uses the boat wake as a ramp. 尾波滑水以船尾流作跳台。
[搭] boat wake 船尾流, in the wake of 紧随……之后, wake turbulence 尾流湍流〔航空〕, cross the wake 横穿尾流
[联] boat 小船, ship 大船, wave 波浪, ferry 渡轮, water 水, sail 航行
[记] 可数/不可数。wake 亦指「守灵、醒来」，语境区分；航空 wake turbulence 指飞机尾涡

**smog check**
英/美 /ˈsmɒɡ tʃek/
n. 尾气检测
[义] A test of vehicle exhaust emissions required in some US states. 美国部分州要求的车辆尾气排放检测（ pass a smog check 通过尾气检测, smog check station 检测站, fail a smog check 检测不合格）。可数。
[例] ① The old car failed its smog check and needed repairs. 旧车尾气检测未过需维修。 ② Smog check stations are busy before registration deadlines. 登记截止前检测站很忙。
[搭] pass a smog check 通过尾气检测, smog check station 检测站, fail a smog check 检测不合格, smog check certificate 检测证书
[联] emission 排放, exhaust 排气管, MOT 年检, car 汽车, pollution 污染, registration 登记
[记] 可数：a smog check。AmE 加州等常用；类似 BrE MOT 排放部分
